# v0.23.0 — READ RECEIPTS, reworked: growth, places and conversations that flow — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** READ RECEIPTS becomes a slice of life. Conversations run as instant, natural episodes, and promises are kept. People open up at their own pace through a closed disclosure vocabulary. The player lives in a flat in Port Merrow with rooms, a building and a street. Meet-ups are played as scenes at venues and homes. Life events happen on both sides. Long-term outcomes run up to marriage. A v0.22.0 save migrates.

**Architecture:**
- **Time moves only by actions** (rule 2).
  - A reply inside an open **episode** lands at the minute of the send that asked for it, so the only wait is the model writing.
  - Gaps come from activities, sleep, work, promises and the person's schedule.
- **A person's growth record** feeds four things: what the voice is told about who they are now (`growth.who_now`), what a scene may ask, what the energy term rewards, and the gate for numbers and meeting.
  - The rungs are built only from `growth.KINDS`, a closed vocabulary checked at load (rule 12).
  - Life logistics are relationship-stage gates in `growth.yaml` `willing:`.
- **The world is the engine's ordinary narrated turn.** The phone is a layer over it, switched by one flag, `state.social["phone_up"]`.
  - A per-session **narration turnstile** keeps a world turn and a voice pass from ever both asking the supervisor for a narration ticket. It is taken before the turn lock, never under it.
  - A scene owns the turn through `engine.social.scene_open`, which both `intents.scene_owns_turn` and `director.ensure_scene` ask.
- **Everything is gated on `paths.social`.** The other six stories stay byte-identical. The goldens and §19.5's parametrized pins prove it.

**Tech Stack:**
- Server: Python 3.11, Flask 3.1 with Flask-SocketIO 5.6, PyYAML (libyaml).
- Python tests: pytest with pytest-xdist and pytest-timeout.
- Client: React 18, Vite 5, vitest 2 with jsdom, and playwright-core for the layout check.
- Client tests follow the repo's convention: `// @vitest-environment jsdom` on line 1, `createRoot` plus `React.act`, and the helpers in `ui/tests/helpers/dom.js` (`mount`, `click`, `typeInto`, `button`, `byTestId`, `byText`). **No `@testing-library/react`.**

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-v0.23.0-read-receipts-rework-design.md` (committed at `960bbb7`; Revision 2). It is the binding authority.
- Section numbers (§N), finding ids (I1-I16, N1-N8, m1-m11) and task numbers (T1-T34) below are the spec's.
- The owner's brief and its addenda are the authority for WHAT is built: `docs/superpowers/briefs/2026-10-13-read-receipts-growth-brief.md`.
- Where this plan and the spec disagree, the spec wins, unless a **Plan decision** below says why not (the spec defers to the code, and every Plan decision is a place where the code disagrees with the spec or the spec is silent).

**House form:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-v0.22.0-read-receipts.md` (v0.22.0's plan). Its Plan decisions 1-32 still describe code that exists, and this plan keeps every one that is not superseded below. The section "Lessons carried forward" names what that build learned and where this plan applies it.

---

## Global Constraints

The spec has no section titled "Global Constraints". The lines below are its project-wide requirements, each quoted **verbatim** and marked "spec", plus the controller's standing rules, marked "standing". Every task's requirements implicitly include this whole section.

**Rule 12 (spec, "Rule 12 in this design", the controller's ruling; it overrides every other line):**
- (spec) "**The engine has NO concept of physical intimacy, in any form.** No data file lists a physical act. No move, gate, refusal, willingness row, depth rung, prompt line or test names one. Nothing anywhere says "too soon"."
- (spec) "**What happens physically, on a date or anywhere else, is the register of the storyteller prompt and the owner's model.** It is never engine data, never a gate and never a refusal. No scene prompt and no texter wording rates it, limits it or tiers it."
- (spec) "**The growth ladder is self-disclosure and topics only.** It measures what a person will talk about and share, and how open they are: small talk, then everyday life, then personal stories, then hopes and fears, then family and the future (§6.1). No rung's meaning implies an intensity tier."
- (spec) "**Life logistics are relationship-stage outcomes.** Staying over, moving in and marriage follow from the stage, the evening's circumstances and the life-event engine (§6.5, §10.5, §12). None is tied to a depth rung, and none is framed as intimacy."
- (spec) "**The guard is structural, not a lexicon** (§19.6). Every rung is built only from topic kinds in a closed disclosure vocabulary, and the validator refuses anything else at load. There is no banned-word list."
- (standing) AGENTS.md rule 12 in full: no content-rating or "safety" layer in any form, no register line in any prompt, no intensity tier, nothing that rates, filters or bounds content. Commit bodies describe engine SCOPE, never a content-policy decision.

**The engine's laws (spec):**
- (spec §2) "Everything the model writes is still proposed and clamped (rule 1). Every write goes through `apply_effect` (rule 3). Every draw comes from a named stream (rule 4). Every number lives in story data and is measured by the simulator before it ships (rule 10)."
- (spec §3.1) "The clock still moves only through `clock.advance_time` from an action (rule 2)."
- (spec, Non-goals) "**No wall-clock time.** R-03 stands. Within a session nothing moves on the wall clock, and the default for away time is now "none" (§3.6)."
- (spec §4.2, I14) "Habits, like pace, home, topics and layers (§6, §10.5), are drawn once per person from a counterless key, `stable_rng(seed, f"{stream}:{pid}:{attr}")`. They never use `person_rng`, so the draw order cannot matter. They are materialised into `people[pid]` by a `social_person` effect at first match (rule 3). A read path that meets an unmaterialised person (the payload, a hint) computes them from the same key and writes nothing."
- (spec §6.4) "**No floor or count reads in-game days** (§1.4's lesson)."
- (spec §6.5) "**No life-logistics commitment may read depth.**"
- (spec §7.4) "`llm.profiles.texter.min_context` stays **4096**, so a 4096-context model still binds."
- (spec §16) "`CURRENT_SAVE_VERSION` is untouched, so no other story's save moves."
- (spec §18.1) "The subjects carry: no `negative` key; no "no X" terms; no rating words." "Plates are unpeopled."

**The other stories (spec §19.5):** "These must not move: the local golden; the LM Studio golden (whose request bytes include the turn grammar's `intent` schema); `app_shape.json`; `games.json`, except one sanctioned READ RECEIPTS row (its manifest now declares art paths and directives)." And: "**Module state.** Every new module-level global and cache gets its `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml` row and its `WARMERS` entry."

**Tests and tasks (spec):**
- (spec §19.7) "Every test uses the stubs. T31's live check, and T9's optional `timings` probe, run under `@pytest.mark.live`."
- (spec §24) "Each task runs `scripts/run_tests.py files` over what it touches. The full suite runs once, at T34."
- (spec §24, N6) "**No task needs a later one (N6).** Where a seam must ship first, the task ships a NAMED STUB. The stub table below says which task replaces each stub, and which test flips when it does."
- (spec §24) "Execution is subagent-driven, with a review between tasks."

**Standing rules (the controller's, every task):**
- **No live model, ever, in a test.**
  - `_no_live_model_calls` stays default-deny.
  - The voicer's model function is a `tests/social_stub.py::StubVoice`. Its summary and recap function is a one-argument stub.
  - The process scheduler is PAUSED in every social test (`tests/social_fixtures.py::_quieted`).
  - A storyteller test passes a stub `llm_fn` to `create_app(testing=True, llm_fn=...)` or `SessionStore`.
  - Only T9's optional probe and T31 run live, marked `@pytest.mark.live`.
- **Never touch the owner's files or services.**
  - Never read or write `config/local.yaml`, the real `data/`, or LM Studio's `mcp.json` (`C:\Users\Knack\.cache\lm-studio\mcp.json`).
  - Tests use `tmp_path`, `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR`, `tests/hosted_app.py` and a patched `engine.config._CONFIG_DIR`.
  - Never load or unload a model.
  - The simulator runs under a throwaway `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR` (v0.22.0 T26's guard, kept).
- **Never signal a process by a bare pid.**
  - Tests use `tests/process_identity.py`.
  - A background server is started with the Bash tool's `run_in_background`, or with `--exit-with-parent`, and stopped with `TaskStop`.
- **AGENTS.md's twelve rules bind every task.**
  - Rule 1: intents; a refusal reaches the prose.
  - Rule 2: `clock.advance_time` only.
  - Rule 3: `effects.apply_effect` only.
  - Rule 4: `world_rng`/`stable_rng`/`person_rng`, never bare `random`.
  - Rule 5: `get_config()`, nothing hardcoded.
  - Rule 6: never gate rest. Sleep is offered in the bedroom with no gate, and "Head home" has no gate.
  - Rule 9: NOT WIRED rows with their files.
  - Rule 10: run `scripts/simulate_social.py` before a balance constant changes.
  - Rule 11: `pathlib` only, no separator, drive letter or `.exe`.
  - Rule 12: as above.
- **How tests run.** A task runs only what it touches, plus the guards:
  - PowerShell: `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\run_tests.py files <paths> <GUARDS>`.
  - Git Bash: `.venv/Scripts/python.exe scripts/run_tests.py files <paths> <GUARDS>`.
  - **`<GUARDS>` is ALWAYS this exact list, in every run step of every task** (the all-stories and all-caches tests, lesson 3): `tests/test_story_content_integrity.py tests/test_presence_every_story.py tests/test_story_surface.py tests/test_new_story.py tests/test_cache_warming.py tests/test_cache_reset_race.py tests/test_module_state_inventory.py tests/test_reachability.py tests/test_social_seam.py tests/test_release_hygiene.py tests/test_finales.py` (`test_finales.py` is the standing rules' stories-wide endings guard; pre-flight F10).
  - **`<GOLDENS>`** is `tests/test_local_mode_golden.py tests/test_llm_golden_lmstudio.py tests/test_thread_safety.py`. Every task that touches a shared engine file (§22's list) adds it.
  - Never the full suite inside a task. Never a hand-built long `pytest` line. T34 alone runs `scripts\run_tests.py full`, once.
  - `npm test --prefix ui` runs whenever `ui/src` or `ui/tests` changes.
- **The client build is committed.** Any `ui/src` change runs `npm run build --prefix ui` and commits `content/scenes/clockwork/static/dist` IN THE SAME COMMIT. `tests/test_ui_contract.py::test_the_committed_build_is_not_behind_its_source` guards it.
- **Docs move with the change** (AGENTS.md), in the same commit, for every doc the change makes stale:
  - `games/read-receipts/CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` and its `README.md`;
  - the root `CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]`, and `README.md` for engine changes;
  - `docs/AUTHORING.md` §3.15;
  - `docs/GOVERNANCE.md`, `docs/STATE.md` and `docs/AGENTS.md` (NOT WIRED rows);
  - `docs/DESIGN.md`, `docs/HOSTING.md` and `docs/MODEL_SERVERS.md`;
  - `CLAUDE.md`, when status, the roadmap or the deferred list moves.

  "Updated" means stale sentences removed, not appended to.
- **Module state is classified.**
  - Every new module-level global, class-level container, `lru_cache`, lock, thread, timer or pool site under `engine/` gets its row in `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml` in the same task.
  - Every cache gets its `WARMERS` loader in `engine/games/caches.py` and, if it is nulled on reset, its `NULLED_ATTRIBUTES` row, and is read once into a local.
  - Every new lock takes its place in `engine/locks.py` and `tests/lock_order.py` (see Plan decision 3 for the turnstile).
- **Git.**
  - The branch is `release/v0.23.0` (checked out).
  - Commit messages inside the branch are free-form. Write each one with the editor tool to `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-v0.23.0-read-receipts-rework/commit-t<N>-msg.txt` (gitignored) and commit with `git commit -F <file>`. Heredocs and `python -c` lose backticks on this machine.
  - Every message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
  - **Nothing is pushed.** The final squash to `main` uses the bare subject `v0.23.0` (T34), on the owner's word.
- **Tests, the discipline.**
  - A fix ships with a test that failed before it.
  - A new guard is canary-checked by reintroducing what it guards. A canary that removes a guard runs only after the home directory, `_CONFIG_DIR`, the storage root and the model endpoints are redirected into the test's temp directory.
  - Mark a test `process` if it starts an interpreter, `loopback` if it binds a loopback server, and `slow` if it is a balance measurement.

## Lessons carried forward from v0.22.0

Each lesson is applied where it is named. A reviewer rejects a task that breaks one.

1. **Force every draw a test depends on (v0.22 F16).**
   - A test never hunts a seed whose draws happen to work.
   - Every probability this plan adds is split into a module-level function that a test can monkeypatch:
     - `convo._ghost_factor`, `convo._reply_factor`, `convo._promise_drawn`, `convo._target_draw`;
     - `growth._jitter`;
     - `plans._flake_drawn`;
     - `scene._moment_draw`;
     - `scene._stay_drawn`;
     - `life._event_drawn`;
     - `life._seen_drawn`.
   - `tests/social_fixtures.py::_replies_forced` is rewritten in T3 to force the per-episode-start draws.
2. **Tests may never reach a live backend.**
   - Stub the voicer (`StubVoice`), the summary and recap functions (one argument), and the storyteller's `llm_fn`.
   - Pause the scheduler (`_quieted`).
   - A storyteller stub returns a schema-valid turn: `tests/social_world_stub.py::StubNarrator` (T11).
3. **Include the all-stories and all-caches tests in each run list.** `<GUARDS>` above is appended to every task's run step.
4. **`engine_requires` is the manifest-contract version** (v0.22 F1). It stays `">=0.2.0"` (`engine/games/__init__.py::ENGINE_VERSION`). The release task changes `pyproject.toml` and `ui/package.json` only. `game.yaml` `version` is the story's own: `0.2.0` from T11, because the manifest gains directives and art.
5. **Client tests use `createRoot` plus `React.act` with jsdom**, through `ui/tests/helpers/dom.js`. There is no `@testing-library`.
6. **Rebuild and commit the dist with any `ui/src` change**, in the same commit.
7. **Hidden-value scans plant sentinel values, never English words.**
   - A test that proves a payload carries no hidden number sets the value to a sentinel such as `openness = 73.123457`, `warmth = 61.987654` or `pace = 1.234567`, then asserts that the sentinel's text is absent from the JSON payload.
   - It never greps for "openness" or "warmth".
8. **Liveness asks the session's own store** (v0.22 Plan decision 28). The turnstile and `_build`'s guard read `session.store`.
9. **One release of the turn lock** (`engine/session/store.py::release_turn`, v0.22 Plan decision 31). `run_guarded`'s world path and the voice pass both use it.
10. **Readers of `session.voicing` and `session.typing` iterate a copy** (v0.22 F22). The same holds for `convo.MARKED` (Plan decision 9).
11. **A balance row that cannot land is DEFERRED by data, never skipped and never widened** (v0.22 F15, Plan decision 30). `tests/test_simulate_social.py` keeps `BANDS` plus `DEFERRED_ROWS` covering exactly `REPORT_ROWS`, and each deferred row's text is in CLAUDE.md.
12. **The voiced commit's snapshot is scoped** (`voicer.ScopedTransaction`, v0.22 T26). Each new write a commit makes is covered by `tests/test_social_commit_scope.py`:
    - `social_episode`;
    - `social_growth`;
    - `social_promise`;
    - `social_memory`'s per-person parts.

    Each lands in the task that adds it.
13. **The harness writes nothing of the owner's**: `simulate_social.main()` runs under a throwaway `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR` (v0.22 T26).

## Plan decisions (where the spec is silent, or the code disagrees with it)

Each decision is repeated, marked **Plan decision N**, at the step it governs.

1. **Global Constraints.** The spec has no section by that name, so the section above quotes its project-wide lines and adds the standing rules, each labelled.
2. **The ids are prefixed so they never collide with existing ones.**
   - Spec §5.1 names promises `"p<n>"`. That collides with person ids (`p0412`), so promises are `"pr<n>"`.
   - Spec §9.1 names plans `"m<n>"`. That collides with message ids (`m<n>`, v0.22 Plan decision 14), so plans are `"pl<n>"`.
   - Episodes stay `"e<n>"`.
   - Counters live in `state.social["counters"]`: `episode`, `promise` and `plan`. Notifications keep v0.22's own `counters["notification"]`, which `messaging.notify` already increments (pre-flight F25; Plan decision 13).
3. **The turnstile's place in the lock order.**
   - Spec §20.1 places the turnstile "immediately BEFORE the session turn lock" in `engine/locks.py`'s one order. But the turn lock (`GameSession.lock`) is NOT in that numbered list. It sits in the docstring's "TWO MORE, HELD ACROSS WHOLE OPERATIONS" section, where it is taken only non-blocking and every listed lock may be taken under it.
   - So the turnstile is documented as a hold of the same kind, outermost of all: a new docstring section, "THE NARRATION TURNSTILE (v0.23.0)", placed after "TWO MORE…" and before "THE SUPERVISOR'S OWN PROCESS", which states:
     - the order is turnstile, then turn lock, then everything in the list;
     - nothing takes the turnstile while holding the turn lock or any listed lock.
   - The turnstile's hold is a logical state (`holder`). Its internal `threading.Condition` lock, `engine.social.turnstile.Turnstile._cond`, is held only to read or change `holder` and `world_waiting`, so it is a LEAF. It is added to both `LEAVES` lists and to `INSTANCE_LOCKS`.
   - The header text "TWO MORE" is not edited, because `tests/test_thread_safety.py` splits the docstring on it.
   - `tests/lock_order.py` gains two pseudo-names, `TURN` and `TURNSTILE`, and their rules (T8):
     - the checker records a turnstile hold through patched `Turnstile` methods;
     - it fails a turnstile taken while `TURN` or any listed lock is held;
     - it fails `acquire_world` taken while anything at all is held.
   - **`renew_after_fork`** registers MODULE locks. The turnstile is per session and is built in the worker after any fork (`SessionStore._build`). The module holds no lock of its own, so there is nothing to register. The new docstring section says so, and `tests/test_module_state_inventory.py` confirms the module has no module-level lock.
4. **The world verbs are system-only skills.**
   - `execute_intent` checks `skill_def.callable_by(agent)` with `agent=AGENT_STORYTELLER`. A storyteller-callable skill would join `SKILL_REGISTRY.tools_for_agent("storyteller")` for EVERY story (the MCP list, the LM Studio golden).
   - So `social_do`, `social_go`, `social_scene` and `social_close` are registered like v0.22's phone skills: `pack="social"`, `category="WORLD"`, `trigger=TRIGGER_SYSTEM`, `agents=[AGENT_SYSTEM]`.
   - `engine/game/intents.py` gains `SOCIAL_WORLD_ACTIONS = frozenset({"do", "go", "scene", "close"})`.
   - `execute_intent` resolves those four actions as `AGENT_SYSTEM`. They are never in another story's `legal_intents`, so the branch is unreachable there.
   - T11 adds `test_every_world_skill_has_a_world_verb` beside v0.22's `test_every_phone_skill_has_a_phone_verb`.
5. **Moves and closers are two verbs, and composite targets carry the arguments.**
   - `intents.normalise` carries only `action`, `target` and `difficulty`, and every verb is capped at eight options (`_MAX_OPTIONS`).
   - **`scene`** carries the conversational moves (§10.3). Its target is `"<move>"` or `"<move>:<topic_id>"`, for example `"ask_about:t_shifts"` or `"playful"`.
   - **`close`** carries the closers (§10.4). Its target is `"<closer>"` or `"<closer>:<arg>"`:
     - `call_it_a_night`;
     - `call_it_a_night:promise:<window>`;
     - `call_it_a_night:plan:soon`;
     - `call_it_a_night:stay`;
     - `go_somewhere_else:<venue>`;
     - `another_round`, `walk_them_home`, `back_to_yours`, `back_to_theirs`, `stay_the_night`.
   - Each verb lists at most eight targets, chosen by a fixed rank (T13).
   - `to_tool_call` splits the target at the first `:`.
6. **`activities.yaml` is found beside `paths.locations`.** Spec §8.4 puts it at `data/world/activities.yaml`, and §22's `game.yaml` row lists no path key for it. `engine/social/__init__.py::load_all` reads it from `Path(paths.locations).parent / "activities.yaml"` into `load_all()["activities"]`. It is `{}` when absent, which is the case until T11.
7. **Fixed per-person attributes are a registry** (`engine/social/attrs.py`, T1).
   - Each owning module registers a drawer, `attrs.drawer(name, stream)`:
     - `habits`: convo (T3);
     - `pace` and `topics`: growth (T2);
     - `home`: places (T10).
   - `attrs.of(state, pid)` returns the stored dict, with any missing key computed from its counterless key and written nowhere.
   - `attrs.materialise(state, pid)` writes them once, through `social_person` `set: {"attrs": …}`. The `social_match` handler (`matching._e_match`) calls it just before `messaging.on_match`, and so does the migration's own pass.
   - A person matched before a later drawer existed reads that attribute computed until the migration or the next match. The answer is identical either way, because the key is counterless.
8. **Homes and venue places live in a small new module, `engine/social/places.py` (T10).** §22's new-module list has no home for the home template or the travel minutes, and T10 lands before `world.py` (T11).
9. **Marks are visible to the mechanics through a ContextVar.**
   - Spec §4.3 needs a send to know whether the due reply is already MARKED by a pass. Marks live on the session (`session.voicing`), and an effect handler sees only `state`.
   - `run_mechanics` sets `convo.MARKED` (a `ContextVar[frozenset[str]]`) to `frozenset(tuple(session.voicing))` around `execute_phone_intent`, and `convo.on_player_send` reads it.
   - Outside an action it is empty, so a harness with no pass sees nothing marked.
   - It gets a module-state row with the verdict `per_context`.
10. **The burst minimum is a field.** Spec §4.5 says "three player messages get at least 2 bubbles". `Decision` gains `min_bubbles: int = 1` beside the spec's `mode`, `end`, `reason`, `promise_id` and `followup_id`. It also gains `plan_id: str = ""`, for the `invite`, `flake` and `stood_up` situations.
11. **Stubbed growth gains are a named set.**
    - `growth._STUBBED = {"moment", "promise_kept", "support"}` (T2). `growth.gain` returns 0 for an event named in it.
    - Each replacing task deletes its name and flips its test (§24's stub table): T4 `promise_kept`, T13 `moment`, T17 `support`.
    - T19's sweep asserts the set is empty.
12. **Sleep has a floor.** Spec §8.4 sleeps "until `clock.morning`". A player who sleeps at 06:30 would wake at 07:00.
    - `conversation.yaml` `clock:` gains `min_sleep_hours: 4`.
    - Sleep runs to the first `morning` hour that is at least that far ahead, or to the waking band when the player's job shape sets one (MINOR 4).
13. **Interruptible activities step through the queue.**
    - Spec §8.4 stops at "the first notifying event before it (`turn._next_notifying_at`)". But a reply decided by a read event that fires DURING the activity is not in the queue when the activity starts.
    - `world.advance_interruptible` therefore advances event by event and stops at the first new notification of a person who may wake the player. It counts notifications by v0.22's existing `state.social["counters"]["notification"]` (read with `.get("notification", 0)`), which `messaging.notify` already increments. No `notify` counter is added (pre-flight F25).
    - It is a superset of the spec's rule and stops at the same minute.
14. **The local turnstile wait is `llm.timeout_seconds`.** Spec §20.1 says "the texter call's timeout locally". The texter profile has no timeout of its own, and the client's per-call timeout is `llm.timeout_seconds` (300).
    - `turnstile.world_wait_seconds()` returns `hosting.queue_wait_seconds` when hosted, and `llm.timeout_seconds` otherwise.
    - The world turn waits at most one call, and the call is bounded by that same timeout.
15. **`social.scene_open(state)` is a package function.**
    - It reads only `state.social["scene"]`, so `intents` and `director` ask it with no submodule imported (spec §10.1).
    - It is written in T1, and is False until T13 opens a scene.
    - It returns False unless `declared()`, so for every other story it is one config read.
16. **The migration renames a location through the state, not `social_go`'s travel.**
    - Spec §16.2 says "`apt_lounge` (`GameState.location_id`, set through `social_go`'s handler)".
    - `social_go`'s handler has two parts: `op: "travel"`, which charges minutes, and `op: "place"`, which writes `location_id` and nothing else. The migration applies `op: "place"`, so no clock moves (rule 2: the migration is not an action).
17. **T5's "depth 0→1 measured on 10 seeds"** is run with v0.22's simulator (still phone-latency-shaped), with the rows that exist, and recorded under Notes. T9 replaces the simulator and re-measures. No constant changes on T5's numbers alone (rule 10: T9 is the first measurement on the new harness).
18. **`played_on_the_phone()` is retired in T11** with the refusal it served, together with its two call sites and its test. T19's sweep checks that nothing refers to it.
19. **Openness "decay" is the loss scale.** Spec §6.2 says "a ghoster's openness decays twice as fast", but defines no decay outside §6.3's losses. `growth.loss` multiplies every loss by the archetype's `growth.decay_mult`, which is 2 for the ghoster and 1 for everyone else (T2).
20. **The reply schema's proposals land with their mechanics.** §13.3 lists `promise`, `invite_over`, `move_in`, `propose` and `stay_or_go` in T7's schema. T7 builds the per-turn mechanism (`legal_proposals` feeds the enum). Each name is added to `legal_proposals` by the task that owns its mechanic (T4, T16, T17, T18), so T7 never offers a proposal whose effect does not exist yet (N6).
21. **Test-only decks are reached by patching the deck module's directory lookup**, `engine.content.deck._decks_dir`, never through `set_overlay`. `set_overlay` replaces the active story's overlay whole, and would drop `paths.social` (T14).
22. **The burst rule and the ghost draw are per episode START.** v0.22's `pending_read` flag is retired. A burst is recognised by an unvoiced reply-family due message in the thread (`convo._due_reply`) or a queued `read` (`convo._read_pending`), both read from state, so a replay and a migrated save behave the same (T3, T20).

Decisions 23-33 were added by the pre-flight rulings (F1-F45, `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-v0.23.0-read-receipts-rework/preflight.md`), each accepted by the controller:

23. **Each effect kind joins `SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` with its handler, never before (pre-flight F1).** Spec §22 and §24's T1 row put all nine new kinds in `kinds.py` at T1. But `tests/test_social_seam.py::test_every_social_kind_has_a_handler` (in `<GUARDS>`) asserts `SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS <= registered_kinds()`, so that would be red from T1 to T20, against N6. So T1 leaves the set as v0.22 shipped it, and each kind is added in the task that registers its handler: T2 `social_growth`, T3 `social_episode`, T4 `social_promise`, T11 `social_go`, T12 `social_plan`, T13 `social_scene` (and T13 REMOVES `social_meet` with `meets.py`'s handler), T17 `social_life`, T18 `social_household`, T20 `social_migrate`. `tests/test_social_slice_v2.py::NEW_KINDS` and `RETIRED_KINDS` track the same in each task.
24. **The no-days test's exemptions are the data's, found by walking it (pre-flight F5, F36).** R15's "only these" exemptions do not cover every day-ish key the shipped data carries. T5 converts or removes the gates and caps (`regard`, `asks` and `media` per-day caps to per-episode; `unsolicited.per_day_at_stage` to `per_episode_at_stage`; `numbers.gate.days` and `exclusive.gate.stage_days` to `{willing: …}` gates), and exempts, each with its reason: `bands[*].contact_days` (an m7 absence measure like `fade_days`: a spec clarification), `interest.left_on_read.grace_hours` and `mood.decay_hours` ("a decay or grace window, not a gate"; `hours?` stays in DAYISH), and `meets.days_ahead`/`meets.cancel_cost[*].within_hours` ("removed in T12", whose rows T12 deletes). Between episodes the per-episode caps are keyed `after:<last closed episode id>` (`convo.cap_key`), not a fixed `"e0"` that would never reset.
25. **"Head home" comes first, then open plans (pre-flight F33).** Spec §8.5 lists the open plans' destinations before Home. The plan puts "Head home" first from every place outside the flat, so "always offered" holds under the 8-option cap (`_MAX_OPTIONS`) however many plans are open (T11).
26. **Staying over is decided by archetype and the evening only (pre-flight F34, the controller's choice).** The spec allows `intent` as an input to the stay decision; `scenes.yaml` `stay.accept` had an `intent: {long_term, short_term}` term that made a casual seeker likelier to stay. The term is dropped entirely: `stay.accept` is keyed by archetype (`default` as the fallback), and `stay_offered`'s evening facts do the rest (T13 data, T16 code). Nothing else about the stay changes.
27. **The opening's intent-carrying choice is `tidy_up` (pre-flight F3).** `make_breakfast` is kitchen-only and the entry is `apt_lounge`, so "Make a cup of tea" → `make_breakfast` would be refused the moment it was picked. The opening offers "Tidy up a bit" → `do tidy_up`, legal at the entry, and the route-level tests post THAT option's `choice_id`: the choice route resolves an intent only from an offered option, and is never widened to accept a client intent (rule 1) (T11, T28).
28. **A story's own `PlayScreen` is given `onChoose` (pre-flight F8).** v0.22's core passes a story's `PlayScreen` only `onAct` (phone actions, `App.jsx:748`), so a world choice had no way out. T21 passes it the same `onChoose={(choice) => send(choice.id, null, choice.text, choice)}` core's own `Play` gets (`App.jsx:775`). No other story has a `PlayScreen`, so nothing else moves.
29. **`SocialWorld` is registered at priority 40 (pre-flight F43).** `StorytellerMind` is at 30; two equal priorities would leave seam pin 4's exact chain order to registration order (T11).
30. **Two payload sections the spec's client needs and no task produced (pre-flight F18).** T11 adds `places` (`{"venues": [{id, label}]}`, the phone `go` verb's legal targets: T22's Places app reads `phone.places.venues`); T13 adds `scene` (`{pid, name, stage_words, closeness_words, portrait}` while a scene is open, `{}` otherwise, from v0.22's `relationships.stage_words`, `relationships.band` and `media.portrait_url`: T23's `Companion` reads `phone.scene`). Both are words only, each with a sentinel test.
31. **A ghost cancels only the reply family (pre-flight F7).** `_episode_start`'s ghost calls `events.cancel(state, pid, events.REPLY_FAMILY)` (`read`, `reply`, `initiate`, `double_text`, `episode_lapse`), never the whole queue: a player's `promise_close` (keyed by the person's pid), a `plan_lapse`, a `life` or an `offer_number` event stays, and open promises and plans are voided through their own handlers (T3).
32. **The migration is one kind with two ops, and `schema` is written last (pre-flight F6).** Spec §16.1: "One `social_migrate` effect kind … It sets `schema: 2` … never half-migrated." The kind stays one; its handler has `op: apply` (every table row) and `op: seal` (`schema: 2`), applied after every other write. `_plan` decides everything first, each read's `when` included, and any exception is a `MigrationError`, so a fault mid-upgrade leaves no slice marked done (T20).
33. **The voice degrades below the configured window, not a literal (pre-flight, T7 note).** Spec §7.4 says "a binding under 8192 tokens". `voicing.window_for` reads `degraded = usable_context < social.voice.window_tokens` (8192 by default), so the two agree at the default and a configured window moves both together (rule 5).

## Review Focus

These are the five input classes or failure modes most likely to bite a player that no spec-named test covers. Each has its test in the owning task, marked **(Review Focus N)**.

1. **Windows that cross midnight.** The player says "message me in the morning" at 00:30, or "tonight" at 23:40. A person expects "morning" to mean the next morning after now, and "tonight" said at 23:40 to mean the rest of tonight, not tomorrow night. The test lives in T4: `test_windows_resolve_across_midnight`, which covers 00:30, 06:59, 07:01, 23:40, and a person on nights.
2. **A text sent while a world turn is running** (the side-by-side layout at 1200 px). The send is refused busy ("A turn is already in progress."). A person expects their typed words to stay in the compose box and to be sendable once the turn ends, not lost. The tests:
   - T22, client: `keeps the draft when a send is refused busy`;
   - T11, server: `test_a_send_during_a_world_turn_is_busy_and_changes_nothing`.
3. **Migrating a person who has gone.** A v0.22 save has a thread whose last word is the player's, but the person is unmatched, blocked, ended or ghosted. A person expects no reply to appear from someone who left. The migration's "answer the unanswered message" row must skip them. The test lives in T20: `test_migration_queues_no_read_for_a_person_who_left`.
4. **Topic labels with punctuation in the energy and agreement matches.** For example "their sister's band", "climbing (bouldering)" or "C++". A person expects naming one to count, and a regex character never to crash a reply. The test lives in T3: `test_a_topic_label_with_punctuation_matches_whole_words`. The matcher uses `re.escape` with word boundaries computed on the label's own edge characters.
5. **Going to bed early or late.** Sleeping at 06:30 must not "sleep" 30 minutes. Sleeping at 02:00 lands at 07:00 the same day. Sleeping at 21:00 lands at 07:00 the next day. A promise that falls in the night is still kept, and shown on waking, when "wake me" is off. The test lives in T11: `test_sleep_lands_in_the_morning_and_promises_wait` (Plan decision 12).

## Shared interfaces (every task uses these names exactly)

### The slice, schema 2 (`engine/social/kinds.py`, T1)

```python
SCHEMA: int = 2

def empty_slice() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema": SCHEMA,
        "player": {}, "seq": 0, "queue": [], "people": {}, "threads": {},
        "notifications": [], "apps": {}, "requests": {}, "media": {}, "games": {},
        "meets": {},                         # v0.22's; T12 stops writing it, T19 removes the key
        "promises": {},                      # promise id -> §5.1 row (T4)
        "plans": {},                         # plan id -> §9.1 row (T12)
        "scene": {},                         # the one open scene, {} when none (T13)
        "household": "",                     # the partner's pid, "" when none (T18)
        "phone_up": True,                    # the phone is in the player's hand (T11)
        "at_work": False,                    # the player is at work (T11)
        "settings": {"receipts": True, "wake_for_messages": False},
        "open_thread": "",
        "counters": {"message": 0, "request": 0, "media": 0, "game": 0, "meet": 0,
                     "episode": 0, "promise": 0, "plan": 0},   # notifications: v0.22's setdefault'd "notification" (F25)
        "last_active_wall": 0.0, "dirty_reason": "",
    }
```

Per person, `people[pid]` gains (each written only by its handler):

| Key | Shape | Writer | Task |
|---|---|---|---|
| `attrs` | `{"habits": {"at_work", "late_night", "in_company"}, "pace": float, "topics": [topic ids], "home": template id}` | `social_person` `set` via `attrs.materialise` | T1-T10 |
| `growth` | `{"depth": 0, "openness": 0.0, "pace": float, "guarded": 0, "guarded_left": 0, "revealed": [], "peak_depth": 0, "reveal_due": ""}` | `social_growth` | T2 |
| `episode` | §4.1's row, or None | `social_episode` | T3 |
| `episodes_done`, `scenes_done` | int | `social_episode` close / `social_scene` end | T3, T13 |
| `memory` | §7.1's parts | `social_memory` | T6 |
| `recap_due` | closed episodes awaiting a recap | `social_episode` close | T3 |
| `last_episode` | the last closed episode's id (keys the between-episode cap) | `social_episode` close | T5 (F36) |
| `life` | `[{id, at, until?}]` | `social_life` | T17 |

Slice-level keys written by `setdefault` in their own handler (not in `empty_slice`): `work_until` and `buzz_from` (`social_settings`, T11) and `player_life`, the PLAYER's life events, `[{id, at, until?}]` (`social_life` with `who == "player"`, T17; pre-flight F28).

The episode row (§4.1, plus the engine's own fields):

```python
{"id": "e<n>", "kind": "chat" | "scene", "thread_id": str, "location": str, "started_at": float,
 "last_at": float, "exchanges": int, "target": int, "energy": float, "mode": "quick",
 "end": "none" | "winding" | "signed_off" | "lapsed", "end_reason": str, "promise_id": str,
 "topics": [topic ids], "warmth_at_open": float, "after_left": int, "away_until": float | None,
 "player_chars": [int, ...]}           # the last talk.player_chars_kept lengths of the player's messages
```

### `Decision` (`engine/social/kinds.py`, T1; Plan decision 10)

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    situation: str
    kind: str = "text"
    max_bubbles: int = 3
    media_kind: str = ""
    subject: str = ""
    reveal: str = ""
    request_id: str = ""
    game_id: str = ""
    meet_id: str = ""
    # v0.23.0 (spec §4.5, §5.2, §7.3; Plan decision 10)
    mode: str = "quick"           # quick | chatting | deep
    min_bubbles: int = 1
    end: str = "none"             # none | winding | sign_off
    reason: str = ""              # the engine's words for why, voiced by the model
    promise_id: str = ""
    followup_id: str = ""
    plan_id: str = ""

MODES: tuple[str, ...] = ("quick", "chatting", "deep")
DECISION_ENDS: tuple[str, ...] = ("none", "winding", "sign_off")
```

### Effect kinds (`engine/social/kinds.py`, T1)

`SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` gains each new kind IN THE TASK THAT LANDS ITS HANDLER, never earlier (pre-flight F1, Plan decision 23): `"social_growth"` (T2), `"social_episode"` (T3), `"social_promise"` (T4), `"social_go"` (T11), `"social_plan"` (T12), `"social_scene"` (T13, which also REMOVES v0.22's `"social_meet"` with `meets.py`'s handler), `"social_life"` (T17), `"social_household"` (T18) and `"social_migrate"` (T20). T1 changes the frozenset not at all. So `test_social_seam.py::test_every_social_kind_has_a_handler` (v0.22, kept, in `<GUARDS>`) is green at every task. Each of those tasks also adds its kind to `tests/test_social_slice_v2.py::NEW_KINDS` (and T13 adds `social_meet` to `RETIRED_KINDS`).

- Produces: `kinds.SCHEMA`, `kinds.empty_slice()`, `kinds.Decision` (new fields), `kinds.STAGES`/`STAGE_RANKS` (new stages), `kinds.SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` (UNCHANGED at T1; F1), `kinds.MODES`, `kinds.DECISION_ENDS`; `rng.SOCIAL_CONVO/GROWTH/LIFE/SCENE`; `social.scene_open`, `social.world_dir`, `social.FILES`; `attrs.drawer`, `attrs.of`, `attrs.materialise`, `attrs.key`.

`engine/social/attrs.py`, complete:

```python
"""
Fixed per-person attributes (v0.23.0 spec §4.2, I14; Plan decision 7).

Each attribute is drawn ONCE per person from a counterless key,
``stable_rng(seed, f"{stream}:{pid}:{name}")`` -- never ``person_rng`` -- so
no draw order anywhere can change it. Its owning module registers a drawer
here at import; ``materialise`` writes them all at first match through
``social_person`` (rule 3); ``of`` answers a read path from the stored row,
computing any missing attribute from the same key and WRITING NOTHING.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import random
from typing import Any, Callable

from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
from engine.game.rng import stable_rng
from engine.game.state import GameState

Drawer = Callable[[GameState, str, random.Random], Any]

#: name -> (stream, drawer). Filled at import by each owning module (registration).
FIXED: dict[str, tuple[str, Drawer]] = {}


def drawer(name: str, stream: str) -> Callable[[Drawer], Drawer]:
    """Register ``fn(state, pid, rng) -> value`` as the drawer of attribute ``name`` on ``stream``."""
    def register(fn: Drawer) -> Drawer:
        FIXED[name] = (stream, fn)
        return fn
    return register


def key(seed: int, stream: str, pid: str, name: str) -> random.Random:
    """The attribute's own generator: counterless, so it is the same whenever it is asked."""
    return stable_rng(seed, f"{stream}:{pid}:{name}")


def _compute(state: GameState, pid: str, name: str) -> Any:
    stream, fn = FIXED[name]
    return fn(state, pid, key(state.rng_seed, stream, pid, name))


def of(state: GameState, pid: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Every registered attribute of ``pid``: stored where materialised, computed (never written) where not."""
    stored = ((state.social.get("people") or {}).get(pid) or {}).get("attrs") or {}
    out = dict(stored)
    for name in FIXED:
        if name not in out:
            out[name] = _compute(state, pid, name)
    return out


def materialise(state: GameState, pid: str) -> None:
    """Write every registered attribute not yet stored, once, through ``social_person`` (rule 3)."""
    stored = ((state.social.get("people") or {}).get(pid) or {}).get("attrs") or {}
    missing = {name: _compute(state, pid, name) for name in FIXED if name not in stored}
    if missing:
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_person", "pid": pid, "set": {"attrs": {**stored, **missing}}})
```

Package additions (`engine/social/__init__.py`):

```python
FILES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "creation", "population", "behaviours", "apps", "relationships", "media", "venues", "games",
    "conversation", "promises", "growth", "topics", "layers", "plans", "scenes", "life_events", "homes",
)


def world_dir() -> Optional[Path]:
    """The directory of `paths.locations` (Plan decision 6): `activities.yaml` is found beside it."""
    rel = str(get_config().get("paths.locations", "") or "").strip()
    return (_ROOT / rel).parent if rel else None


def scene_open(state: Any) -> bool:
    """
    Whether a social scene owns the turn (spec §10.1; Plan decision 15).
    Reads `state.social["scene"]` only: no submodule is imported, so
    `intents.scene_owns_turn` and `director.ensure_scene` may ask it for
    every story. False unless the running story declares `paths.social`.
    """
    if not declared():
        return False
    return bool(((getattr(state, "social", None) or {}).get("scene") or {}).get("pid"))
```

`load_all` builds `cached["activities"] = load_file(world_dir(), "activities")` when `world_dir()` is set (and `{}` otherwise). `boot()` gains `from engine.social import attrs  # noqa: F401  (v0.23 T1)` as its FIRST line, so every drawer registered later finds the registry.

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from tests.social_fixtures import matched, receipts_active, receipts_state  # noqa: F401


def test_the_slice_v2_skeleton() -> None:
    from engine.social import kinds

    slice_ = kinds.empty_slice()
    assert slice_["schema"] == kinds.SCHEMA == 2
    for key in ("promises", "plans", "scene", "household", "phone_up", "at_work"):
        assert key in slice_, key
    assert slice_["phone_up"] is True and slice_["at_work"] is False and slice_["household"] == ""
    assert "auto_wait" not in slice_["settings"] and slice_["settings"]["wake_for_messages"] is False
    assert {"episode", "promise", "plan"} <= set(slice_["counters"])
    assert "notify" not in slice_["counters"]          # F25: v0.22's "notification" counter is reused


#: The v0.23 effect kinds that have LANDED, each with its handler (pre-flight F1, Plan decision 23).
#: Empty at T1. Each task that lands a handler adds its kind here AND to kinds.SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS
#: in the same commit: T2 social_growth, T3 social_episode, T4 social_promise, T11 social_go,

RETIRED_KINDS: set[str] = set()


def test_the_landed_kinds_are_engine_only() -> None:

    # because no kind is named before its handler is registered.

    assert stage_rank("exclusive") < stage_rank("living_together") < stage_rank("engaged") < stage_rank("married")
    assert stage_rank("winding_down") == -1


def test_the_four_new_streams_are_distinct() -> None:
    from engine.game import rng

    names = [rng.SOCIAL_CONVO, rng.SOCIAL_GROWTH, rng.SOCIAL_LIFE, rng.SOCIAL_SCENE]

        "quick", 1, "none", "", "", "", "")


def test_an_attribute_is_the_same_whatever_was_drawn_before(receipts_state) -> None:
    """Counterless (I14): drawing a person's other streams first never changes an attribute."""
    from engine.game.rng import SOCIAL_CONVO
    from engine.social import attrs, behaviour

    @attrs.drawer("probe_t1", SOCIAL_CONVO)

    finally:
        receipts_state.social["scene"] = {}


def test_read_paths_draw_and_write_nothing(receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    """§19.1 (I14): building the payload and every hint leaves the save and every counter unchanged.
    Extended by each task that adds a read path (T3 availability, T4 promises, T5 hints, T11 world)."""
    from engine.social import payload

    matched("matchstick")
    before = json.dumps(receipts_state.to_save_dict(), sort_keys=True, default=str)
    for _ in range(3):
        payload.phone_block(receipts_state)
        receipts_state.to_client_dict()
    assert json.dumps(receipts_state.to_save_dict(), sort_keys=True, default=str) == before
```

**The closed vocabulary, in code (the rule-12 guard's anchor):**

```python
RUNGS: tuple[tuple[str, frozenset[str]], ...] = (
    ("small_talk", frozenset({"places", "hobbies", "work_outline", "the_apps"})),
    ("everyday", frozenset({"daily_life", "work", "friends", "plans", "opinions"})),
    ("personal_stories", frozenset({"upbringing", "family_outline", "past", "travel", "proud_of"})),
    ("hopes_and_fears", frozenset({"hopes", "worries", "regrets", "values"})),
    ("family_and_future", frozenset({"family", "future_plans", "home_life", "money"})),
)
RUNG_IDS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(rung for rung, _ in RUNGS)
KINDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset().union(*(kinds for _, kinds in RUNGS))
COMMITMENTS: tuple[str, ...] = ("number", "meet", "come_over", "go_to_theirs", "stay_over",
                                "exclusive", "meet_family", "move_in", "propose", "stay_or_go")
DEPTH_COMMITMENTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"number", "meet"})

DEPTH_ROW_KEYS = frozenset({"id", "kinds", "floors", "counts"})
FLOOR_KEYS = frozenset({"warmth", "trust"})
COUNT_KEYS = frozenset({"episodes", "scenes"})
TOPIC_KEYS = frozenset({"id", "kind", "label", "when", "follows_up"})
LAYER_KEYS = frozenset({"id", "kind", "text", "when"})
WHEN_KEYS = frozenset({"job", "job_shape", "flag", "hobby", "life"})
WILLING_KEYS = frozenset({"stage_min", "stages", "warmth", "trust", "episodes", "scenes", "needs",
                          "depth", "days_since_exclusive", "no_household"})
TOP_KEYS = frozenset({"kinds", "depths", "pace", "trait_mult", "gain", "loss", "guarded_episodes",
                      "topics_per_kind", "life_lines", "closer_soon_at", "willing"})

#: Gains that a later task switches on (Plan decision 11; spec §24's stub table).
_STUBBED: set[str] = {"moment", "promise_kept", "support"}
```

`check_data`, complete:

```python
def _fault(file: str, key: str, message: str) -> ValueError:
    return ValueError(f"social: {file}.yaml: {key}: {message}")


def _unknown(file: str, where: str, row: dict[str, Any], allowed: frozenset[str]) -> None:
    extra = sorted(set(row) - allowed)
    if extra:
        raise _fault(file, where, f"unknown key(s) {extra} (allowed: {sorted(allowed)})")


def _check_when(file: str, where: str, when: Any) -> None:
    if when is None:
        return
    if not isinstance(when, dict):
        raise _fault(file, f"{where}.when", "must be a mapping")
    _unknown(file, f"{where}.when", when, WHEN_KEYS)


def check_data(growth: dict[str, Any], topics: dict[str, Any], layers: dict[str, Any], *,
               traits: frozenset[str] = frozenset()) -> None:
    """Refuse at load anything outside the closed disclosure vocabulary (spec §6.7, §19.6; rule 12).
    With ``traits`` (population.yaml's trait ids, passed by ``_check_loaded``), every ``trait_mult``
    key must be a real trait, so no multiplier is dead data (pre-flight F23)."""
    if not growth:
        return
    _unknown("growth", "(top)", growth, TOP_KEYS)
    if traits:
        stray = sorted(set(growth.get("trait_mult") or {}) - set(traits))
        if stray:
            raise _fault("growth", "trait_mult", f"{stray} are not population traits")
    data_kinds = set(growth.get("kinds") or ())
    if data_kinds != set(KINDS):
        raise _fault("growth", "kinds", f"must be exactly the engine's vocabulary; "
                                        f"extra {sorted(data_kinds - KINDS)}, missing {sorted(KINDS - data_kinds)}")
    depths = growth.get("depths") or []
    if [row.get("id") for row in depths] != list(RUNG_IDS):
        raise _fault("growth", "depths", f"must list the rungs in order: {list(RUNG_IDS)}")
    for i, row in enumerate(depths):
        where = f"depths[{i}]"
        _unknown("growth", where, row, DEPTH_ROW_KEYS)
        own = RUNGS[i][1]
        listed = set(row.get("kinds") or ())
        if not listed <= own:
            raise _fault("growth", f"{where}.kinds", f"{sorted(listed - own)} are not {row['id']} kinds")
        _unknown("growth", f"{where}.floors", row.get("floors") or {}, FLOOR_KEYS)
        _unknown("growth", f"{where}.counts", row.get("counts") or {}, COUNT_KEYS)
        for key, value in (row.get("counts") or {}).items():
            if not isinstance(value, int) or value < 0:
                raise _fault("growth", f"{where}.counts.{key}", "a whole number of episodes or scenes, never days")
    for name, gate in (growth.get("willing") or {}).items():
        where = f"willing.{name}"
        if name not in COMMITMENTS:
            raise _fault("growth", where, f"not a commitment ({list(COMMITMENTS)})")
        _unknown("growth", where, gate, WILLING_KEYS)
        if "depth" in gate and name not in DEPTH_COMMITMENTS:
            raise _fault("growth", f"{where}.depth", "only number and meet may read depth (rule 12)")
        if "days_since_exclusive" in gate and name != "move_in":
            raise _fault("growth", f"{where}.days_since_exclusive", "only move_in counts days (R15)")
    for file, rows, allowed in (("topics", topics.get("topics") or [], TOPIC_KEYS),
                                ("layers", layers.get("layers") or [], LAYER_KEYS)):
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            where = f"{file}[{i}]"
            _unknown(file, where, row, allowed)
            if row.get("kind") not in KINDS:
                raise _fault(file, f"{where}.kind", f"{row.get('kind')!r} is not in the disclosure vocabulary")
            if row.get("id") in seen:
                raise _fault(file, f"{where}.id", f"{row.get('id')!r} is repeated")
            seen.add(str(row.get("id")))
            _check_when(file, where, row.get("when"))
```

**Pace and topics, the two drawers:**

```python
@attrs.drawer("pace", SOCIAL_GROWTH)
def _pace(state: GameState, pid: str, rng: random.Random) -> float:
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    arch_pace = float(behaviour.archetype(person.archetype).growth.get("pace", 1.0))
    mults = _data().get("trait_mult") or {}
    traits = math.prod(float(mults.get(t, 1.0)) for t in person.traits)
    return round(arch_pace * traits * _jitter(rng), 4)


def _jitter(rng: random.Random) -> float:
    """growth.yaml `pace.jitter`, lognormal (split out so a test can force it: lesson 1)."""
    return math.exp(float((_data().get("pace") or {}).get("jitter", 0.2)) * rng.gauss(0, 1))


@attrs.drawer("topics", SOCIAL_GROWTH)
def _topics(state: GameState, pid: str, rng: random.Random) -> list[str]:
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    per = int(_data().get("topics_per_kind", 1))
    rows = [r for r in social.data("topics").get("topics") or [] if profile_when(person, {}, r.get("when"))]
    chosen: list[str] = []
    for kind in sorted(KINDS):
        pool = sorted((r for r in rows if r["kind"] == kind), key=lambda r: r["id"])
        chosen.extend(r["id"] for r in rng.sample(pool, min(per, len(pool))))
    return chosen
```

**The growth record and the handler** (every write, rule 3):

```python
def _fresh(state: GameState, pid: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"depth": 0, "openness": 0.0, "pace": float(attrs.of(state, pid)["pace"]), "guarded": 0,
            "guarded_left": 0, "revealed": [], "peak_depth": 0, "reveal_due": ""}


def record(state: GameState, pid: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Read-only: the stored record, or a fresh one computed and NOT written (I14)."""
    stored = ((state.social.get("people") or {}).get(pid) or {}).get("growth")
    return dict(stored) if stored else _fresh(state, pid)


def depth(state: GameState, pid: str) -> int:
    return int(record(state, pid)["depth"])


def _cap(state: GameState, pid: str, *, in_person: bool) -> int:
    arch = behaviour.archetype(population.profile(state, pid).archetype).growth
    return int(arch.get("cap_in_person" if in_person else "cap", 4))


def gain(state: GameState, pid: str, event: str, *, mode: str = "", band: str = "") -> float:
    """Openness from an engine-measured event (spec §6.3). The model never writes openness."""
    if event in _STUBBED:
        return 0.0
    g = _data().get("gain") or {}
    rec = record(state, pid)
    if event == "exchange":
        amount = float(g.get("exchange", 0)) * rec["pace"] * float((g.get("mode_mult") or {}).get(mode, 1.0))
    elif event == "good_end":
        amount = float(g.get("good_end", 0)) * rec["pace"]
    elif event == "moment":
        amount = float((g.get("moment") or {}).get(band, 0)) * rec["pace"]
    else:                                               # promise_kept, support: flat, not paced
        amount = float(g.get(event, 0))
    if rec["guarded_left"] > 0:
        amount /= 2.0 ** int(rec["guarded"])
    if amount <= 0:
        return 0.0
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_growth", "op": "gain", "pid": pid, "amount": amount,
                         "in_person": event == "moment"})
    return amount


def loss(state: GameState, pid: str, event: str) -> float:
    """Openness lost (spec §6.3). An archetype's `growth.decay_mult` scales every loss: a ghoster's
    openness "decays twice as fast" (§6.2) -- the spec names no separate decay, so a loss IS the decay."""
    arch = behaviour.archetype(population.profile(state, pid).archetype).growth
    amount = float((_data().get("loss") or {}).get(event, 0)) * float(arch.get("decay_mult", 1.0))
    if amount > 0:
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_growth", "op": "gain", "pid": pid, "amount": -amount})
    return amount


@effect_kind("social_growth")
@recorded
def _e_growth(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pid = str(effect["pid"])
    person = state.social.setdefault("people", {}).setdefault(pid, {})
    rec = person.get("growth") or _fresh(state, pid)
    op = effect.get("op")
    if op == "gain":
        rec["openness"] = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(rec["openness"]) + float(effect["amount"])))
        person["growth"] = rec
        stepped = _maybe_step(state, pid, rec, in_person=bool(effect.get("in_person")))
        return {"type": "social_growth", "ok": True, "pid": pid, "stepped": stepped}
    if op == "guard":                                    # a betrayal (§6.4, §12.4): guarded +1
        rec["guarded"] = int(rec["guarded"]) + 1
        rec["guarded_left"] = int(_data().get("guarded_episodes", 6))
    elif op == "tick":                                   # one episode closed: the guarded count runs down
        rec["guarded_left"] = max(0, int(rec["guarded_left"]) - 1)
    elif op == "reveal":                                 # the queued layer voiced (`opens_up`)
        rec["revealed"] = [*rec["revealed"], str(effect["layer"])]
        rec["reveal_due"] = ""
    person["growth"] = rec
    return {"type": "social_growth", "ok": True, "pid": pid, "op": op}


def _floors_hold(person: dict[str, Any], row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    floors, counts = row.get("floors") or {}, row.get("counts") or {}
    return (float(person.get("warmth", 0)) >= float(floors.get("warmth", 0))
            and float(person.get("trust", 0)) >= float(floors.get("trust", 0))
            and int(person.get("episodes_done", 0)) >= int(counts.get("episodes", 0))
            and int(person.get("scenes_done", 0)) >= int(counts.get("scenes", 0)))


def _maybe_step(state: GameState, pid: str, rec: dict[str, Any], *, in_person: bool) -> bool:
    """Inside the handler: openness 100, the next rung's floors and counts, the cap -> one rung up (§6.4)."""
    current = int(rec["depth"])
    if rec["openness"] < 100.0 or current >= len(RUNGS) - 1 or current + 1 > _cap(state, pid, in_person=in_person):
        return False
    person = state.social["people"][pid]
    row = (_data().get("depths") or [])[current + 1]
    if not _floors_hold(person, row):
        return False
    rec["depth"], rec["openness"] = current + 1, 0.0
    rec["peak_depth"] = max(int(rec["peak_depth"]), current + 1)
    rec["reveal_due"] = _next_layer(state, pid, current + 1, rec["revealed"])
    from engine.social import relationships   # lazily: relationships imports growth from T5
    relationships.add_history(state, pid, "growth", _STEP_LINES[RUNG_IDS[current + 1]])
    return True
```

`_STEP_LINES` maps each rung to its history row, e.g. `"personal_stories": "Opened up about their past"`. `_next_layer(state, pid, rung, revealed) -> str` draws from `layers.yaml` rows whose `kind` is in that rung's kinds, whose `when` holds and which are not yet revealed. It uses `attrs.key(state.rng_seed, SOCIAL_GROWTH, pid, f"layer:{rung}")`, so the draw is counterless, and returns "" when none holds. `profile_when(person, person_state, when)` reads `job` (a list of job ids), `job_shape`, `flag`, `hobby` and `life`. The `life` key matches entries in `person_state["life"]` and is empty until T17. (It is deliberately not named `when_holds`: `messaging.when_holds(state, pid, when, at)` already exists for an initiation's `when`; pre-flight F25.)

**`who_now` (§6.6)**, read-only: the voice's block. It contains:
- name, age, job, neighbourhood, and `_home_line` (stub "");
- traits and the texting style. At depth ≥ 2 with `in_episode_mode` in `("chatting", "deep")`, a `short` style reads `medium`;
- at depth 0 only, the dating bio and the prompt answers;
- "Since you met:" followed by `_since_lines` (stub []);
- "What you talk about with them now:" and the labels of `topics_of(state, pid, up_to=depth)`;
- "What you still keep to yourself:" and the labels of the next rung's topics.

T2 ships the home line and "Since you met" as stubs (§24's table). `test_who_now_names_their_home` ships SKIPPED, in `tests/test_social_growth.py`, with reason "T10 replaces the home stub". T10 un-skips it IN PLACE in that file and writes no copy anywhere (pre-flight F14).

**`growth.yaml` (first values; §6.4's table):**

```yaml
kinds: [places, hobbies, work_outline, the_apps, daily_life, work, friends, plans, opinions,
        upbringing, family_outline, past, travel, proud_of, hopes, worries, regrets, values,
        family, future_plans, home_life, money]
depths:
  - {id: small_talk, kinds: [places, hobbies, work_outline, the_apps], floors: {}, counts: {}}
  - {id: everyday, kinds: [daily_life, work, friends, plans, opinions], floors: {warmth: 25, trust: 18}, counts: {episodes: 1}}
  - {id: personal_stories, kinds: [upbringing, family_outline, past, travel, proud_of], floors: {warmth: 40, trust: 32}, counts: {episodes: 3, scenes: 1}}
  - {id: hopes_and_fears, kinds: [hopes, worries, regrets, values], floors: {warmth: 55, trust: 48}, counts: {scenes: 3}}
  - {id: family_and_future, kinds: [family, future_plans, home_life, money], floors: {warmth: 68, trust: 62}, counts: {scenes: 8}}
pace: {jitter: 0.2}
trait_mult: {private: 0.7, shy: 0.7, warm: 1.3, confident: 1.3}   # real population.yaml trait ids (F23)
gain: {exchange: 6, good_end: 10, moment: {lands: 14, fine: 6, flat: 0, misstep: 0},
       promise_kept: 8, support: 15, mode_mult: {quick: 0.6, chatting: 1.0, deep: 1.5}}
loss: {broken_promise: 12, left_on_read: 6, fight: 10, too_deep: 8}

```

`topics.yaml` holds at least 2 topics per kind, so 44 or more rows of `{id: t_<slug>, kind, label, when?, follows_up?}`. Examples:
- `{id: t_shifts, kind: work, label: their shifts, when: {job_shape: [shifts]}}`
- `{id: t_sister, kind: family_outline, label: their sister}`
- `{id: t_the_apps, kind: the_apps, label: how the apps are going}`

`layers.yaml` holds at least 3 layers per kind for rungs 1-4, so 54 or more rows of `{id: l_<slug>, kind, text, when?}`. Example: `{id: l_dad_fisherman, kind: family_outline, text: "Their dad was a fisherman out of the Quays", when: {}}`.

Each text is a plain fact, and none names a physical act (rule 12; the structural test is below).

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy

import pytest

from tests.social_fixtures import _set_person, matched, receipts_active, receipts_state  # noqa: F401


@pytest.fixture
def paced(monkeypatch):
    """Lesson 1 / pre-flight F16: the openness loops never ride the drawn pace. `_jitter` is forced to 1.0
    (before `matched` materialises the attribute), and `pin(state, pid)` stores pace 1.0 outright, so
    neither the archetype nor the traits can move a loop's count."""
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.social import attrs, growth

    monkeypatch.setattr(growth, "_jitter", lambda rng: 1.0)

    def pin(state, pid: str, pace: float = 1.0) -> None:
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_person", "pid": pid,
                             "set": {"attrs": {**attrs.of(state, pid), "pace": pace}}})
    return pin


def _with_data(**over):
    """A copy of the loaded growth/topics/layers with `over` applied, for the load-error tests."""
    from engine import social

    data = {name: copy.deepcopy(social.data(name)) for name in ("growth", "topics", "layers")}
    for name, change in over.items():
        change(data[name])
    return data


def test_rungs_are_built_from_the_closed_vocabulary(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine.social import growth

    loaded = _with_data()
    growth.check_data(loaded["growth"], loaded["topics"], loaded["layers"])     # the shipped data passes
    for i, row in enumerate(loaded["growth"]["depths"]):
        assert row["id"] == growth.RUNG_IDS[i]
        assert set(row["kinds"]) <= growth.RUNGS[i][1]
        assert set(row) <= growth.DEPTH_ROW_KEYS
    for row in loaded["topics"]["topics"] + loaded["layers"]["layers"]:
        assert row["kind"] in growth.KINDS

    bad_key = _with_data(growth=lambda g: g["depths"][2].update({"willing": ["x"]}))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"growth\.yaml: depths\[2\]: unknown key"):
        growth.check_data(bad_key["growth"], bad_key["topics"], bad_key["layers"])
    bad_kind = _with_data(topics=lambda t: t["topics"].append({"id": "t_x", "kind": "zz_q7", "label": "x"}))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"topics\.yaml: .*kind: 'zz_q7' is not in the disclosure vocabulary"):
        growth.check_data(bad_kind["growth"], bad_kind["topics"], bad_kind["layers"])
    wrong_rung = _with_data(growth=lambda g: g["depths"][1]["kinds"].append("money"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"depths\[1\]\.kinds"):
        growth.check_data(wrong_rung["growth"], wrong_rung["topics"], wrong_rung["layers"])

def test_openness_fills_and_a_rung_steps_with_a_reveal(paced, receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    from engine.social import growth

    pid = matched("matchstick").split(":")[1]
    paced(receipts_state, pid)
    _set_person(receipts_state, pid, warmth=30, trust=20, episodes_done=1)
    for _ in range(60):
        growth.gain(receipts_state, pid, "exchange", mode="deep")
        if growth.depth(receipts_state, pid) == 1:
            break
    rec = growth.record(receipts_state, pid)
    assert rec["depth"] == 1 and rec["openness"] == 0.0 and rec["peak_depth"] == 1
    assert rec["reveal_due"] == "" or rec["reveal_due"].startswith("l_")

def test_depth_never_goes_down_and_guarded_halves(paced, receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.social import growth

    pid = matched("matchstick").split(":")[1]
    paced(receipts_state, pid)
    plain = growth.gain(receipts_state, pid, "good_end")
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_growth", "op": "guard", "pid": pid})
    halved = growth.gain(receipts_state, pid, "good_end")
    assert halved == pytest.approx(plain / 2)
    # Step to depth 1 FIRST, so "never goes down" is not asserted vacuously at depth 0 (pre-flight T2 note).
    _set_person(receipts_state, pid, warmth=99, trust=99, episodes_done=9)
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_growth", "op": "gain", "pid": pid, "amount": 100})
    assert growth.depth(receipts_state, pid) == 1
    for _ in range(20):
        growth.loss(receipts_state, pid, "fight")
    assert growth.depth(receipts_state, pid) == 1 and growth.record(receipts_state, pid)["openness"] == 0.0

def test_a_bad_rung_in_a_story_copy_fails_the_load(receipts_active, tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """§19.6 "loads a story copy" (pre-flight F39): the guard is wired into `load_all`, not only callable.
    The v0.22 pattern of tests/test_social_relationships.py (copytree, `social_dir` patched, cache nulled)."""
    import shutil
    from pathlib import Path

    import yaml

    from engine import social

    src = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "games" / "read-receipts" / "data" / "social"
    shutil.copytree(src, tmp_path / "social")
    path = tmp_path / "social" / "growth.yaml"
    data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    data["depths"][1]["kinds"].append("money")                 # a family_and_future kind on everyday
    path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(data), encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(social, "social_dir", lambda: tmp_path / "social")
    monkeypatch.setattr(social, "_DATA_CACHE", None)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"growth\.yaml: depths\[1\]\.kinds"):
        social.load_all()
    assert social._DATA_CACHE is None
```
