**Window resolution (`engine/social/windows.py`), complete for T3's initiation windows.** T4 adds the promise windows to the same function.

```python
"""
Named windows resolved against a schedule (v0.23.0 spec §4.7, §5.1).

A window is resolved against ONE schedule: a person's (`population.profile`)
or the player's (their creation job's shape). Everything is derived; nothing
draws or writes. `resolve` returns the next occurrence of the window that is
not over at `at`, as absolute in-game hours `(start, end)`, or None when this
schedule has no such window within a week.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any, Optional

from engine import social
from engine.game.state import GameState
from engine.social import behaviour, population

INITIATION_WINDOWS: tuple[str, ...] = ("waking", "commute", "lunch", "after_work", "evening", "late")
PROMISE_WINDOWS: tuple[str, ...] = ("lunch", "after_work", "tonight", "morning", "weekend", "at_work", "later")

_DAY = 24.0


def schedule_of(state: GameState, who: str) -> Any:
    """Something with `.job_shape`: the person, or the player (their job's shape; office when none).
    The player's job is stored by creation under `player["attributes"]["job"]` (creation.yaml's
    `fields.attributes.job`), the key `agreement.py` already reads -- never `player["job"]` (pre-flight F24)."""
    if who != "player":
        return population.profile(state, who)
    player = state.social.get("player") or {}
    job = str((player.get("attributes") or {}).get("job") or "")
    shapes = {str(r.get("id")): str(r.get("shape")) for r in social.data("population").get("jobs") or []}
    return SimpleNamespace(job_shape=shapes.get(job, "office"))


def _bands(sched: Any, day: int) -> list[tuple[float, float, str]]:
    week = behaviour._week(sched.job_shape)
    rows = week.get(behaviour.WEEKDAYS[day % 7]) or []
    return sorted(((day * _DAY + float(a), day * _DAY + float(b), str(s)) for a, b, s in rows),
                  key=lambda band: band[0])


def _on_day(sched: Any, window: str, day: int) -> Optional[tuple[float, float]]:
    bands = _bands(sched, day)
    awake = [b for b in bands if b[2] != "asleep"]
    work = [b for b in bands if b[2] == "work"]
    base = day * _DAY
    if window == "waking" and awake:
        return awake[0][0], awake[0][0] + 1.0
    if window == "commute" and work:
        return work[0][0] - 1.0, work[0][0]
    if window == "lunch":
        noon = (base + 12.0, base + 14.0)
        if any(b[0] < noon[1] and b[1] > noon[0] for b in awake):
            return noon
        return None
    if window == "after_work" and work:
        return work[-1][1], work[-1][1] + 2.0
    if window == "evening":
        span = (base + 18.0, base + 22.0)
        return span if any(b[0] < span[1] and b[1] > span[0] and b[2] != "work" for b in awake) else None
    if window == "late":
        span = (base + 22.0, base + 25.0)
        return span if any(b[1] > span[0] for b in awake) else None
    return None


def resolve(state: GameState, who: str, window: str, at: float) -> Optional[tuple[float, float]]:
    """The next occurrence of `window` for `who` that has not ended by `at` (clipped to start no earlier than `at`)."""
    sched = schedule_of(state, who)
    first = int(at // _DAY)
    for day in range(first - 1, first + 8):          # the day before: a window that crosses midnight
        span = _on_day(sched, window, day)
        if span is not None and span[1] > at:
            return max(span[0], at), span[1]
    return None
```

T4 extends `_on_day` with `morning`, `tonight`, `weekend`, `at_work` and `at:<hour>`, adds `later` and `first_free` (Review Focus 1's test lives there), and adds the speaker rule.

**Availability and the boundary (§4.2, §4.4), complete:**

```python
class Availability(NamedTuple):
    yes: bool
    cap: str = ""          # "" | "quick" | "glance"


@attrs.drawer("habits", SOCIAL_CONVO)
def _habits(state: GameState, pid: str, rng: random.Random) -> dict[str, str]:
    arch = population.profile(state, pid).archetype
    weights = (_data().get("habits") or {}).get("weights") or {}
    table = weights.get(arch) or weights.get("default") or {}
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for habit in ("at_work", "late_night", "in_company"):
        options = sorted((table.get(habit) or {}).items())
        names, counts = [n for n, _ in options], [float(w) for _, w in options]
        out[habit] = rng.choices(names, weights=counts)[0] if sum(counts) > 0 else names[0]
    return out


def _status(state: GameState, pid: str, at: float) -> str:
    """Their schedule status with habits applied: a night owl is still up in the first hours of their sleep band."""
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    status = behaviour.status_at(person, at)
    if status == "asleep" and attrs.of(state, pid)["habits"]["late_night"] == "night_owl":
        shift = float((_data().get("habits") or {}).get("night_owl_shift_hours", 3))
        if behaviour.status_at(person, at - shift) != "asleep":
            return "free"
    return status


def available(state: GameState, pid: str, at: float) -> Availability:
    """Derived: will they answer a cold message now? Draws nothing, writes nothing (I14)."""
    habits = attrs.of(state, pid)["habits"]
    status = _status(state, pid, at)
    if status == "free":
        return Availability(True)
    if status == "out":
        return Availability(True, "quick")
    if status == "work":
        return {"chatty": Availability(True), "glances": Availability(True, "glance")}.get(
            habits["at_work"], Availability(False))
    if habits["late_night"] == "sometimes" and behaviour.status_at(population.profile(state, pid), at - 1.0) != "asleep":
        return Availability(True, "quick")                    # their first sleep hour
    return Availability(False)


def next_available(state: GameState, pid: str, at: float) -> float:
    """The first whole hour (or `at` itself) at which they are available: a schedule boundary, never a draw."""
    if available(state, pid, at).yes:
        return at
    hour = math.floor(at) + 1.0
    for _ in range(24 * 8):
        if available(state, pid, hour).yes:
            return hour
        hour += 1.0
    raise ValueError(f"social: population.yaml: {pid} is never available in a week")


def boundary_minutes(state: GameState, pid: str, at: float) -> Optional[float]:
    """Minutes until their status next changes in a way that ends talk; None when no boundary in 24 h.
    A night owl's late band and a chatty worker's shift start are not boundaries (§4.4)."""
    habits = attrs.of(state, pid)["habits"]
    now = _status(state, pid, at)
    hour = math.floor(at) + 1.0
    for _ in range(24):
        nxt = _status(state, pid, hour)
        if nxt != now and not (nxt == "work" and habits["at_work"] == "chatty"):
            return (hour - at) * 60.0
        hour += 1.0
    return None
```

**The instant-reply chain (§4.3), complete.** These functions are called inside `social_message`'s handler (a player's send) and inside the `read` event's handler, so every write is under rule 3:

```python
#: The due messages a live voicing pass has marked, visible to the running action (Plan decision 9).
MARKED: ContextVar[frozenset[str]] = ContextVar("social_marked", default=frozenset())


@contextlib.contextmanager
def marking(ids: Iterable[str]) -> Iterator[None]:
    token = MARKED.set(frozenset(ids))
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        MARKED.reset(token)


def episode(state: GameState, pid: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    row = ((state.social.get("people") or {}).get(pid) or {}).get("episode")
    return row if row and row.get("end") in ("none", "winding", "signed_off") else None


def _due_reply(state: GameState, thread_id: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """The thread's unvoiced reply-family due message, if one waits."""
    t = messaging.thread(state, thread_id) or {}
    for m in reversed(t.get("messages") or []):
        if m.get("voiced") is False and ((m.get("decision") or {}).get("situation") in _REPLY_SITUATIONS):
            return m
        if m.get("from") != "player":
            return None
    return None


def on_player_send(state: GameState, pid: str, thread_id: str, at: float) -> None:
    """
    A player's message (called from `social_message`'s handler). Either:
    - a reply is already due and UNMARKED: it is re-decided in place, so a burst gets one reply (§4.3);
    - one is due and MARKED: nothing now -- the pass's commit sees the player spoke since its
      snapshot and queues the next decision then (`after_commit`, I6);
    - an episode is open and they are not away: their read lands NOW (`at`), so the reply is due
      at the same minute -- the send's own `advance_time` walks it (§4.3);
    - otherwise: the read is queued at `next_available` (they read it when they can, §3.5 item 3).
    """
    _note_player_chars(state, pid, thread_id, at)
    due = _due_reply(state, thread_id)
    if due is not None:
        if due["id"] not in MARKED.get():
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "redecide", "pid": pid,
                                 "thread_id": thread_id, "message_id": due["id"], "at": at})
        return
    person = state.social["people"].get(pid) or {}
    if person.get("ghosted") or apps.gone(state, pid) or _read_pending(state, pid, thread_id):
        return
    ep = episode(state, pid)
    away_until = (ep or {}).get("away_until")
    if ep is not None and ep["end"] != "signed_off":
        events.push(state, at, pid, "read", thread_id=thread_id, episode=ep["id"])
    elif ep is not None and int(ep.get("after_left", 0)) > 0:
        events.push(state, at, pid, "read", thread_id=thread_id, episode=ep["id"], one_more=True)
    else:
        when = next_available(state, pid, max(at, float(away_until or at)))
        events.push(state, when, pid, "read", thread_id=thread_id)


@events.handles("read")
def _on_read(state: GameState, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pid, thread_id, at = str(event["pid"]), str(event["thread_id"]), float(event["at"])
    person = state.social["people"].setdefault(pid, {})
    if not messaging.is_open(messaging.thread(state, thread_id)) or apps.gone(state, pid) or person.get("ghosted"):
        return {"ok": False, "why": "closed"}
    relationships.settle_left_on_read(state, pid, at)
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_read", "thread_id": thread_id, "reader": pid, "at": at})
    if episode(state, pid) is None:
        start = _episode_start(state, pid, at)        # the per-episode draws (§4.6)
        if start != "reply":
            return {"ok": True, start: True}
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "open", "pid": pid, "thread_id": thread_id,
                             "at": at, "kind": "chat"})
    asked = relationships.pending_asks(state, pid)
    if asked:
        return messaging.answer_ask(state, pid, asked[0], thread_id, at)
    decision = decide(state, pid, thread_id, at)
    messaging.queue_due(state, pid, thread_id, decision, at)
    if event.get("one_more"):
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "one_more", "pid": pid})
    return {"ok": True, "mode": decision.mode, "end": decision.end}


def _ghost_factor(state: GameState, pid: str) -> float:
    """`ghost_per_episode × (1 - interest/100) × stage_factor` (§4.6); split out to be forced (lesson 1)."""
    person = state.social["people"][pid]
    arch = behaviour.archetype(population.profile(state, pid).archetype)
    factors = (_data().get("reply") or {}).get("stage_factor") or {}
    stage = str(person.get("stage") or "matched")
    return (float(arch.ghost_per_episode) * (1 - float(person.get("interest", 50)) / 100.0)
            * float(factors.get(stage, factors.get("default", 1.0))))


def _reply_factor(state: GameState, pid: str) -> float:
    """`reply_prob × interest_factor × depth_factor` (§4.6); split out to be forced (lesson 1)."""
    person = state.social["people"][pid]
    arch = behaviour.archetype(population.profile(state, pid).archetype)
    low, high = (_data().get("reply") or {}).get("interest_factor") or [0.3, 1.5]
    interest = float(person.get("interest", 50)) / 100.0
    depth_factor = ((_data().get("reply") or {}).get("depth_factor") or [1.0] * 5)[growth.depth(state, pid)]
    return float(arch.reply_prob) * (low + (high - low) * interest) * float(depth_factor)


def _ghost_protected(state: GameState, pid: str) -> bool:
    """STUB (spec §24's table): T4 replaces it with `bool(promises.open_by(state, pid, by="person"))`."""
    return False


def _episode_start(state: GameState, pid: str, at: float) -> str:
    """The draws per EPISODE START (§4.6): ghost (never while they hold a promise), else reply, else left on read."""
    rng = behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_CONVO, pid)      # inside the read event's handler: rule 3
    if not _ghost_protected(state, pid) and rng.random() < _ghost_factor(state, pid):
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_person", "pid": pid, "set": {"ghosted": True}})
        # Only the reply family (pre-flight F7). A player's `promise_close` (keyed by this pid), a
        # `plan_lapse`, a `life` or an `offer_number` event stays queued; open promises and plans are
        # voided through their OWN handlers (T4, T12), never by dropping their events here.
        events.cancel(state, pid, events.REPLY_FAMILY)
        return "ghosted"
    if rng.random() >= _reply_factor(state, pid):
        return "left_on_read"
    return "reply"
```

`decide` (§4.4-§4.5) and the commit hook (§4.3, I6):

```python
def _pressure(state: GameState, pid: str, ep: dict[str, Any], at: float) -> tuple[float, str]:
    talk = _data().get("talk") or {}
    terms = {"length": ep["exchanges"] / max(1, ep["target"]), "energy": 1.0 - ep["energy"] / 100.0}
    left = boundary_minutes(state, pid, at)
    window = float(talk.get("boundary_minutes", 20))
    if left is not None and left < window:
        terms["boundary"] = 1.0 + (window - left) / window          # steep: past sign_off_at at once
    reason = max(terms, key=terms.get)
    return terms[reason], reason


def _reason_words(state: GameState, pid: str, term: str, at: float) -> str:
    words = _data().get("reasons") or {}
    if term != "boundary":
        return str(words.get(term, ""))
    left = boundary_minutes(state, pid, at) or 0.0
    then = _status(state, pid, at + left / 60.0 + 0.01)
    now = _status(state, pid, at)
    if then == "work":
        if now == "free" and 11 <= at % 24 <= 15:
            return str(words.get("lunch_over", ""))
        return str(words.get("work", "")).format(hour=int((at + left / 60.0) % 24))
    return str(words.get({"asleep": "asleep", "out": "out"}.get(then, "early"), ""))


def _mode(state: GameState, pid: str, ep: dict[str, Any], cap: str, end: str) -> str:
    modes = _data().get("modes") or {}
    if cap in ("quick", "glance") or end == "sign_off" or ep["exchanges"] < int(modes.get("chatting_after", 3)):
        return "quick"
    chars = ep.get("player_chars") or [0]
    deep = (ep["energy"] > float(modes.get("deep_energy", 40))
            and growth.depth(state, pid) >= 2
            and sum(chars) / len(chars) > float(modes.get("deep_player_chars", 90))
            and _touched_at_depth(state, pid, ep))
    return "deep" if deep else "chatting"


def decide(state: GameState, pid: str, thread_id: str, at: float, *, situation: str = "reply") -> Decision:
    """The reply's shape, from the episode, their schedule and depth (inside a social handler)."""
    ep = episode(state, pid) or {}
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "exchange", "pid": pid, "at": at})
    ep = episode(state, pid) or ep
    cap = available(state, pid, at).cap
    pressure, term = _pressure(state, pid, ep, at)
    talk = _data().get("talk") or {}
    if cap == "glance" and ep["exchanges"] >= 1:
        end, term = "sign_off", "boundary"
    elif pressure >= float(talk.get("sign_off_at", 1.0)):
        end = "sign_off"
    elif pressure >= float(talk.get("winding_at", 0.8)):
        end = "winding"
    else:
        end = "none"
    mode = _mode(state, pid, ep, cap, end)
    caps = ((_data().get("modes") or {}).get("caps") or {}).get(mode) or {"bubbles": [1, 2]}
    burst = messaging.player_burst(state, thread_id)
    promise_id = ""
    reason = _reason_words(state, pid, term, at) if end != "none" else ""
    if end == "sign_off":
        promise_id = _maybe_promise(state, pid, thread_id, at, cap)       # T3: always "" (T4 makes promises)
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "end", "pid": pid, "at": at,
                             "end": "signed_off", "reason": reason, "promise_id": promise_id})
    elif end == "winding":
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "end", "pid": pid, "at": at,
                             "end": "winding", "reason": reason})
    growth.gain(state, pid, "exchange", mode=mode)
    return Decision(situation=situation, kind="text", mode=mode,
                    max_bubbles=int(caps["bubbles"][1]),
                    min_bubbles=max(int(caps["bubbles"][0]), 2 if burst >= 3 else 1),
                    end="sign_off" if end == "sign_off" else end, reason=reason, promise_id=promise_id)


def _maybe_promise(state: GameState, pid: str, thread_id: str, at: float, cap: str) -> str:
    """T3 ships no promises (T4 replaces this body; spec §24's table)."""
    return ""


def after_commit(state: GameState, pid: str, thread_id: str, through_id: str, at: float) -> bool:
    """
    The voicing pass committed a reply whose snapshot read the thread up to message `through_id`
    (CALLER HOLDS the turn lock, inside the commit's transaction). If the player spoke after it,
    the NEXT decision is made now, at the commit's minute, as a due message -- never discarded (I6).
    Returns True when one was queued (the pass then sets `rerun`).
    """
    t = messaging.thread(state, thread_id) or {}
    after = False
    for m in t.get("messages") or []:
        if after and m.get("from") == "player":
            if _due_reply(state, thread_id) is not None or episode(state, pid) is None:
                return False
            decision = decide(state, pid, thread_id, at)
            messaging.new_message(state, thread_id, pid, "text", at=at, voiced=False,
                                  decision=dataclasses.asdict(decision))
            return True
        if m.get("id") == through_id:
            after = True
    return False
```

The private helpers named above, each small and read-only unless it says otherwise:
- `_REPLY_SITUATIONS = frozenset({"reply", "answer_request", "promised_message", "followup", "news", "opens_up", "offer_number"})`: the situations a burst re-decides.
- `_read_pending(state, pid, thread_id) -> bool`: a `read` event for that thread is already queued (the burst rule for an unavailable person).
- `_note_player_chars(state, pid, thread_id, at)`: inside the handler, appends the player's last message's length to the open episode's `player_chars` (kept at `talk.player_chars_kept`).
- `_touched_at_depth(state, pid, ep) -> bool`: the episode's `topics` include one whose kind is in the person's current rung.
- `names_label(text, label) -> bool`: the whole-word, punctuation-safe match (Step 3, Review Focus 4).
- `_data()`: `social.data("conversation")`.

The `social_episode` handler (ops):
- `open` draws `target` with `person_rng(SOCIAL_CONVO)`, through `_target_draw(state, pid, rng) -> int`, which is forceable. It scales by `per_depth[depth]`, by `late_mult` when it is 21:00-04:00, and by `work_mult` when the status is work. It writes the §4.1 row with `energy: 100.0` and `warmth_at_open`, increments `counters.episode`, and pushes `episode_lapse` at `at + episode_gap_minutes/60`.
- `exchange` increments `exchanges`, sets `last_at`, and subtracts `energy_per_exchange`. A short player message (under `short_player_chars`) costs `short_extra_energy` more. A topic or hobby label named in the player's last message (whole words, Review Focus 4) gives back `topic_energy_back` and appends the topic id to `topics`. It re-pushes the `episode_lapse` at `last_at + gap`, after `events.cancel(state, pid, {"episode_lapse"})`.
- `end` writes the end state, the reason and the promise id. For `signed_off` it also writes `away_until = next_available(at + 0.25)` and `after_left`, drawn from `talk.after_sign_off`.
- `one_more` decrements `after_left`.
- `close` (from the lapse event, or `scene` close from T13):
  - sets `end` (`lapsed` or the given value);
  - appends the row to `people[pid]["recap_due"]`, which T6 consumes;
  - increments `episodes_done`;
  - applies `growth.gain("good_end")` when warmth rose across the episode (`warmth > warmth_at_open`) and it ended `signed_off`;
  - applies `growth` op `tick`;
  - sets `episode` to None.
- `redecide` replaces the due message's `decision` with `decide(...)`'s, under the same message id. A burst's next send gets one reply.

`episode_lapse` handler: when `episode` is open, of kind `chat`, and `last_at + gap <= at`, it closes the episode with `end: lapsed`. A scene episode never lapses (m9).

**Initiations by window (§4.7).** The daily hook, `convo._daily_initiations(state)`, appended to `events.daily_hooks`:
- For each live person, and for each `behaviours.yaml` `initiations:` row:
  - the window is resolved by `windows.resolve(state, pid, row["window"], day_start)`;
  - the probability is `row["prob"][depth]`, drawn with `person_rng(SOCIAL_CONVO)`;
  - the minute is drawn uniformly inside the window (the same rng);
  - an `initiate` event is queued with the row's `instruction` as situation (`window_message` by default).
- The `initiate` handler skips when any of these holds:
  - an episode with that person is open;
  - the player wrote to them within `initiation.skip_if_talked_minutes`;
  - the situation is a bare `good_morning`/`check_in`/`late_night` (retired, §13.1).
- Otherwise it opens an episode (`open`) and queues the due message.

`behaviours.yaml` rows become, for example, `{id: good_morning, window: waking, prob: [0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.8, 0.9], kind: text, instruction: window_message}`.

**`messaging.py` after T3.** These are kept, and some are renamed public because convo calls them:
- `player_burst` (was `_player_burst`);
- `is_open` (was `_open`);
- `answer_ask(state, pid, request, thread_id, at)` (was `_answer_ask`, with its due at `at`, decided by `convo.decide(..., situation="answer_request")` and with `kind`/`request_id` set by `dataclasses.replace`).

`_e_message` calls `convo.on_player_send(state, pid, tid, at)` in place of `schedule_reply`. `_cancel_waiting` stays. The double text stays (it is a person's initiation on silence) and is skipped while an episode is open.

**`turn.wait` after T3** (T4 adds `promise:<id>` and T11 moves sleep to the world):

```python
WAIT_UNTIL: tuple[str, ...] = ("next", "1h", "evening", "morning")


def wait(state: GameState, args: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Let in-game time pass (§3.6) through `clock.advance_time` only; the jump is the player's choice."""
    until = str(args.get("until") or "next")
    now = state.world_clock_hours
    clock_rows = social.data("conversation").get("clock") or {}
    if until == "1h":
        hours = 1.0
    elif until in ("evening", "morning"):
        target = float(clock_rows.get(until, 18 if until == "evening" else 7))
        hours = (target - now % 24) % 24 or 24.0
    else:
        due = _next_notifying_at(state)
        cap = float(social.setting("wait_next_cap_hours", 8))
        hours = cap if due is None else min(cap, max(0.0, due - now))
    clock.advance_time(state, hours)
    return {"ok": True, "waited_hours": hours}
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from tests.social_fixtures import (  # noqa: F401
    _act, _open_episode, _replies_forced, _set_clock, _set_person, matched, receipts_active, receipts_state,
)


def _theirs(state, thread):
    return [m for m in state.social["threads"][thread]["messages"] if m["from"] not in ("player", "system")]


def test_a_reply_in_an_open_episode_lands_at_the_sends_minute(receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    with _replies_forced():
        _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
        sent_at = receipts_state.world_clock_hours
        assert _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="how was work?")["success"]
    due = [m for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["voiced"] is False]
    assert len(due) == 1 and due[0]["at"] == pytest.approx(sent_at)
    assert receipts_state.world_clock_hours == pytest.approx(sent_at + 1 / 60)      # costs.text: 1 minute


def test_ten_exchanges_need_no_wait(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Acceptance 1, engine half: 10 exchanges, no `wait`, each reply at its send's minute."""
    from engine.social import convo
    from tests.social_fixtures import _voice_all
    from tests.social_stub import StubVoice

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_target_draw", lambda state, pid_, rng: 40)
    with _replies_forced():
        _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
        for i in range(10):
            assert _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text=f"message {i} about the climb")["success"]
            _voice_all(receipts_state, StubVoice("zero"))
    assert receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["episode"]["exchanges"] >= 10
    assert all(m["voiced"] is not False for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread))


def test_a_burst_before_the_mark_is_one_reply_after_the_mark_is_two(receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    """§4.3, I6: unmarked -> re-decided in place; marked -> the commit queues the next decision.
    Pre-flight F15: `after_commit` returns False while an unvoiced reply-family message waits
    (`_due_reply`), so the commit is SIMULATED by setting the marked message `voiced = True` first,
    exactly as the pass's commit would. Then one NEW unvoiced decision exists, not two."""
    from engine.social import convo

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    with _replies_forced():
        _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
        _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="hey")
        _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="also")
        due = [m for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["voiced"] is False]
        assert len(due) == 1                                   # unmarked burst: re-decided in place
        marked_id = due[0]["id"]
        with convo.marking([marked_id]):
            _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="and one more")
        assert [m["id"] for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["voiced"] is False] == [marked_id]
        through = [m for m in receipts_state.social["threads"][thread]["messages"]
                   if m["from"] == "player"][1]["id"]           # the snapshot read up to "also"
        next(m for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["id"] == marked_id)["voiced"] = True  # the commit
        assert convo.after_commit(receipts_state, pid, thread, through, receipts_state.world_clock_hours) is True
    fresh = [m for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["voiced"] is False]
    assert len(fresh) == 1 and fresh[0]["id"] != marked_id     # exactly one new decision, for "and one more"


def test_an_unavailable_persons_read_lands_at_a_boundary(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.social import attrs, convo

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    # Pre-flight F16: force the habits too, so `available`'s `late_night == "sometimes"` branch (which
    # reads the REAL `behaviour.status_at`) can never fire by seed.
    monkeypatch.setattr(attrs, "of", lambda state, pid_: {
        "habits": {"at_work": "never", "late_night": "no", "in_company": "glances"},
        "pace": 1.0, "topics": [], "home": ""})
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_status", lambda state, pid_, at: "asleep" if at % 24 < 7 else "free")
    _set_clock(receipts_state, hour=2)
    _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="you up?")
    read = next(e for e in receipts_state.social["queue"] if e["pid"] == pid and e["kind"] == "read")
    assert read["at"] % 24 == pytest.approx(7.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("habit,status,expected", [
    ({"late_night": "night_owl", "at_work": "never", "in_company": "glances"}, "night_owl_3am", True),
    ({"late_night": "no", "at_work": "chatty", "in_company": "glances"}, "work", True),
    ({"late_night": "no", "at_work": "never", "in_company": "glances"}, "work", False),
])
def test_night_owls_answer_late_and_never_workers_do_not(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch, habit, status, expected) -> None:
    from engine.social import attrs, behaviour, convo

    pid = matched("matchstick").split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(attrs, "of", lambda state, pid_: {"habits": habit, "pace": 1.0, "topics": [], "home": ""})
    if status == "night_owl_3am":
        monkeypatch.setattr(behaviour, "status_at", lambda p, at: "asleep" if 1 <= at % 24 < 8 else "free")
        at = 24 * 3 + 3.0
    else:
        monkeypatch.setattr(behaviour, "status_at", lambda p, at: "work")
        at = 24 * 3 + 11.0
    assert convo.available(receipts_state, pid, at).yes is expected


def test_a_sign_off_names_its_boundary(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.social import convo

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "boundary_minutes", lambda state, pid_, at: 5.0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_status", lambda state, pid_, at: "work" if at % 24 >= 7 else "free")
    with _replies_forced():
        _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
        _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="morning!")
    due = next(m for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["voiced"] is False)
    assert due["decision"]["end"] == "sign_off" and due["decision"]["reason"]


def test_modes_grow_with_the_episode(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.social import convo, growth

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_target_draw", lambda state, pid_, rng: 60)
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "boundary_minutes", lambda state, pid_, at: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(growth, "depth", lambda state, pid_: 2)
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_touched_at_depth", lambda state, pid_, ep: True)
    modes = []
    with _replies_forced():
        _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
        for _ in range(6):
            _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="x" * 120)
            due = next(m for m in _theirs(receipts_state, thread) if m["voiced"] is False)
            modes.append(due["decision"]["mode"])
            due["voiced"] = True                               # a test shortcut: no pass, only the shape matters
    assert modes[0] == "quick" and "deep" in modes[3:]


def test_no_ghost_inside_an_episode_and_ghosts_between(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.social import convo

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_ghost_factor", lambda state, pid_: 1.0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_reply_factor", lambda state, pid_: 1.0)
    _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)                # opened: no draw inside it
    _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="hi")
    assert not receipts_state.social["people"][pid].get("ghosted")
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "close", "pid": pid,
                                  "at": receipts_state.world_clock_hours, "end": "lapsed"})
    _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="hello again")
    from engine.game import clock
    clock.advance_time(receipts_state, 24)
    assert receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["ghosted"] is True


def test_an_episode_lapses_after_the_gap(receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    from engine.game import clock

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
    clock.advance_time(receipts_state, 1.0)                    # past episode_gap_minutes (45)
    person = receipts_state.social["people"][pid]
    assert person["episode"] is None and person["recap_due"][-1]["end"] == "lapsed"


def test_a_topic_label_with_punctuation_matches_whole_words(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """(Review Focus 4) a label carrying regex characters is matched literally, on whole words."""

    assert convo.names_label("I write C++ for a living", "C++")
    assert convo.names_label("climbing (bouldering) tonight?", "climbing (bouldering)")

def test_a_ghost_cancels_only_the_reply_family(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Pre-flight F7: a ghost drops `read`/`reply`/`initiate`/`double_text`/`episode_lapse` for the pid,
    and leaves every other kind (here a stand-in `promise_close`, pushed directly) queued."""
    from engine.game import clock
    from engine.social import convo, events

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_ghost_factor", lambda state, pid_: 1.0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "next_available", lambda state, pid_, at: at)    # the read lands now (F16)
    at = receipts_state.world_clock_hours
    events.push(receipts_state, at + 5, pid, "promise_close", promise_id="pr1")   # a social_queue write
    _act(receipts_state, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="hi")   # queues the read
    clock.advance_time(receipts_state, 1)                     # the read fires; the ghost draw is forced
    kinds_left = [e["kind"] for e in receipts_state.social["queue"] if e["pid"] == pid]
    assert receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["ghosted"] is True
    assert not set(kinds_left) & events.REPLY_FAMILY
    assert "promise_close" in kinds_left                      # (a match's own `expire` row may sit beside it)


def test_the_players_schedule_reads_their_creation_job(receipts_state) -> None:
    """Pre-flight F24: a nights player (creation's `attributes.job`, e.g. bartender) resolves to `nights`,
    not to the office default."""

    receipts_state.social.setdefault("player", {}).setdefault("attributes", {})["job"] = "bartender"
    try:
        assert windows.schedule_of(receipts_state, "player").job_shape == "nights"
    finally:
        receipts_state.social["player"]["attributes"].pop("job", None)

    assert away.away_hours(receipts_state, 1_000.0 + 3 * 3600) == 0.0
```

**`promises.yaml`:**

```yaml
later_min_hours: 2
max_ahead_hours: 48
grace_minutes: 60
kept_trust: 1
broken: {trust: -3, warmth: -2}
phrases:                         # with no model (§5.2): whole phrase, case-folded, not in a question
  you:                           # "message me ..." -> the PERSON promises to write
    morning: ["message me in the morning", "text me in the morning", "message me tomorrow morning"]
    at_work: ["message me at work", "text me at work"]
    lunch: ["message me at lunch", "text me at lunch"]
    after_work: ["message me after work", "text me after work"]
    tonight: ["message me tonight", "text me tonight", "message me later tonight"]
  me:                            # "I'll message you ..." -> the PLAYER promises
    morning: ["i'll message you in the morning", "i'll text you in the morning"]
    lunch: ["i'll message you at lunch"]
    after_work: ["i'll message you after work"]
    tonight: ["i'll message you tonight"]
    at_work: ["i'll message you at work"]
words:                           # how a window reads, by speaker (spec §5.1's truthful wording)
  morning: "in the morning"
  lunch: "at lunch"
  after_work: "after work"
  tonight: "tonight"
  weekend: "at the weekend"
  at_work: "at work"
  later: "later"
  fallback: "{window}, so {when}"           # a window that does not exist for them, worded truthfully
```

**The speaker rule and the promise windows (`windows.py`), complete additions:**

```python
def _promise_day(sched: Any, window: str, day: int, *, morning: float) -> Optional[tuple[float, float]]:
    bands = _bands(sched, day)
    awake = [b for b in bands if b[2] != "asleep"]
    work = [b for b in bands if b[2] == "work"]
    base = day * _DAY
    if window == "morning" and awake:
        start = max(awake[0][0], base + morning)
        return (start, start + 3.0) if start < base + 12.0 else None
    if window == "tonight":
        return (base + 18.0, base + 24.0) if any(b[1] > base + 18.0 for b in awake) else None
    if window == "weekend":
        return (base + 9.0, base + 22.0) if behaviour.WEEKDAYS[day % 7] in ("sat", "sun") else None
    if window == "at_work" and work:
        return work[0][0], work[-1][1]
    if window.startswith("at:"):
        hour = float(window.split(":", 1)[1])
        return base + hour, base + hour + 1.0
    return _on_day(sched, window, day)                    # lunch, after_work


def resolve(state: GameState, who: str, window: str, at: float) -> Optional[tuple[float, float]]:
    """The next occurrence of `window` for `who` not yet over at `at` (now covering both window sets)."""
    sched = schedule_of(state, who)
    morning = float((social.data("conversation").get("clock") or {}).get("morning", 7))
    if window == "later":
        lead = float(social.data("promises").get("later_min_hours", 2))
        return first_free(state, who, at + lead)
    first = int(at // _DAY)
    for day in range(first - 1, first + 8):
        span = (_promise_day(sched, window, day, morning=morning) if window in PROMISE_WINDOWS or window.startswith("at:")
                else _on_day(sched, window, day))
        if span is not None and span[1] > at:
            return max(span[0], at), span[1]
    return None


def first_free(state: GameState, who: str, at: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """The first free (awake, not working) band starting at or after `at`."""
    sched = schedule_of(state, who)
    first = int(at // _DAY)
    for day in range(first, first + 8):
        for start, end, status in _bands(sched, day):
            if status in ("free", "out") and end > at:
                return max(start, at), end
    raise ValueError(f"social: population.yaml: {who} has no free band in a week")


def owner_of(window: str, *, by: str, pid: str) -> str:
    """Whose schedule resolves a promise's window (m4): the SENDER's, but `at_work` is the RECEIVER's."""
    sender = pid if by == "person" else "player"
    receiver = "player" if by == "person" else pid
    return receiver if window == "at_work" else sender
```

**`promises.py` core, complete:**

```python
def _rows(state: GameState) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    return state.social.setdefault("promises", {})


def open_by(state: GameState, pid: str, by: Optional[str] = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return sorted((r for r in _rows(state).values()
                   if r["pid"] == pid and r["status"] == "open" and (by is None or r["by"] == by)),
                  key=lambda r: r["opens_at"])


def make(state: GameState, pid: str, *, by: str, window: str, about: str = "",
         at: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Record a promise (a chip, a sign-off decision, an accepted proposal or a phrase). None if it cannot resolve."""
    now = state.world_clock_hours if at is None else float(at)
    who = windows.owner_of(window, by=by, pid=pid)
    span = windows.resolve(state, who, window, now)
    fallback = False
    if span is None:                                     # no such window for them: their next free band (§5.1)
        span, fallback = windows.first_free(state, who, now), True
    limit = float(social.data("promises").get("max_ahead_hours", 48))
    if span[0] - now > limit:
        return None
    result = apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_promise", "op": "make", "pid": pid, "by": by, "window": window,
                                  "opens_at": span[0], "closes_at": span[1], "about": about[:100],
                                  "made_at": now, "fallback": fallback})
    return result.get("promise")


@effect_kind("social_promise")
@recorded
def _e_promise(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows = _rows(state)
    op = effect.get("op")
    if op == "make":
        counters = state.social.setdefault("counters", {})
        counters["promise"] = int(counters.get("promise", 0)) + 1
        row = {"id": f"pr{counters['promise']}", "pid": str(effect["pid"]), "by": str(effect["by"]),
               "window": str(effect["window"]), "opens_at": float(effect["opens_at"]),
               "closes_at": float(effect["closes_at"]), "about": str(effect.get("about") or ""),
               "made_at": float(effect["made_at"]), "status": "open", "fallback": bool(effect.get("fallback"))}
        rows[row["id"]] = row
        if row["by"] == "person":                        # kept: their message lands inside the window (§5.3)
            rng = behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_CONVO, row["pid"])
            due_at = row["opens_at"] + rng.random() * max(0.0, min(1.0, row["closes_at"] - row["opens_at"]))
            events.push(state, due_at, row["pid"], "promise_due", promise_id=row["id"])
        else:                                            # the player's: judged at the window's close + grace
            grace = float(social.data("promises").get("grace_minutes", 60)) / 60.0
            events.push(state, row["closes_at"] + grace, row["pid"], "promise_close", promise_id=row["id"])
        return {"type": "social_promise", "ok": True, "promise": dict(row), "save_reason": "promise"}
    row = rows.get(str(effect.get("promise_id") or ""))
    if row is None or row["status"] != "open":
        return {"type": "social_promise", "ok": False, "text": "no open promise"}
    row["status"] = {"keep": "kept", "break": "broken", "void": "void"}[str(op)]
    return {"type": "social_promise", "ok": True, "promise": dict(row)}


def on_player_message(state: GameState, pid: str, at: float) -> None:
    """A player's message in any of that person's threads inside a window keeps their promise (§5.3)."""
    for row in open_by(state, pid, by="player"):
        if row["opens_at"] <= at <= row["closes_at"]:
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_promise", "op": "keep", "promise_id": row["id"]})
            relationships.apply_regard_exact(state, pid, trust=int(social.data("promises").get("kept_trust", 1)))
            growth.gain(state, pid, "promise_kept")


@events.handles("promise_due")
def _on_promise_due(state: GameState, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    row = _rows(state).get(str(event["promise_id"]))
    pid = str(event["pid"])
    if row is None or row["status"] != "open":
        return {"ok": False}
    if apps.gone(state, pid) or relationships.ended(state, pid):
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_promise", "op": "void", "promise_id": row["id"]})
        return {"ok": True, "void": True}
    thread_id = messaging.thread_for(state, pid)
    at = float(event["at"])
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "open", "pid": pid, "thread_id": thread_id,
                         "at": at, "kind": "chat"}) if convo.episode(state, pid) is None else None
    decision = dataclasses.replace(convo.decide(state, pid, thread_id, at, situation="promised_message"),
                                   promise_id=row["id"])
    messaging.queue_due(state, pid, thread_id, decision, at)
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_promise", "op": "keep", "promise_id": row["id"]})
    growth.gain(state, pid, "promise_kept")
    return {"ok": True, "kept": row["id"]}


@events.handles("promise_close")
def _on_promise_close(state: GameState, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    row = _rows(state).get(str(event["promise_id"]))
    if row is None or row["status"] != "open":
        return {"ok": False}
    pid = row["pid"]
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_promise", "op": "break", "promise_id": row["id"]})
    cost = social.data("promises").get("broken") or {}
    relationships.apply_regard_exact(state, pid, trust=int(cost.get("trust", 0)), warmth=int(cost.get("warmth", 0)))
    growth.loss(state, pid, "broken_promise")
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_person", "pid": pid,
                         "set": {"broken_promise": row["id"]}})        # the next decision carries it once
    return {"ok": True, "broken": row["id"]}
```

`relationships.apply_regard_exact(state, pid, *, warmth=0, trust=0)` is added here. It applies an exact engine change through `social_regard` with `exact: True`, which skips the model clamps but keeps the 0-100 bounds. `relationships.ended(state, pid)` is True for `ended`, `faded` and `expired`. `convo.decide` reads `person["broken_promise"]` once: it sets `Decision.reason` to the window words ("you said you'd message at lunch") with situation `reply`, and then clears the field through `social_person`. A follow-up row is written by T6 when memory exists, so T4 records it only as this flag (§24: T6 adds the `open` row).

`convo._maybe_promise` (replacing the stub):

```python
def _promise_drawn(state: GameState, pid: str) -> bool:
    """The archetype's promise_prob, on SOCIAL_CONVO (lesson 1: forceable). A breadcrumber's is 0."""
    arch = behaviour.archetype(population.profile(state, pid).archetype)
    return behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_CONVO, pid).random() < float(arch.promise_prob)


def _maybe_promise(state: GameState, pid: str, thread_id: str, at: float, cap: str) -> str:
    if not _promise_drawn(state, pid):
        return ""
    window = "lunch" if cap == "glance" else _sign_off_window(state, pid, at)
    row = promises.make(state, pid, by="person", window=window, at=at)
    return row["id"] if row else ""
```

`_sign_off_window(state, pid, at)` picks the first of `("after_work", "tonight", "morning")` that resolves for them within `max_ahead_hours`, and `"later"` when none does. `convo._ghost_protected` becomes `bool(promises.open_by(state, pid, by="person"))`.

The `sign_off` phone verb:
- payload `{"who": enum(me, you), "window": enum(lunch, after_work, tonight, morning, at_work)}`;
- `takes_text` (the player's line, or a default: "Talk later — I'll message you {words}" or "Message me {words}?");
- skill `social_sign_off`, label "Talk later";
- legal for a thread whose person has an open episode.

It sends the line through `messaging.send`, then `promises.make(state, pid, by="player" if who == "me" else "person", window=...)`. An "at_work" from the player ("message me at work") resolves against the PLAYER's work band (`owner_of`), and the person's message lands there whatever their own habits (§5.1).

The `promise` proposal: `relationships.yaml` `proposals:` gains `promise: {window: [lunch, after_work, tonight, morning, at_work]}`. It is legal only while the episode is `winding` or `signed_off` (`legal_proposals` reads `convo.episode(...)["end"]`). It is accepted when `promises.make` returns a row. No model means no proposals, so `phrase_match(text)` runs from `on_player_message`'s caller in `_e_message` when the player writes. A match records a pending `promise` request, which the person's next decision accepts (as route 2): `promises.make(..., by="person"|"player")`.

### Task 5: Willingness, numbers and per-episode caps (§1.4, §6.4's caps, §6.5, §15, §19.3's static test) — M

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_social_willing.py`, `tests/test_social_no_days.py`
- Modify:
  - `engine/social/growth.py` (`willing`, `closer_soon`);
  - `engine/social/relationships.py`:
    - `ask_decision` reads `growth.willing`, and its `why` gains `not_yet`;
    - `regard.per_day_gain` becomes `regard.per_episode_gain`;
    - `asks.per_day` becomes `asks.per_episode`;
    - `regard.fallback.per_day` becomes `per_episode`;
    - the stage gates are simplified: `numbers.gate` is `{willing: number}` (its `days` key goes), and `exclusive.gate` is `{willing: exclusive}` (its `stage_days` key goes: pre-flight F5);
  - `engine/social/media.py` (`budget.*.per_day` becomes `per_episode`; `unsolicited.per_day_at_stage` becomes `unsolicited.per_episode_at_stage`: pre-flight F5);
  - `engine/social/convo.py` (the `offer_number` initiation on reaching depth 1; the `social_episode` `close` op also writes `people[pid]["last_episode"] = <the closed episode's id>`, which keys the between-episode cap: pre-flight F36);
  - `engine/social/events.py` (`KINDS` gains `offer_number`, so the "unknown kind" log line names it: pre-flight F45);
  - `engine/social/payload.py` consumer (the `hints` section: per person `{"closer_soon": bool, "available_words": str, "number": {"ok": bool, "why": str}}`; no numbers);
  - `games/read-receipts/data/social/growth.yaml` (`willing:` filled), `relationships.yaml`, `media.yaml`;
  - `tests/test_social_relationships.py`, `tests/test_social_media.py` (the per-day tests become per-episode);
  - `tests/test_social_slice_v2.py` (read paths: `hints`);
  - the story CHANGELOG, the root `CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]`, `docs/AUTHORING.md`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T2 growth, T3 convo episodes (`episodes_done`, the episode id that keys a cap), T4 promises.
- Produces:
  - `growth.willing(state, pid, commitment) -> tuple[bool, str]`, where `why` is one of `"" | "not_yet" | "stage" | "needs" | "household"`;
  - `growth.closer_soon(state, pid) -> bool`;
  - `relationships.ask_decision(...)["why"]` gains `"not_yet"`;
  - the `hints` payload section;
  - the per-episode caps, keyed by `people[pid]["episode"]["id"]`. A scene counts as an episode (§6.4). Between episodes, the cap is keyed by `f"after:{people[pid].get('last_episode', '')}"`, so it resets each time an episode closes (pre-flight F36; a single `"e0"` key would never reset). One helper, `convo.cap_key(state, pid) -> str`, returns whichever key applies, and every per-episode counter reads it.

`growth.yaml` `willing:` (§6.5's first values):

```yaml
willing:
  number:       {depth: 1, warmth: 25}
  meet:         {depth: 1, warmth: 30, trust: 22}
  come_over:    {stage_min: meeting, scenes: 1}
  go_to_theirs: {stage_min: meeting, scenes: 1}
  stay_over:    {stages: [dating, fling, exclusive, living_together, engaged, married]}
  exclusive:    {stages: [dating], warmth: 70, trust: 65, scenes: 4, needs: {both: long_term}}
  meet_family:  {stage_min: exclusive}
  move_in:      {stage_min: exclusive, days_since_exclusive: 14, scenes: 12, warmth: 78, trust: 72, no_household: true}
  propose:      {stages: [exclusive, living_together], warmth: 85, trust: 80}
  stay_or_go:   {warmth: 70, trust: 65}
```

`growth.willing`, complete:

```python
def willing(state: GameState, pid: str, commitment: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Whether `commitment` holds for `pid` now, and why not (spec §6.5). Read-only."""
    gate = ((_data().get("willing") or {}).get(commitment))
    if gate is None:
        raise ValueError(f"social: growth.yaml: willing.{commitment}: not declared")
    person = (state.social.get("people") or {}).get(pid) or {}
    stage = str(person.get("stage") or "matched")
    if "stages" in gate and stage not in gate["stages"]:
        return False, "stage"
    if "stage_min" in gate and stage_rank(stage) < stage_rank(gate["stage_min"]):
        return False, "stage"
    if "depth" in gate and depth(state, pid) < int(gate["depth"]):
        return False, "not_yet"
    for key, field in (("warmth", "warmth"), ("trust", "trust")):
        if key in gate and float(person.get(field, 0)) < float(gate[key]):
            return False, "not_yet"
    if int(person.get("episodes_done", 0)) < int(gate.get("episodes", 0)) or \
       int(person.get("scenes_done", 0)) < int(gate.get("scenes", 0)):
        return False, "not_yet"
    if "days_since_exclusive" in gate:
        since = person.get("exclusive_since")
        if since is None or state.world_clock_hours - float(since) < 24.0 * float(gate["days_since_exclusive"]):
            return False, "not_yet"
    if gate.get("no_household") and state.social.get("household"):
        return False, "household"
    if "needs" in gate and not relationships.needs_hold_spec(state, pid, gate["needs"]):
        return False, "needs"
    return True, ""


def closer_soon(state: GameState, pid: str) -> bool:
    """The client's "Getting closer" hint (§14.2): a boolean, never a number."""
    return record(state, pid)["openness"] >= float(_data().get("closer_soon_at", 70))
```

`convo._offer_drawn(state, pid) -> bool` is the `offer_number_prob` draw (`person_rng(SOCIAL_CONVO)`, forceable). `relationships.needs_hold_spec(state, pid, needs) -> bool` lifts the body of v0.22's `needs_hold` (`both: long_term` reads both intents) so that a `willing` row can use it. `exclusive_since` is written by `social_stage` on entering `exclusive`, which T5 adds to the handler.

**The number offer (§15.2).** In `growth._maybe_step`, when the new depth is 1, push an `offer_number` initiation:
- at the current minute if an episode is open;
- otherwise at the person's next available minute.

Its handler draws `offer_number_prob` (`person_rng(SOCIAL_CONVO)`, forceable as `convo._offer_drawn`). On yes it queues `Decision(situation="offer_number")`, and the player's answer chip swaps numbers through v0.22's number path. A scene ending `fine` or better swaps numbers in T13.

**The static no-days test (`tests/test_social_no_days.py`), complete:**

```python
"""
No gate reads days (spec §19.3, I9, R15, m7). Walks growth.yaml, relationships.yaml and media.yaml and fails on
any key naming days, hours or a per-day cap -- but for the named exemptions, each with its reason.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

from tests.social_fixtures import receipts_active  # noqa: F401

#: (file, dotted path with list indexes written `[*]`) -> its reason. Nothing else may count days
#: (pre-flight F5: every row below was found by walking the shipped data at a2ed481).
EXEMPT: dict[tuple[str, str], str] = {
    ("growth", "willing.move_in.days_since_exclusive"): "R15: move_in's 14 days since exclusive",
    ("relationships", "wedding_after_days"): "R15: the wedding follows the engagement by days",
    ("relationships", "fade_days"): "m7: an absence measure, days of no contact",
    ("relationships", "cold.days"): "m7: an absence measure, days of a cold spell",
    ("relationships", "cold.recover_days"): "m7: an absence measure, days of warming back",
    ("relationships", "bands[*].contact_days"): "m7: an absence measure, like fade_days (spec clarification, F5)",
    ("relationships", "interest.left_on_read.grace_hours"): "a decay or grace window, not a gate",
    ("relationships", "mood.decay_hours"): "a decay or grace window, not a gate",
    ("relationships", "meets.days_ahead"): "removed in T12 (T12 deletes this row)",
    ("relationships", "meets.cancel_cost[*].within_hours"): "removed in T12 (T12 deletes this row)",
}
DAYISH = re.compile(r"(^|_)(days?|per_day|hours?|stage_days)($|_)")


def _walk(node, path=""):
    """Yield (path, key) with every list index written `[*]`, so one EXEMPT row covers a list."""
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        for key, value in node.items():
            here = f"{path}.{key}" if path else str(key)
            yield here, str(key)
            yield from _walk(value, here)
    elif isinstance(node, list):
        for value in node:
            yield from _walk(value, f"{path}[*]")


def test_no_gate_reads_days(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine import social

    offenders = []
    for name in ("growth", "relationships", "media"):
        for path, key in _walk(social.data(name)):
            if DAYISH.search(key) and (name, path) not in EXEMPT:
                offenders.append(f"{name}.yaml: {path}")
    assert not offenders, offenders


def test_every_exemption_still_exists(receipts_active) -> None:
    """An exemption whose key was removed is stale and is deleted from EXEMPT (no silent drift)."""
    from engine import social

    present = {(name, path) for name in ("growth", "relationships", "media") for path, _ in _walk(social.data(name))}
    assert set(EXEMPT) <= present, sorted(set(EXEMPT) - present)
```

`wedding_after_days` is added to `relationships.yaml` here as data (14). It is read by T18. `life_events.yaml`'s `prob_per_week` and `cooldown_days` are out of this test's three files by spec (the world's calendar, R15).

**Every day-ish key in the three files at `a2ed481`, and what T5 does with it (pre-flight F5, verified by walking the data):**

| File | Path (`[*]` = any index) | T5 |
|---|---|---|
| relationships | `regard.per_day_gain`, `regard.fallback.per_day`, `asks.per_day` | converted to `per_episode` |
| relationships | `stages.numbers.gate.days` | removed (`numbers.gate: {willing: number}`) |
| relationships | `stages.exclusive.gate.stage_days` | removed (`exclusive.gate: {willing: exclusive}`) |
| media | `budget.photo/voice/video.per_day` | converted to `per_episode` |
| media | `unsolicited.per_day_at_stage` | converted to `unsolicited.per_episode_at_stage` |
| relationships | `fade_days`, `cold.days`, `cold.recover_days`, `bands[*].contact_days` | EXEMPT, m7 absence measures (`contact_days` recorded as a spec clarification) |
| relationships | `interest.left_on_read.grace_hours`, `mood.decay_hours` | EXEMPT: "a decay or grace window, not a gate" (the recommended option: exempt explicitly, keep `hours?` in DAYISH) |
| relationships | `meets.days_ahead`, `meets.cancel_cost[*].within_hours` | EXEMPT "removed in T12"; T12 deletes both rows from `EXEMPT` when it removes `meets:` |
| growth | `willing.move_in.days_since_exclusive` | EXEMPT, R15 |
| relationships | `wedding_after_days` (new here) | EXEMPT, R15 |

```python
def test_a_declined_number_ask_carries_why_not_yet(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:

        _act(receipts_state, "ask", target=thread, args={"what": "number"})
    clock.advance_time(receipts_state, 0.1)
    answer = next(m for m in receipts_state.social["threads"][thread]["messages"]
                  if (m.get("decision") or {}).get("situation") == "answer_request")
    request = receipts_state.social["requests"][answer["decision"]["request_id"]]
    assert request["status"] == "declined" and request["why"] == "not_yet"


def test_no_life_logistics_commitment_reads_depth(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine import social
    from engine.social import growth

    for name, gate in social.data("growth")["willing"].items():
        if name not in growth.DEPTH_COMMITMENTS:
            assert "depth" not in gate, name


def test_regard_is_capped_per_episode_not_per_day(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Pre-flight F16: the archetype is pinned to `steady` (warmth_mult 1.0, no warmth cap band), so the
    "+4" below is the proposal itself, never the seed's archetype."""
    from engine.game import clock
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.social import relationships
    from tests.social_fixtures import _force_archetype

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    _force_archetype(monkeypatch, receipts_state, pid, "steady")
    _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
    start = receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["warmth"]
    for _ in range(10):
        relationships.apply_regard(receipts_state, pid, {"warmth": 4, "trust": 2}, day=receipts_state.world_day)
    cap = relationships._block("regard")["per_episode_gain"]["warmth"]
    assert receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["warmth"] - start == cap
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "close", "pid": pid,
                                  "at": receipts_state.world_clock_hours, "end": "lapsed"})
    _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)                    # the same in-game day, a new episode
    relationships.apply_regard(receipts_state, pid, {"warmth": 4, "trust": 2}, day=receipts_state.world_day)
    assert receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["warmth"] - start == cap + 4


def test_the_between_episode_cap_resets_when_an_episode_closes(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Pre-flight F36: outside any episode the cap is keyed `after:<last closed episode id>`, so closing
    another episode gives a fresh between-episode allowance."""
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.social import convo
    from tests.social_fixtures import _force_archetype

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    _force_archetype(monkeypatch, receipts_state, pid, "steady")
    assert convo.cap_key(receipts_state, pid) == "after:"
    _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
    first = receipts_state.social["people"][pid]["episode"]["id"]
    assert convo.cap_key(receipts_state, pid) == first
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_episode", "op": "close", "pid": pid,
                                  "at": receipts_state.world_clock_hours, "end": "lapsed"})
    assert convo.cap_key(receipts_state, pid) == f"after:{first}"


def test_reaching_everyday_queues_their_number_offer(receipts_state, matched, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    from engine.social import convo

    thread = matched("matchstick")
    pid = thread.split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(convo, "_offer_drawn", lambda state, pid_: True)
    _set_person(receipts_state, pid, warmth=40, trust=30, episodes_done=1)
    _open_episode(receipts_state, pid, thread)
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_growth", "op": "gain", "pid": pid, "amount": 100})
    from engine.game import clock
    clock.advance_time(receipts_state, 0.05)
    assert any((m.get("decision") or {}).get("situation") == "offer_number"
               for m in receipts_state.social["threads"][thread]["messages"])


def test_hints_carry_booleans_and_words_never_numbers(receipts_state, matched) -> None:
    """Lesson 7: sentinels planted, then absent from the JSON payload."""
    import json

    from engine.social import payload

    pid = matched("matchstick").split(":")[1]
    _set_person(receipts_state, pid, warmth=61.987654, trust=48.123457)
    receipts_state.social["people"][pid].setdefault("growth", {})       # a probe of the payload's read only
    from engine.game.effects import apply_effect
    apply_effect(receipts_state, {"type": "social_growth", "op": "gain", "pid": pid, "amount": 73.123457})
    block = json.dumps(payload.phone_block(receipts_state), default=str)
    for sentinel in ("61.987654", "48.123457", "73.123457"):
        assert sentinel not in block
    hint = payload.phone_block(receipts_state)["hints"][pid]
    assert hint["closer_soon"] is True and isinstance(hint["number"]["ok"], bool)
```

**Files:**

  - `engine/social/promises.py`: a broken player promise writes a follow-up `open` row.
  - `engine/memory/summarizer.py`: nothing new; `summarize_with` is reused. Its `fallback` takes TWO arguments, `(existing, items)` (`summarizer.py:291`), so the recap passes a two-argument adapter (pre-flight F22).
  - `engine/games/caches.py`: `NULLED_ATTRIBUTES` gains `("engine.social.memory", "_RECAP_PROMPT_CACHE")`; `WARMERS` gains `("engine.games.caches", "_warm_recap_prompt")`, a warmer that, like `_warm_texter`, imports `engine.social.memory` only when `social.declared()` (pre-flight F37).
  - `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml`: `engine.social.memory._RECAP_PROMPT_CACHE: {verdict: warmed, loader: engine.games.caches._warm_recap_prompt, note: "the active story's prompts/episode_recap.md (v0.23 T6); warmed only for a story declaring paths.social"}` (F37).

  - `tests/test_social_memory.py` (thread-summary tests replaced), `tests/test_social_commit_scope.py`, `tests/test_social_voicer.py`, the story CHANGELOG.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T3 `recap_due`, `episode`; T4 promises; v0.22 `memory.summarize_with` (`engine/memory/summarizer.py`, Plan decision 6 of v0.22), `voicer.set_summary_fn` (one argument, F10).
- Produces:
  - `memory.person(state, pid) -> dict` (read-only: `current`, `bridge`, `episodes`, `story`, `open`, `canon`, `knows`, `told`, `history`);
  - `memory.fresh() -> dict` and `memory.cap_story(text) -> str` (used by T20's migration);
  - `memory.add_recap(state, pid, row)`, which appends a closed episode's recap row through `social_memory` `op: recap` (T12's stub uses it);
  - `memory.topic_rows(state, pid) -> list[dict]`, the person's drawn topic rows (`{id, kind, label}`), read-only;
  - `memory.note_spoken(state, pid, message)`, called from `social_message` for either side;
  - `memory.recap_view(state, pid) -> Optional[dict]`;
  - `memory.recap_fallback(view) -> str`;
  - `memory.commit_recap(state, view, text) -> dict`;
  - `memory.add_open(state, pid, row)`, `memory.close_open(state, pid, open_id)`;
  - `memory.followup_due(state, pid, at) -> Optional[dict]`.

  `RecapFn` is the v0.22 one-argument `SummaryFn`, renamed in the voicer as `set_summary_fn` (kept name).

**The per-person memory shape (§7.1):**

```python
def fresh() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"current": [], "bridge": [], "episodes": [], "story": "", "open": [],
            "canon": [], "knows": [], "told": [], "history": []}


def cap_story(text: str) -> str:
    """The story so far bounded to `memory.story_words`, keeping the NEWEST sentences (§7.2's fold fallback)."""
    return last_words(text, int(social.setting("memory.story_words", 300)))
```

`canon`, `knows`, `told` and `history` move from their v0.22 places under the person, with the same bounds. `social_memory` gains these ops, each bounded in its handler:
- `spoken`: append to `current`, capped at `memory.verbatim`;
- `recap`: append to `episodes`, clear `current`, set `bridge` to the last `bridge_bubbles`, and fold the oldest into `story` past `episodes_kept`;
- `fold`;
- `open_add`;
- `open_close`.

`note_spoken` runs from `_e_message` for the player's messages and from `social_voiced`'s handler for theirs. That means the SAME `current` list whatever app, which is the cross-app continuity (§15.5).

**Recap jobs (§7.2).**
- `voicer._recap_one(session, p, *, store)` replaces `_summarise_one`. It takes the oldest row of any person's `recap_due` and builds `memory.recap_view` holding no lock: the episode's messages, read off `current` plus the archive (`memory.with_archive`, kept).
- It calls the summary function through `memory.summarize_with(existing="", items=…, renderer=_render_episode, fallback=lambda existing, items: recap_fallback(view), system=recap_prompt(), new_label="THIS CONVERSATION", llm_fn=fn, max_words=recap_words)`. The `fallback` is that two-argument lambda, because `summarize_with` calls `fallback(existing, items)`; `recap_fallback` itself keeps its one argument, `view` (pre-flight F22).
- `memory.recap_prompt() -> str` reads `prompts/episode_recap.md` once per story into `memory._RECAP_PROMPT_CACHE`, the same way `voicing.texter_prompt()` fills `_TEXTER_CACHE`: the cache is read ONCE into a local and returned from the local (a reset nulls it with no lock: `tests/test_cache_reset_race.py`), and "" with one WARNING when the story has none, as `summary_prompt` does. It is warmed by `caches._warm_recap_prompt` and nulled on reset (`NULLED_ATTRIBUTES`), so its key effectively includes the active story (pre-flight F37).
- It commits under the tried lock with the `base` check: a `recap_due` row id that is no longer present means the recap is stale and is refused.
- The fold of the oldest episode into `story` runs through `summarize_with` the same way (its fallback keeps the newest sentences), as its own job.
- Each is one utility call. The turnstile per call arrives in T8 and wraps this call too.

`recap_fallback(view)`, complete. It is deterministic and built from engine facts (§7.2):

```python
def recap_fallback(view: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    names = {t["id"]: t["label"] for t in view.get("topic_rows") or []}
    topics = [names[t] for t in view.get("topics") or [] if t in names]
    parts: list[str] = []
    if topics:
        parts.append("You talked about " + _join(topics))
    reason = str(view.get("end_reason") or "")
    if reason:
        parts.append(_they(reason))                      # "you're winding down for the night" -> "they had to go to sleep"
    promise = view.get("promise_words")
    if promise:
        parts.append(promise)                            # "they said they'd message you in the morning"
    if view.get("band"):                                 # a scene (T13): the band's words
        parts.append(str(view["band_words"]))
    return "; ".join(parts) + "." if parts else "You talked for a while."
```

`_they(reason)` maps each `conversation.yaml` `reasons:` key to a third-person line, through a table `conversation.yaml` `reasons_recap:` with the same keys (data, so an author owns the words).

**Follow-ups (§7.3).**
- The reply schema gains `followups` (`maxItems` 2, each `{about ≤ 100, when: tomorrow|days:<n>|after:<window>}`) in T7. T6 lands the engine side.
- `memory.add_open(state, pid, {"kind": "followup", "about", "due_at", "by": "person"})` records one, bounded at `open_max`, with the oldest closed first.
- `followup_due(state, pid, at)` returns the oldest due row.
- `convo.decide` sets `Decision.followup_id` to it, and the voice gets "They had X; ask how it went" (T7's `RIGHT NOW`).
- A follow-up is closed when its decision is committed (`social_voiced`'s handler calls `close_open`).
- A broken player promise writes `{"kind": "followup", "about": "you said you'd message {words}", "due_at": now, "by": "person"}`.

  - agreement checks `promise_claimed` and `commitment_claimed`.

The block order (§13.2), pinned. v0.22's `BLOCK_TITLES` is rewritten in place (pre-flight F25); the user message keeps v0.22's form, each block `f"{title}\n{body}"`, blocks joined by a blank line (`voicing.py:702`):

```python
BLOCK_TITLES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "WHO YOU ARE NOW", "YOUR STORY TOGETHER", "WHAT YOU KNOW ABOUT THEM", "STILL OPEN",
    "YOU AND THEM", "THIS CONVERSATION", "RIGHT NOW", "FORMAT",
)   # the persona is the system message, block 1
```

The window and its degradation (§7.4, I7), complete:

```python
def window_for(usable_context: int, mode: str) -> tuple[int, bool]:
    """
    The voice prompt's token budget for one call: min(social.voice.window_tokens, the binding's usable
    context) minus the MODE's reply cap (MINOR 8). `degraded` is True on a binding under 8192 tokens:
    deep uses chatting's caps, episodes keep 3, the story is cut to 120 words first.
    """
    ceiling = int(social.setting("voice.window_tokens", 8192))
    degraded = usable_context < ceiling          # the configured window, not a literal (pre-flight T7 note)
    caps = mode_caps(mode, degraded=degraded)
    return max(1024, min(ceiling, int(usable_context)) - int(caps["max_tokens"])), degraded


def mode_caps(mode: str, *, degraded: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    caps = (social.data("conversation").get("modes") or {}).get("caps") or {}
    if degraded and mode == "deep":
        mode = "chatting"
    return dict(caps.get(mode) or caps.get("quick") or {"bubbles": [1, 2], "bubble_chars": 280, "max_tokens": 300})
```

The budget's trim order (§7.4) in `memory.budgeted(parts, window, *, degraded)`:
1. older `episodes`, down to 3;
2. `story` words (on a degraded binding, `story` is first cut to 120 words before any other trim);
3. `canon`;
4. `history`, down to `history_min`;
5. `current`, down to `verbatim_min` (16).

`now`, `who_now`, `open` and `bridge` are NEVER trimmed. When the window cannot hold the never-trimmed parts plus the floors, the function still returns them all, and the call's `max_tokens` is the mode cap: the binding's own context check is the backstop.

`texter.md` is rewritten as §13.1 says:
- the persona keeps first person and no stage directions;
- it drops "Most texts are short…";
- it gains §13.1's quoted paragraph verbatim;
- the `## Instructions` table holds:
  - `reply`;
  - `winding` and `sign_off` (each with `{reason}`);
  - `promised_message`, `followup`, `news`, `opens_up`;
  - `stood_up`, `flake`, `window_message`;
  - and the kept v0.22 rows: `opener`, `double_text`, `decline_*`, `send_*`, `post_meet`, `reconnect`, `game_banter`, `offer_number`, `ask_meet`, `answer_request`.
- `good_morning`, `check_in` and `late_night` are removed.
- `## Fallbacks` is keyed `situation.mode`, for example `reply.quick` or `sign_off.chatting`, with templated lines over engine facts:
  - `How did {followup} go?`
  - `Got to go — {reason}. {promise_words}`
  - `I was just thinking about {topic}`

  At least 3 lines per situation × mode.
- There is no line rating, limiting or tiering content (rule 12). v0.22's `situation_faults` check is extended to the new table.

**The schema's additions (§13.3).** `reply_schema(decision, proposals, moods, *, promise_windows=())` takes the bubble count and `maxLength` from `mode_caps(decision.mode)`, and gains three things:
- `followups`: `maxItems` 2, each `{about: string ≤ 100, when: enum}`, where the `when` enum is `tomorrow`, `days:1`-`days:7`, and `after:<window>` for each initiation window. The engine side is T6's.
- `promise`: present ONLY when `decision.end` is `winding` or `sign_off`, with `window` an enum of `promise_windows`. The voicer passes the windows that resolve for this person within `max_ahead_hours`, so an illegal window is unsamplable.
- the new proposal names, held by their commitment rows through `relationships.legal_proposals` (built per turn, unsamplable when not legal). Each is added to `legal_proposals` by the task that owns its mechanic, and the schema needs no change when they land:
  - `promise` (T4);
  - `invite_over` (T16, `willing(come_over)`);
  - `move_in` and `propose` (T18);
  - the `stay_or_go` answer (T17).

`commitment_claimed` (§13.4) is matched only and covers facts, not tone:
- The reply states a step from `relationships.yaml` `claims.commitment` (`"let's move in"`, `"will you marry me"`, `"we should be exclusive"`, `"come over tonight"`), each row tagged with its commitment id.
- It fails when `growth.willing(...)` for that id does not hold in the view (`AgreementView.willing: frozenset[str]`, built in `view_from_state`/`view_from_snapshot`) and no closer recorded it (`AgreementView.recorded: frozenset[str]`, empty until T13).

`promise_claimed` fails a reply that promises a window different from `decision.promise_id`'s row (§5.2), or promises one when the decision carries none and the archetype makes none.

`engine/social/turnstile.py`, complete:

```python
"""
The per-session narration turnstile (v0.23.0 spec §20.1; N1-N3, m10; Plan decision 3).

One player's world turn and their voice pass must never BOTH ask the
supervisor for a narration ticket: under one account the second would be
refused `other_window`. Only the turnstile's holder may acquire the ticket.

- The WORLD turn waits (`acquire_world`, bounded) BEFORE it tries the turn lock,
  so it never waits holding the lock. While it waits, `world_waiting > 0`.
- A VOICE pass never waits: `try_acquire_voice` succeeds only when nobody holds
  it and no world turn is waiting, and the pass holds it for ONE model call.
  So a world turn waits at most one call.
- The condition's lock (`_cond`) is held only to read or change `holder` and
  `world_waiting`, never across work: a LEAF. The HOLD is a logical lock taken
  before the turn lock and before every ordered lock (engine/locks.py,
  "THE NARRATION TURNSTILE").

Built per session by `SessionStore._build`, only for a story declaring
`paths.social`; a session built by hand gets `NULL` (always free).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
import time
from typing import Any, Optional

from engine.config import get_config, hosting_enabled

WORLD = "world"
VOICE = "voice"


class Turnstile:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._cond = threading.Condition(threading.Lock())
        self.holder: Optional[str] = None
        self.world_waiting = 0
        #: Seconds held, by side: the narration-share metric (T28).
        self.held_seconds: dict[str, float] = {WORLD: 0.0, VOICE: 0.0}
        self._since = 0.0

    def acquire_world(self, timeout: float) -> bool:
        """Wait at most `timeout` seconds for the turnstile; the caller must hold NO lock (checked by test)."""
        deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(timeout))
        with self._cond:
            self.world_waiting += 1
            try:
                while self.holder is not None:
                    left = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if left <= 0:
                        return False
                    self._cond.wait(left)
                self.holder = WORLD
                self._since = time.monotonic()
                return True
            finally:
                self.world_waiting -= 1

    def try_acquire_voice(self) -> bool:
        """Never waits: free and no world turn waiting, or False."""
        with self._cond:
            if self.holder is not None or self.world_waiting:
                return False
            self.holder = VOICE
            self._since = time.monotonic()
            return True

    def release(self, who: str) -> None:
        with self._cond:
            if self.holder != who:
                raise RuntimeError(f"turnstile released by {who!r} while held by {self.holder!r}")
            self.held_seconds[who] += time.monotonic() - self._since
            self.holder = None
            self._cond.notify_all()


class _NullTurnstile:
    """For a session with no turnstile (built by hand): always free, never waits.
    `held_seconds` is a class-level dict that is NEVER written (`release` is a no-op): it has its
    own module-state row (pre-flight F37)."""

    holder = None
    world_waiting = 0
    held_seconds = {WORLD: 0.0, VOICE: 0.0}

    def acquire_world(self, timeout: float) -> bool:
        return True

    def try_acquire_voice(self) -> bool:
        return True

    def release(self, who: str) -> None:
        return None


NULL = _NullTurnstile()


def of(session: Any) -> "Turnstile | _NullTurnstile":
    return getattr(session, "narration_turnstile", None) or NULL


def world_wait_seconds() -> float:
    """Hosted: `hosting.queue_wait_seconds`; local: the per-call timeout, `llm.timeout_seconds` (Plan decision 14)."""
    if hosting_enabled():
        return float(get_config().get("hosting.queue_wait_seconds", 600))
    return float(get_config().get("llm.timeout_seconds", 300))
```

`engine/session/store.py`:
- `GameSession` gains `narration_turnstile: Any = field(default=None, repr=False, compare=False)`, documented "never saved".
- `_build` sets it BEFORE publishing the session into `_sessions`, so no other thread can see the session without it:

