```python
        if social.declared():
            from engine.social.turnstile import Turnstile

            session.narration_turnstile = Turnstile()
```

**The per-call pass (`voicer.py`), complete.** It replaces `run_pass`'s body. The v0.22 docstring's steps 2-5 are rewritten to "per call".

```python
OUTCOMES: tuple[str, ...] = ("ok", "busy", "dropped", "discarded", "fallback", "cancelled", "deferred")


def run_pass(p: VoicePass, *, llm_fn: Optional[voicing.LLMFn], admit: bool, store: Any = None) -> str:
    """
    One pass, ONE MODEL CALL AT A TIME under the session's narration turnstile (§20.1): for each
    person's reply (and then one recap), try the turnstile; busy -> `deferred` (nothing marked for
    that call; not an attempt). Holding it: admission (hosted), the tried snapshot of ONE person,
    the call, the tried commit, then release. A waiting world turn takes it between two calls.
    """
    session = p.session
    if p.cancel.cancelled or not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
        return "cancelled"
    gate = turnstile.of(session)
    p.started = time.monotonic()
    p.deadline = p.started + _deadline_seconds()
    p.statuses = []
    limit = max(1, int(social.setting("voice.max_per_pass", 3)))
    try:
        for _ in range(limit):
            if p.cancel.cancelled or not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
                return "cancelled"
            if not gate.try_acquire_voice():
                return _deferred(p)
            try:
                step = _one_call(p, llm_fn=llm_fn, admit=admit, store=store)
            finally:
                gate.release(turnstile.VOICE)
            if step in ("cancelled", "busy"):
                return step
            if step == "ended":
                return "cancelled"
            if step == "empty":
                break
        if gate.try_acquire_voice():
            try:
                _recap_one(session, p, store=store)          # at most one utility call, under the turnstile too
            finally:
                gate.release(turnstile.VOICE)
        else:
            return _deferred(p)
        if _more_due(session):
            p.rerun = True
        outcome = _outcome(p.statuses)
        if p.statuses:
            _report_pass(session, outcome, _elapsed_ms(p))
        return outcome
    finally:
        _clear_marks(session, p)


def _deferred(p: VoicePass) -> str:
    if p.statuses:
        _report_pass(p.session, _outcome(p.statuses), _elapsed_ms(p))
    return "deferred"


def _one_call(p: VoicePass, *, llm_fn: Optional[voicing.LLMFn], admit: bool, store: Any) -> str:
    """One person's reply, holding the turnstile: `voiced`, `empty`, `busy`, `cancelled` or `ended`."""
    session = p.session
    admission = None
    try:
        if admit:
            from engine.llm.gate import InferenceBusy, admit_turn

            if _try_lock(session, p.cancel):
                try:
                    if not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
                        return "cancelled"
                    _drain_inbox(session)
                    due = pending(session)
                finally:
                    _release(session)
                if not due:
                    return "empty"
            elif p.cancel.cancelled:
                return "cancelled"
            try:
                admission = admit_turn(timeout=float(social.setting("voice.admit_wait_seconds", 5)), label="voice",
                                       account=str(session.owner or ""), deadline_seconds=_hosting_deadline(),
                                       cancel=p.cancel)
            except InferenceBusy:
                return "cancelled" if p.cancel.cancelled else "busy"
        if not _try_lock(session, p.cancel):
            return "cancelled" if p.cancel.cancelled else "busy"
        try:
            if not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
                return "cancelled"
            _drain_inbox(session)
            snaps = snapshot(session, p, limit=1)          # READ, then MARK, one person
        finally:
            _release(session)
        if not snaps:
            return "empty"
        snap = snaps[0]
        if time.monotonic() >= p.deadline:
            reply = _deadline_reply(snap)
        elif snap.recap:
            reply = _voice(snap, llm_fn)           # a system card: nobody is typing it (v0.22's branch, kept: F44)
        else:
            session.typing.add(snap.thread_id)
            try:
                reply = _voice(snap, llm_fn)
            finally:
                session.typing.discard(snap.thread_id)
        if p.cancel.cancelled:
            return "cancelled"
        result = commit(session, p, reply, store=store, last=not _more_due_unlocked(session, p))
        return "ended" if result == "ended" else "voiced"
    finally:
        if admission is not None:
            admission.release()
```

`_more_due(session)` reads `pending(session)` under a tried lock (False when the lock is busy: the rerun comes from the requester). `_more_due_unlocked(session, p)` is the snapshot's own count of due people beyond this one. `snapshot` records it on `p.more` when it marks.

**The scheduler's side (`VoicingScheduler._after`)** gains a `deferred` branch ahead of `busy`:

```python
            if outcome == "deferred":
                # Not an attempt (§20.1 N3): re-queued on the commit_retry_ms schedule; the world turn's own
                # request (after its turn) re-queues it sooner. No scheduler thread ever waits on a turnstile.
                p.deferrals = getattr(p, "deferrals", 0) + 1
                delay_ms = _retry_ms()[min(p.deferrals, len(_retry_ms())) - 1]
                self._queued[p.key] = p
                heapq.heappush(self._timed, (self._time() + delay_ms / 1000.0, next(self._seq), p))
                self._wake.set()
                return
```

`VoicePass` gains `deferrals: int = 0` and `more: int = 0`. `live` in `_after` includes `"deferred"`.

**`run_until_quiet` stops on a deferral (pre-flight F17).** Its stop set becomes `("cancelled", "busy", "deferred")` (v0.22 stops only on the first two, `voicer.py:882`), so a pass deferred by a held turnstile returns at once instead of spinning all its passes.

**The open-episode fallback (§20.1's last bullet).** When `_after` would DROP a pass (`attempts >= max_attempts`) or a pass hits its deadline, `voicer.commit_open_fallbacks(session)` runs:
- on the dropping thread, holding nothing;
- it tries the turn lock on the retry schedule;
- for every due reply-family message whose person has an open chat episode, it commits `voicing.fallback_reply_for(...)` through `_apply_reply`;
- a busy lock parks them in the inbox.

So no open episode is left silent (§13.1's templated fallback).

**`engine/locks.py`'s new docstring section** (inserted after the "TWO MORE…" section, before "THE SUPERVISOR'S OWN PROCESS"):

```text
THE NARRATION TURNSTILE (v0.23.0, READ RECEIPTS only): a per-session hold
taken BEFORE the turn lock and before every lock in the list above
(``engine/social/turnstile.py``, spec §20.1). A world turn waits for it
(``acquire_world``, bounded by ``turnstile.world_wait_seconds``) holding NO
lock, then tries the turn lock non-blocking; a voice pass only ever TRIES it
(``try_acquire_voice``), once per model call, holding nothing, and takes the
turn lock (tried) and its lane under it. Nothing takes the turnstile while
holding a turn lock or any listed lock. Its condition's lock
(``Turnstile._cond``) is a leaf (above). It is per session, built in the
worker by ``SessionStore._build`` after any fork, so ``renew_after_fork`` has
no module lock to register for it. ``tests/lock_order.py`` checks it as
``TURNSTILE`` against ``TURN``.
```

`- engine.social.turnstile.Turnstile._cond` is appended to the docstring's LEAF LOCKS list. "Twenty-one locks" becomes "Twenty-two locks".

**`tests/lock_order.py` additions:**

```python
TURN = "engine.session.store.GameSession.lock"
TURNSTILE = "engine.social.turnstile.Turnstile.hold"
LEAVES = (*LEAVES_V022, "engine.social.turnstile.Turnstile._cond")   # written out in full in the file
INSTANCE_LOCKS += (("engine.social.turnstile", "engine.social.turnstile.Turnstile._cond"),)
```

`Checker.before` gains, at the top of its loop. Both pseudo-names are handled BEFORE any `RANK[...]` lookup, because neither is in `RANK`: v0.22's last rule (`RANK[held] > RANK[name]`, `tests/lock_order.py:172`) would raise `KeyError` when `TURN` is taken under a listed lock (pre-flight F38):

```python
        for held in self._stack():
            if held == name:
                continue                      # v0.22's first rule (an RLock re-entered), unchanged
            if name == TURNSTILE:
                if held == TURN:
                    self._violation(f"took the turnstile while holding a turn lock")
                elif blocking:
                    self._violation(f"waited for the turnstile while holding {held}")
                continue
            if name == TURN:
                if held != TURNSTILE and held not in LEAVES and held != LANE:
                    self._violation(f"took a turn lock while holding {held}")
                continue                      # TURN has no RANK: never reaches the RANK rule (F38)
            if held in (TURN, TURNSTILE):
                continue                      # every lock may be taken under either hold
            ...                               # the existing rules (leaves, LANE, RANK), unchanged
```

`install_turnstile(monkeypatch, checker)` wraps `Turnstile.acquire_world` (`before(TURNSTILE, blocking=True)`, then push on success), `Turnstile.try_acquire_voice` (`before(TURNSTILE, blocking=False)`, then push on success) and `Turnstile.release` (pop). `track_turn_lock(session, checker)` replaces `session.lock` with `TrackedLock(session.lock, TURN, checker)`.

**The real-time cost model**, written into the harness as data:

```python
REAL_SECONDS: dict[str, float] = {"send": 30.0, "read_bubble": 3.0, "swipe": 4.0, "world": 35.0, "moment": 40.0}
SESSION_REAL_SECONDS = 40 * 60


class RealClock:
    """Real seconds spent by the policy's actions in one session (no wall clock is read: rule R-03)."""

    def __init__(self, model_seconds: float) -> None:
        self.model_seconds = float(model_seconds)
        self.spent = 0.0

    def charge(self, action: str, *, bubbles: int = 0, replies: int = 0) -> None:
        self.spent += REAL_SECONDS.get(action, 0.0) + REAL_SECONDS["read_bubble"] * bubbles
        self.spent += self.model_seconds * replies

    @property
    def over(self) -> bool:
        return self.spent >= SESSION_REAL_SECONDS
```

**A phone-only session (T9):**
1. The policy acts until `RealClock.over`.
2. It then "goes to bed" with `wait {until: morning}` (phone only; T29 swaps in the world's sleep).
3. Each send is charged `send`, plus the model's seconds for each reply it got, plus `read_bubble` per bubble read.
4. A swipe is charged `swipe`.
5. A `wait` costs no real seconds.

The harness reports `ratio.chat` as in-game minutes over real minutes during open episodes. It is DERIVED and never banded (§3.4).

**The rows T9 measures** (10 seeds, at 6, 15 and 30 s; §17.1's seed table):
- `exchanges_per_episode.d0` and `.d2plus` (d2plus is `None` while no run reaches depth 2 in phone-only sessions; it is deferred to T29 by data);
- `end_reason.*`, `cold_unanswered_1h.d0`, `ghosted_per_episode.*`;
- `chat_minutes_to_numbers.{fast,mid,slow}` (pace terciles over `attrs.of(...)["pace"]`);
- `numbers_within_session`;
- `person_promises_kept` (asserted 1.0, not banded: §17.2 "100%, asserted");
- `player_promises_kept.{attentive,flaky}`;
- `full_replies_in_long_episodes`.

The extra rows `also.chat_minutes_to_depth1.{fast,mid,slow}` are reported and not banded. Every other row is `None` in T9 and is in `DEFERRED_ROWS` with the text "measured from T29 (world, scenes, deep growth): spec §24 stub table". T29 moves them to `BANDS`.

**The latency probe (§20.2):**
1. From the stubs, record the prompt and reply token counts per mode: the prompt is measured with `memory._estimate`, and the reply is the mode's `max_tokens` at p95 and half that at p50.
2. Take the tokens-per-second figure from `tests/test_texter_latency_probe.py`, run live only if `CLOCKWORK_LIVE_LLM` names the configured server. Otherwise it is ASSUMED at 20 tok/s, prompt-eval weighted, for a 4B model on the RTX 2060, and stated as assumed.
3. Compute the p50 and p95 per mode.
4. Apply §20.2's pre-committed levers in `config/default.yaml` or `conversation.yaml`, each with its measured line in a comment:

| Measured p95 | Lever |
|---|---|
| Over 15 s in `chatting` | `conversation.yaml` `modes.chatting_after: 5` |
| Over 20 s | `social.voice.window_tokens: 6144` |
| Over 30 s | `deep` uses `chatting`'s caps (`mode_caps` reads a new `conversation.yaml` `modes.deep_as_chatting: true`), and MODEL_SERVERS.md recommends a smaller `texter` binding |

5. Calibrate `REAL_SECONDS` for phone actions against v0.22 T30's cadence (p95 32 actions a minute) and record the result in the harness header.

**The minutes edge (§8.1, I3), complete:**

```python
# engine/game/locations.py, inside _clean_edge, BEFORE the _EDGE_DEFAULTS loop:
    if "minutes" in edge:
        # A minutes edge (v0.23.0 I3): `hours` defaults to 0, not _EDGE_DEFAULTS' 1; checked here only,
        # so an edge with no `minutes` (every other story's) is untouched (§19.5 pin 1).
        try:
            minutes = int(edge["minutes"])
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ValueError(f"locations: {src}>{dst}: minutes must be a whole number")
        edge.setdefault("hours", 0)
        if not 0 <= minutes <= 59:
            raise ValueError(f"locations: {src}>{dst}: minutes {minutes} is outside 0-59")
        if int(edge["hours"]) == 0 and minutes == 0:
            raise ValueError(f"locations: {src}>{dst}: hours and minutes are both 0")
        edge["minutes"] = minutes
```

`_clean_edge` raises here, where it logs and clamps elsewhere. That is deliberate: a minutes edge is new, so no shipped story can carry a malformed one, and §8.1 says "refuses". Read `_clean_edge`'s callers first and make the `ValueError` surface through the loader's existing load-fault path (`validate_content.py` reports it).

**The handoff, complete (`engine/social/world.py`):**

```python
PHONE_UP_REFUSAL = "Put the phone down first."


def world_turn_refusal(state: GameState) -> str:
    """Why a world turn may not run now ("" when it may). Asked by the choice route and again under the lock."""
    return PHONE_UP_REFUSAL if (state.social or {}).get("phone_up") else ""


def pick_up(state: GameState) -> None:
    """Any phone action picks the phone up (§8.2, MINOR 6): free, except during a scene (its `in_company`)."""
    if not state.social.get("phone_up"):
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "phone_up", "value": True})
        if social.scene_open(state):
            from engine.social import scene   # lazily: scene imports world
            scene.phone_in_company(state)


def put_down(state: GameState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The phone verb `put_down` (§8.2): free; the world comes forward."""
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "phone_up", "value": False})
    return {"ok": True, "phone_up": False}
```

`social_settings`' handler accepts `phone_up`, `at_work`, `work_until` and `wake_for_messages` as fields (T11 widens its allowed set).

`run_mechanics`' first line after draining the inbox is `world.pick_up(state)`. The `put_down` verb's result then sets it false within the same action.

**The world turn's side of the turnstile (§20.1 steps 1-6; the ONLY change to `run_guarded`), complete:**

```python
def run_guarded(session, action, intent, *, authored=None, emit_callback=None, present=None, cancel=None,
                runner=None, admit=True, metric="turn"):
    """(the docstring gains one paragraph: a phone story's WORLD turn takes the narration turnstile
    first -- spec §20.1 -- and every other caller's path is unchanged.)"""
    run = runner or run_turn
    gate = getattr(session, "narration_turnstile", None) if runner is None else None
    if gate is not None:
        return _run_world_turn(session, gate, action, intent, authored=authored, emit_callback=emit_callback,
                               present=present, cancel=cancel, admit=admit, metric=metric)
    if not session.lock.acquire(blocking=False):
        _report(session, metric, 0.0, 0.0, "busy")
        return None, "A turn is already in progress.", True
    try:
        return _run_locked(session, run, action, intent, authored=authored, emit_callback=emit_callback,
                           present=present, cancel=cancel, admit=admit, metric=metric)
    finally:
        # Hosted (v0.20.0 T15 fix round 1): release_turn also ends a session an admin ended mid-turn.
        release_turn(session)


def _run_locked(session, run, action, intent, *, authored, emit_callback, present, cancel, admit, metric):
    """The body run_guarded always ran under the lock (lifted VERBATIM: admission, the run, the metric,
    the last-line-of-defence except). The caller holds the turn lock and releases it."""
    began = time.monotonic()
    admit_ms = 0.0
    try:
        ...                                    # lines 333-389 of v0.22.0's run_guarded, unchanged
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- last line of defence
        ...                                    # lines 390-411, unchanged


def _run_world_turn(session, gate, action, intent, *, authored, emit_callback, present, cancel, admit, metric):
    """
    A phone story's world turn (spec §20.1, T11): the turnstile FIRST, holding no lock (the only
    blocking wait); then the turn lock, tried; the phone_up re-check; the inbox drained before the
    turn's buzz and narration block are built; the turn; the inbox drained again; the lock, then the
    turnstile, released; then a voicing pass requested for whatever fell due or was deferred.
    """
    from engine.social import turnstile as social_turnstile   # only reached for a paths.social session
    from engine.social import voicer
    from engine.social import world as social_world

    if not gate.acquire_world(social_turnstile.world_wait_seconds()):
        _report(session, metric, 0.0, 0.0, "busy")
        logger.info("[default_scene] World turn not run: the turnstile stayed busy "
                    "(operation=run_guarded, id=%s)", getattr(session, "session_id", "") or "")
        return None, STORYTELLER_BUSY, True
    try:
        if not session.lock.acquire(blocking=False):
            _report(session, metric, 0.0, 0.0, "busy")
            return None, "A turn is already in progress.", True
        try:
            refusal = social_world.world_turn_refusal(session.engine.state)
            if refusal:
                return None, refusal, False
            voicer.drain_inbox(session)
            result = _run_locked(session, run_turn, action, intent, authored=authored, emit_callback=emit_callback,
                                 present=present, cancel=cancel, admit=admit, metric=metric)
            voicer.drain_inbox(session)
            return result
        finally:
            release_turn(session)
    finally:
        gate.release(social_turnstile.WORLD)
        voicer.request(session)
```

**The interruptible advance (§8.4, I11; Plan decisions 12-13), complete:**

```python
def _wakes(state: GameState, row: dict[str, Any], *, sleeping: bool) -> bool:
    """Whether notification `row` stops the activity: any, awake; asleep, only a person's promise or a
    late_night person's message, and only with "wake me for messages" on (§8.4)."""
    if not sleeping:
        return True
    if not (state.social.get("settings") or {}).get("wake_for_messages"):
        return False
    pid = str(row.get("pid") or "")
    if row.get("promise_id"):
        return True
    from engine.social import attrs
    return bool(pid) and attrs.of(state, pid)["habits"]["late_night"] != "no"


def advance_interruptible(state: GameState, until_at: float, *, sleeping: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Advance toward `until_at`, event by event, stopping at the first new notification that may wake
    the player (Plan decision 13). Every step goes through `clock.advance_time` (rule 2).
    Returns {"stopped": bool, "at": float, "who": [names]}.
    """
    notes = state.social.setdefault("notifications", [])
    seen = int(state.social.get("counters", {}).get("notification", 0))     # v0.22's counter (F25)
    while state.world_clock_hours < until_at - 1e-9:
        now = state.world_clock_hours
        nxt = _next_event_at(state)
        step_to = until_at if nxt is None or nxt >= until_at else max(nxt, now + 1.0 / 60.0)
        clock.advance_time(state, min(step_to, until_at) - now)
        count = int(state.social.get("counters", {}).get("notification", 0))
        if count > seen:
            fresh = notes[-min(len(notes), count - seen):]
            waking = [r for r in fresh if _wakes(state, r, sleeping=sleeping)]
            if waking:
                return {"stopped": True, "at": state.world_clock_hours,
                        "who": sorted({_name(state, str(r.get("pid") or "")) for r in waking})}
            seen = count
    return {"stopped": False, "at": state.world_clock_hours, "who": []}


def _next_event_at(state: GameState) -> Optional[float]:
    queue = state.social.get("queue") or []
    return float(queue[0]["at"]) if queue else None


def _until(state: GameState, row: dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    now = state.world_clock_hours
    clock_rows = social.data("conversation").get("clock") or {}
    if row.get("until") == "morning":
        start, _ = windows.resolve(state, "player", "waking", now + float(clock_rows.get("min_sleep_hours", 4))) \
            or (None, None)
        if start is None:
            morning = float(clock_rows.get("morning", 7))
            start = now + ((morning - now % 24) % 24 or 24.0)
        return float(start)
    if row.get("until") == "evening":
        evening = float(clock_rows.get("evening", 18))
        return now + ((evening - now % 24) % 24 or 24.0)
    if row.get("until") == "work_end":
        return float(state.social.get("work_until") or _player_work_end(state, now))
    return now + float(row.get("minutes", 0)) / 60.0


def do(state: GameState, activity_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    row = _activity(activity_id)
    if row is None:
        return {"ok": False, "error": f"no activity {activity_id!r}"}
    if activity_id == "go_to_work":
        until = _player_work_end(state, state.world_clock_hours)
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "at_work", "value": True})
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "work_until", "value": until})
    if activity_id == "leave_early":
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "at_work", "value": False})
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_go", "op": "place", "to": "apt_hall"})
        return {"ok": True, "activity": activity_id, "narrate": row.get("narrate", "")}
    target = _until(state, row)
    if row.get("interruptible"):
        stopped = advance_interruptible(state, target, sleeping=activity_id == "sleep")
    else:
        clock.advance_time(state, max(0.0, target - state.world_clock_hours))
        stopped = {"stopped": False, "at": state.world_clock_hours, "who": []}
    if state.social.get("at_work") and state.world_clock_hours >= float(state.social.get("work_until") or 0) - 1e-9:
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "at_work", "value": False})
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_go", "op": "place", "to": "apt_hall"})
    result = {"ok": True, "activity": activity_id, "narrate": row.get("narrate", ""), "stopped": stopped["stopped"]}
    if stopped["stopped"]:
        result["buzz"] = "Your phone buzzes: " + ", ".join(stopped["who"])
        result["resume_until"] = target                  # "Carry on" resumes to the original end
    return result
```

`_player_work_end(state, now)` is the end of the player's current or next work band (`windows.resolve(state, "player", "at_work", now)[1]`). When the player has no work band, `go_to_work` is not offered.

**The world verbs (`world.world_verbs`), registered from `intents.legal_intents` under `paths.social`.** In `legal_intents`, AFTER the four existing open-thing branches (job, challenge, card, encounter), add:

```python
    if _social_world(state):
        from engine.social import world as social_world

        if state.social.get("at_work"):
            return social_world.work_verbs(state)            # carry_on_working, leave_early (m11); phone stays usable
        verbs.extend(social_world.world_verbs(state))
```

T13 inserts its scene branch above the `at_work` line. `_social_world(state)` is `bool(getattr(state, "social", None)) and social.declared()`, so no other story calls it (`state.social` is `{}` for them).

`world.world_verbs(state)` returns:
- `do`, whose targets are the activities whose `at` includes the player's location and whose `when` holds. Labels come from `label`, and the 8-option cap holds.
- `go`, whose targets are `go_targets(state)`. They exist only outside a scene. The rules (§8.5, N5):
  - From the flat, the building or the street: the open plans' destinations, then venues ranked by shared hobbies and last visit, then a home with an open plan, then `registry_office` on its event.
  - From every place outside the flat and building: "Head home" (`apt_lounge`) FIRST and ALWAYS, then the above. (Spec §8.5 lists open plans before Home; Home first keeps "always offered" true under the 8-option cap: pre-flight F33, Plan decision 25.)
  - At most 8.

**Rule 6.** `sleep` is offered in the bedroom with no gate, and "Head home" has no gate.

**`go` and the `social_go` handler (§8.5; Plan decision 16):**

```python
def go(state: GameState, to: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if to not in dict(go_targets(state)):
        return {"ok": False, "error": f"you can't get to {to} from here right now"}
    minutes = places.travel_minutes(to if to != "apt_lounge" else state.location_id)
    clock.advance_time(state, minutes / 60.0)                          # the travel, an action's cost (rule 2)
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_go", "op": "place", "to": to})
    result: dict[str, Any] = {"ok": True, "to": to, "minutes": minutes}
    result.update(_arrive(state, to))
    return result


def _arrive(state: GameState, to: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """STUB (this plan's stub table): every arrival is a quiet visit until T12 replaces this body with
    `from engine.social import plans; return plans.arrive(state, to)`."""
    return {"quiet": True}
```

`plans` does not exist until T12, so `_arrive` is a named stub, recorded in this task's commit and in the Notes. T12 replaces its body.

The `social_go` handler:
- `op: place` writes `state.location_id = to` and nothing else (the `arrest` precedent, `effects.py:1510`);
- `op: travel` is not used by the engine; the cost is charged by the caller, above.

**The buzz (§8.2):** `world.buzz(state) -> {"count": int, "who": [names]}`. It counts the notifications since the phone was last put down, read off `counters["notification"]` (v0.22's counter, F25) against `state.social["buzz_from"]` (written by `put_down`). Names only, never words.

**`_wakes` sees promises (pre-flight F35).** `messaging.notify(..., promise_id="")` gains the keyword and writes `row["promise_id"]` only when it is non-empty (no other row's shape moves); `messaging.queue_due` passes `decision.promise_id` when `decision.situation == "promised_message"`. Without it `_wakes`' `row.get("promise_id")` is always empty and a promise never wakes a sleeper.

**`SocialWorld` (§8.3).** Its priority is 40, not 30: `StorytellerMind` is registered at 30, and seam pin 4 pins the chain's exact order, which two equal priorities would leave to registration order (pre-flight F43):

```python
@interceptor(PHASE_DIRECTIVE, priority=40)
class SocialWorld:
    """PRE: a phone story's world, in engine words (spec §8.3): where, who is here, the buzz count and who,
    open plans, the household. Never a message's content. A no-op without `paths.social`."""

    def run_pre(self, state: GameState, system_prompt: str, *, player_action: str = "", **_: Any) -> str:
        from engine import social

        if not social.declared() or not getattr(state, "social", None):
            return system_prompt
        from engine.social import world

        block = world.narration_block(state)
        return f"{system_prompt}\n\n{block}" if block else system_prompt
```

`narration_block` lines:
- "You are in {place}."
- From T15, the companion block.
- "Your phone has buzzed {n} times: {names}." (only when n > 0)
- "Open plans: {pid name} — {kind words}." (from T12)
- "{partner} lives here with you." (from T18)

T11 ships the first and third. Each later task adds its own line.

**`storyteller.md` (§14.4), rewritten.** It narrates the player's life: the flat, the city, in-person scenes, the companion's spoken lines from the companion block, and the household. It keeps three rules: never write a phone message; everything on the phone arrives as a fact; the invented-names rule. It adds two: render the moment's band and never decide it; voice commitments only as the receipt states them. **No register line, and no line about how anything physical may be told** (rule 12). It has no `agents.yaml` (§8.3).

**The opening.** `game.yaml` `entry.opening` keeps its narration. Its choices become three world choices with intents:
- `{text: "Pick up your phone", intent: none}` (the client's phone layer opens; no engine action);
- `{text: "Tidy up a bit", intent: {action: do, target: tidy_up}}`;
- `{text: "Look around the flat", intent: none}`.

The intent-carrying choice is `tidy_up`, not "Make a cup of tea" → `make_breakfast`: `make_breakfast` is `at: [apt_kitchen]` and the entry is `apt_lounge`, so it would be refused the moment it was picked. `tidy_up` is legal at the entry, and it is the choice the route-level tests post by `choice_id` (pre-flight F3; Plan decision 27).

Read `default_state.opening` to see how authored opening choices carry intents, and match that shape. The route resolves a choice's intent ONLY from the option the player was shown (`default_state.resolve_player_intent` → `_chosen(session, choice_id)`); typed `custom_text` carries no intent by design, and the route is never widened to accept a client `intent` (rule 1; F3).

**`tests/social_world_stub.py::StubNarrator`** is an `llm_fn` for the storyteller. It returns a schema-valid turn JSON whose narration names the receipt's activity, with choices taken from the first three legal intents, so a world-turn test runs with no model (lesson 2). Write it from `engine/llm/schemas.py::storyteller_turn_schema` and the existing test stubs (`Grep "def _stub_llm\|llm_fn=" tests/test_clockwork_scene.py`).

```python
def _choose(scene, session, text: str):
    """Pick the OFFERED option whose text is `text`, by its `choice_id` (pre-flight F3): the route resolves the
    intent from the option shown (`session.last_turn`), never from the client, and typed text carries none."""
    choice = next(c for c in session.last_turn["choices"] if c.get("text") == text)
    assert choice.get("intent"), choice                    # the opening's world choice carries its intent
    return scene.app.test_client().post("/api/game/choice", json={"session_id": session.session_id,
                                                                  "choice_id": choice["id"]})

    pid = _matched_in(session, "matchstick").split(":")[1]

    for start, lands in ((2.0, 7.0), (21.0, 31.0), (6.5, 31.0)):
        day0 = int(state.world_clock_hours // 24) + 1

    row = promises.make(state, pid, by="person", window="morning")

def test_every_location_reaches_home(receipts_active) -> None:
    """§8.5 (N5): from each of the 28 locations, with no scene open, a legal sequence reaches apt_lounge."""
    from engine.game.intents import legal_intents
    from engine.game.locations import LOCATIONS
    from engine.agents.tool_dispatcher import execute_intent
    from engine.game.engine import GameEngine

    scene, session = _live_session()
    state = session.engine.state
    state.social["phone_up"] = False
    for start in sorted(LOCATIONS):
        state.location_id = start
        state.social["at_work"] = False
        for _ in range(6):
            if state.location_id == "apt_lounge":
                break
            verbs = {v.action: v for v in legal_intents(state)}
            if "go" in verbs and "apt_lounge" in verbs["go"].targets:
                execute_intent({"action": "go", "target": "apt_lounge"}, GameEngine(state))
            elif "travel" in verbs:
                target = "apt_lounge" if "apt_lounge" in verbs["travel"].targets else (
                    "apt_hall" if "apt_hall" in verbs["travel"].targets else verbs["travel"].targets[0])
                execute_intent({"action": "travel", "target": target}, GameEngine(state))
        assert state.location_id == "apt_lounge", start

    """(Review Focus 2, server half) the phone answers busy; the thread is unchanged."""

    assert session.lock.acquire(blocking=False)              # a world turn is running
    try:
        before = json.dumps(session.engine.state.social["threads"][thread], default=str)
        resp = scene.app.test_client().post("/api/phone/action", json={
            "session_id": session.session_id, "action": "send", "target": thread,
            "args": {"kind": "text"}, "text": "still there?"})
        assert resp.status_code == 409 and resp.get_json().get("busy") is True
        assert json.dumps(session.engine.state.social["threads"][thread], default=str) == before
    finally:
        session.lock.release()


def test_phone_send_during_world_wait_is_not_refused(receipts_active) -> None:
    """§20.1: the world turn waits on the turnstile; a send meanwhile is answered; the world turn then refuses.
    `run_guarded` is called directly with the engine-offered intent (F3's second option)."""
    from engine.scenes.default_scene import run_guarded
    from engine.social.turnstile import VOICE

    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    thread = _matched_in(session, "matchstick")
    _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    gate = session.narration_turnstile
    assert gate.try_acquire_voice()                          # a voice call in flight
    out: list = []
    t = threading.Thread(target=lambda: out.append(run_guarded(session, "Tidy up", {"action": "do",
                                                                                     "target": "tidy_up"})))
    t.start()
    _wait_until(lambda: gate.world_waiting == 1)
    _phone(scene, session, "send", target=thread, args={"kind": "text"}, text="hey")   # answered, not refused
    gate.release(VOICE)
    t.join(5.0)
    payload, error, busy = out[0]
    assert payload is None and error == "Put the phone down first." and not busy
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T5 `growth.willing(..., "meet")`; T3 `convo.available`, `convo.next_available`, `convo.boundary_minutes`, the `social_episode` `close` op; T11 `world.go_targets`, `world._arrive`.

**`plans.yaml`:**

```yaml
kinds: [coffee, drinks, dinner, walk, visit, film, climbing, games, gig, their_place, your_place]
patience_hours: {now: 1.5, tonight: 4}
now_min_hours: 2
late_grace_hours: 0.5
soon_patience_episodes: 6
stood_up: {warmth: -8, trust: -6, interest: -6}
flake: {stage_prob: {numbers: 0.10, meeting: 0.06, default: 0.03}}
invite: {prob: [0, 0.05, 0.12, 0.2, 0.25], window: evening}
```

The core of `arrive`, complete:

```python
def arrive(state: GameState, location: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The player arrived at `location` (from `world.go`). A `now`/`tonight` plan there within patience opens
    its scene; past patience it is stood up; with no plan it is a quiet visit (§8.5, §9.3)."""
    now = state.world_clock_hours
    for row in open_for(state):
        if _place_of(state, row) != location or row["when"] == "soon":
            continue
        if now - row["made_at"] > float(row["patience_hours"]):
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_plan", "op": "stood_up", "plan_id": row["id"], "at": now})
            return {"stood_up": row["id"], "quiet": True}
        return _on_arrival_scene(state, row, location)
    return {"quiet": True}


def _on_arrival_scene(state: GameState, row: dict[str, Any], location: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """STUB (spec §24's table): T13 replaces this body with `scene.open_scene(state, row["pid"], location,
    plan_id=row["id"])`. Until then arrival records `met` with a stub recap and moves numbers -> meeting."""
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_plan", "op": "met", "plan_id": row["id"], "at": state.world_clock_hours})
    name = population.profile(state, row["pid"]).name
    memory.add_recap(state, row["pid"], {"id": f"stub-{row['id']}", "kind": "scene",
                                         "recap": f"You met {name} at {places.label(location)}.",
                                         "topics": [], "end_reason": "met"})
    if state.social["people"][row["pid"]].get("stage") == "numbers":
        relationships.set_stage(state, row["pid"], "meeting")
    return {"met": row["id"], "quiet": False}
```

`_place_of(row)` is the venue, the person's home location (`places.home_of` → its template id, which is also its location id) for `their_place`, and `apt_lounge` for `your_place`.

The rest:
- `make` is answered inside the episode by `growth.willing(state, pid, "meet")` and by availability:
  - `now` needs `available(...).yes` and `now_min_hours` free (`boundary_minutes` ≥ that);
  - `tonight` needs `windows.resolve(state, pid, "evening", now)`;
  - `soon` needs willingness only.
- A flake is drawn at make (`_flake_drawn`) for `now`/`tonight`. On yes, a `plan_flake` notification is queued with `Decision(situation="flake", plan_id=…)` at the next minute, and the plan is `cancelled`.
- A "running late!" decision extends patience once by `late_grace_hours`, through `social_plan` `late`.
- `soon` lapses after `soon_patience_episodes` of the person's closed episodes, counted from `make` via `episodes_done`. This is checked on each episode `close`, which T12 hooks.
- **Plan decision 2:** plan ids are `"pl<n>"`.

**`scene_owns_turn`, complete (§10.1, N4):**

```python
def _social_scene_open(state: GameState) -> bool:
    """A phone story's in-person scene (spec §10.1): asked only of a state that carries a social slice,
    through the package's config-only check, so no other story imports a social submodule."""
    if not getattr(state, "social", None):
        return False
    from engine import social

    return social.scene_open(state)


def scene_owns_turn(state: GameState) -> bool:
    return bool(
        _challenge_open(state) or _card_open(state) or _scene_open(state) or _job_open(state)
        or _social_scene_open(state)
    )
```

In `legal_intents`, inside the `_social_world(state)` block added by T11 and BEFORE its `at_work` branch:

```python
        if _social_scene_open(state):
            return social_world.scene_verbs(state)       # `scene`, `close`, and `do` for scenes.yaml's shared activities
```

`world.scene_verbs` delegates to `scene.scene_verbs`. `go` appears only through `close`'s `go_somewhere_else:<venue>` and `call_it_a_night` (§10.1).

```yaml
  spread: 0.18

moves:
  ask_about:  {label: "Ask about {topic}", minutes: 20, fit: {traits: {curious: 0.06}}, bands: {lands: {warmth: 3, trust: 2}, fine: {warmth: 1, trust: 1}, flat: {}, misstep: {warmth: -2}}}
  tell_about: {label: "Tell them about {topic}", minutes: 20, fit: {traits: {warm: 0.05}}, bands: {lands: {warmth: 2, trust: 3}, fine: {trust: 1}, flat: {}, misstep: {trust: -1}}}
  playful:    {label: "Be playful", minutes: 15, fit: {archetypes: {banterer: 0.1, romantic: 0.05}}, bands: {lands: {warmth: 4}, fine: {warmth: 1}, flat: {}, misstep: {warmth: -3}}}
  quiet:      {label: "Let a silence sit", minutes: 15, fit: {traits: {laid_back: 0.06}}, bands: {lands: {trust: 3}, fine: {trust: 1}, flat: {}, misstep: {warmth: -1}}}
  phone:      {label: "Check your phone", minutes: 5, fit: {}, bands: {lands: {}, fine: {}, flat: {}, misstep: {warmth: -3}}}
closers: [another_round, go_somewhere_else, walk_them_home, back_to_yours, back_to_theirs, stay_the_night, call_it_a_night]
close:
  energy_out: 0                            # their energy at 0 -> they close
  misstep_run: 2                           # two missteps in a row -> they close
end_bands: {great: 0.72, good: 0.56, fine: 0.42, awkward: 0.3}    # average moment score -> scene band
too_deep_band: flat
shared_activities: {apt_kitchen: [make_dinner], apt_dining: [eat], apt_lounge: [watch_something]}
stay:                                      # §10.5, used from T16
  energy_min: 35
  early_start_hours: 9
  accept: {default: 0.6, romantic: 0.75, slow_burner: 0.35}   # by archetype only: no `intent` term (F34)
wedding: {moments: 3, moves: [quiet, tell_about, playful]}   # used from T18
```

Each move row's keys are exactly `label`, `minutes`, `fit` and `bands` (§19.6). No move has a floor or a refusal. Every `fit.traits` key is a real `population.yaml` trait id (`warm`, `laid_back`, `curious`: v0.22 has no `open` or `calm` trait), and `scene.check_data` refuses one that is not at load (pre-flight F23). `stay.accept` is keyed by archetype only: the controller's ruling on pre-flight F34 drops the `intent` term entirely, so staying over rests on the archetype and the evening (Plan decision 26).

**A moment (§10.3), complete:**

```python
def _moment_draw(state: GameState, pid: str) -> float:
    """The moment's one SOCIAL_SCENE draw, a centred spread (lesson 1: forceable)."""
    spread = float((_data().get("moment") or {}).get("spread", 0.2))
    return behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_SCENE, pid).gauss(0.0, spread)


def _band_of(score: float) -> str:
    cut = (_data().get("moment") or {}).get("bands") or {}
    for name in ("lands", "fine", "flat"):
        if score >= float(cut.get(name, 1.0)):
            return name
    return "misstep"


def move(state: GameState, target: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    scn = state.social.get("scene") or {}
    pid = str(scn.get("pid") or "")
    name, _, topic = target.partition(":")
    row = (_data().get("moves") or {}).get(name)
    if not pid or row is None:
        return {"ok": False, "error": "there is nobody here to talk to"}
    too_deep = name == "ask_about" and _topic_rung(topic) > growth.depth(state, pid)
    score = (float(scn.get("prior", 0.5)) + _fit(state, pid, row) + _topic_fit(state, pid, topic)
             + _mood_term(state, pid) + _moment_draw_in_handler(state, pid))
    band = (_data().get("too_deep_band") or "flat") if too_deep else _band_of(score)
    result = apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_scene", "op": "moment", "move": name, "topic": topic,
                                  "band": band, "score": score, "minutes": int(row.get("minutes", 15))})
    clock.advance_time(state, int(row.get("minutes", 15)) / 60.0)           # the moment's minutes (rule 2)
    if too_deep:
        growth.loss(state, pid, "too_deep")
    else:
        growth.gain(state, pid, "moment", band=band)
    return {"ok": True, "move": name, "topic": topic, "band": band, "words": _band_words(name, band, topic),
            **({"closed_by_them": result["closed"]} if result.get("closed") else {})}
```

`_moment_draw_in_handler` is the draw made inside the `social_scene` handler. Implement it by moving the score computation into the handler (the effect carries `move`/`topic`, and the handler computes the score and band). The shape above shows the arithmetic; the WRITE (the draw's counter) happens inside `social_scene` `moment` (rule 3). Write it that way: `move` passes `{"op": "moment", "move", "topic"}`, and the handler returns `band` and `score`.

The handler applies the band's `bands.<band>` effects as an exact regard (`relationships.apply_regard_exact`), appends the moment, decrements energy, and closes on `energy_out`/`misstep_run` or a schedule boundary (`convo.boundary_minutes` under `talk.boundary_minutes`), returning `closed`.

**The end (§10.4):** `end_scene(state, reason)`:
- averages the moment scores into an `end_bands` band;
- increments `scenes_done`;
- adds `meets_good` when the band is `great`/`good` (v0.22's branch carries over: `relationships.branch_for`);
- closes the scene episode (`social_episode` `close`, `end: scene_end`), which queues the recap;
- swaps numbers when the band is `fine` or better and none are swapped (§15.3, through v0.22's number path);
- queues `post_meet` at their next available window;
- sets `state.social["scene"] = {}`.

**Closers (§10.4; Plan decision 5):** `close(state, target)`.
- `call_it_a_night[:promise:<w>|:plan:soon|:stay]` ends the scene. `then: home` is the default and charges `social_go` to `apt_lounge` with its travel; `stay` stays put. A promise argument calls `promises.make(..., by="player")`. A plan argument calls `plans.make(..., when="soon")`.
- `back_to_yours` and `back_to_theirs` are offered only when `willing(come_over)` / `willing(go_to_theirs)` holds. Each makes a `now` plan of kind `your_place`/`their_place`, ends this scene and travels. Arrival opens the home scene (T16 fills the home side).
- `another_round` resets a third of the energy and costs 30 minutes.
- `go_somewhere_else:<venue>` ends the scene, makes a `now` plan at the venue and travels.
- `walk_them_home` costs their home's travel minutes plus 10, then calls it a night with `then: home`.
- `stay_the_night` is T16's (offered from T16).

The agreement view's `recorded` is the set of commitments the closer recorded this turn (T7's `commitment_claimed` reads it).

**The evaluator (§10.3, §22):**

A module-level pattern beside `_CLAIMS_ARRIVAL`, and one branch at the END of `contradicts`'s receipt loop (after the `job_stage` branch):

```python
_CLAIMS_LANDED = re.compile(
    r"(?i)(?:^|[.!?]\s+|\band\s+)(?:it|that|the\s+(?:joke|question|story))\s+(?:lands|landed|goes\s+down\s+well|went\s+down\s+well)\b"
)

        # inside `for receipt in receipts:`, last:
        if skill == "social_scene" and str(result.get("band") or "") == "misstep":
            if _CLAIMS_LANDED.search(narration):
                return "narrated a moment landing over a misstep"
```

This is read only from `social_scene` receipts, so no other story's evaluation moves (§19.5).

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("slug", SIX)
def test_the_director_is_unchanged_for_every_other_story(slug: str) -> None:
    """§19.5 pin 8: ensure_scene deals exactly as before, and engine.social's submodules are never imported."""
    import sys

    from engine.content import director
    from engine.games import registry
    from engine.game import procgen

    registry.activate(slug)
    try:
        before = set(sys.modules)
        state = procgen.new_game_state(seed=5)
        first = director.ensure_scene(state)
        added = {n for n in set(sys.modules) - before if n.startswith("engine.social.")}
        assert not added
        state2 = procgen.new_game_state(seed=5)
        assert director.ensure_scene(state2) == first
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
```

  - `engine/agents/storyteller.py` or the turn's evaluator path: the social fact checks after a scene turn, a retry with the correction line, and the receipt-line fallback on a second failure. Find the evaluator retry seam first (`Grep "retry" engine/agents/storyteller.py engine/agents/turn*.py`) and hang the social checks there, gated on `social.scene_open(state)` before the turn;
  - `engine/hosting/metrics_schema.py`: the counter `social_scene_prose_fallback`, and its emit;

- Create: `tests/test_social_companion.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T2 `who_now`; T6 `memory.person`; T7 `memory.budgeted`; T13 the scene and moment receipts; v0.22 `agreement` checks (`profile_contradicted`, `another_person`, `stage_claimed`); T4/T7 `promise_claimed`; T12 `plan_claimed`; T7 `commitment_claimed`.
- Produces:
  - `scene.companion_block(state, *, tokens: int) -> str`;
  - `agreement.check_scene_prose(narration: str, view: AgreementView, moment: dict) -> list[Failure]`;
  - `scene.receipt_lines(state) -> str` (the engine's words for the scene's last moment, the no-model rendering of §21);
  - `agreement.scene_view(state, pid) -> AgreementView`: `view_from_state` plus `recorded`, the commitments the scene's closers recorded this turn;
  - `agreement._sentences(text) -> list[str]`: the prose split on sentence ends (the evaluator's `_SENTENCE_END` pattern), so each sentence is checked as one bubble;
  - the metric `social_scene_prose_fallback`.

The companion block is built from the texter's own builders, budgeted to `companion_tokens`:
- `who_now(state, pid)`;
- "Where you are: {place}";
- the person's memory parts (`story`, the last 3 recaps, `open`, `bridge`) through `memory.budgeted`;
- "This moment: {move} — {band words} — {topic label}".

It is plain titled lines and carries no register line (rule 12).

**The fact checks on scene prose, complete:**

```python
def check_scene_prose(narration: str, view: AgreementView, moment: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Failure]:
    """Every texter fact check (§10.6), run over the narrator's prose as if it were one reply: the
    companion's words and the narration alike. Commitments are judged against what a closer RECORDED
    this turn (`view.recorded`), so a narrated promise, plan or step no closer made fails (I10)."""
    reply = {"bubbles": [{"text": sentence} for sentence in _sentences(narration)]}
    decision = Decision(situation="scene", end="sign_off" if moment.get("closer") else "none",
                        promise_id=str(moment.get("promise_id") or ""), plan_id=str(moment.get("plan_id") or ""))
    names = ("profile_contradicted", "another_person", "stage_claimed", "promise_claimed",
             "plan_claimed", "commitment_claimed")
    return [f for f in check(reply, decision, view) if f.check in names]
```

The turn's handling:
1. On the **first failure**, the storyteller turn is retried once, with the failures' correction lines (`voicing._correction`) appended as one system line, through the evaluator's retry path.
2. On a **second failure**, the narration is REPLACED by `scene.receipt_lines(state)`, the moment's engine words (the same path as §21's no-model scene). The failure is logged at INFO (`operation=scene_prose`, check names only, never the prose) and counted (`social_scene_prose_fallback`).

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from tests.social_fixtures import receipts_active  # noqa: F401
```

`StubNarrator` gains `narration=` and `calls` in this task. How the session's storyteller takes its `llm_fn` is read from `engine/session/store.py::_build` (`StorytellerAgent(engine, llm_fn=llm_fn, …)`). Use the attribute that agent exposes. These tests swap one stub for another on `session.storyteller.llm_fn`: the session was BUILT with a `StubNarrator` by `in_a_scene` (`_live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())`, pre-flight F2), so no window exists in which the production client is set.

### Task 16: Homes and visits (§10.5) — M

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `engine/social/scene.py`: home scenes; `stay_the_night`; the morning;
  - `engine/social/plans.py`: `their_place` and `your_place` plans resolve to the home's location;
  - `engine/social/world.py`: `go` to a home only with a plan or invitation;
  - `games/read-receipts/data/social/scenes.yaml`: the `stay` block, already present;
  - `tests/test_social_scene.py`, the story CHANGELOG.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T10 `places.home_of`, `home_label`, `homes.yaml` `stay_reasons`; T13 scenes and closers; T5 `willing` (`come_over`, `go_to_theirs`, `stay_over`); T3 `convo.boundary_minutes`.
- Produces:
  - `scene.stay_offered(state) -> bool`;
  - `scene._stay_drawn(state, pid) -> bool` (forceable);
  - `scene.stay_the_night(state) -> dict`;
  - the history row "Stayed over".
  - **T16's stub:** the stay-over ends with the clock at the morning and the player at the host's home, with NO morning scene (§24's table). `test_morning_scene_after_stay_over` ships SKIPPED with the reason "T24, if not cut at C1".

**`stay_the_night`, complete (§10.5, rule 12: logistics only):**

```python
def stay_offered(state: GameState) -> bool:
    """Offered at a home when the stage holds `stay_over` AND the evening allows it (§10.5)."""
    scn = state.social.get("scene") or {}
    pid = str(scn.get("pid") or "")
    if not pid or not places.is_home(scn.get("location", "")) and scn.get("location") != "apt_lounge":
        return False
    if not growth.willing(state, pid, "stay_over")[0]:
        return False
    stay = _data().get("stay") or {}
    left = convo.boundary_minutes(state, pid, state.world_clock_hours)
    early = left is not None and left / 60.0 < float(stay.get("early_start_hours", 9)) and \
        convo._status(state, pid, state.world_clock_hours + left / 60.0 + 0.01) == "work"
    return (_band_so_far(scn) in ("great", "good", "fine")
            and float(scn.get("energy", 0)) > float(stay.get("energy_min", 35)) and not early)


def stay_the_night(state: GameState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    scn = state.social.get("scene") or {}
    pid = str(scn["pid"])
    accepted = _stay_drawn_in_handler(state, pid)       # one SOCIAL_SCENE draw, inside social_scene (rule 3)
    if not accepted:
        reason = _not_tonight_reason(state, pid, scn)   # energy ("they're shattered") or the home's stay_reasons, else ""
        return {"ok": True, "stayed": False, "reason": reason}
    end_scene(state, reason="stayed_over", then="stay")
    relationships.add_history(state, pid, "stay", "Stayed over")
    target = _player_morning(state)
    clock.advance_time(state, max(0.0, target - state.world_clock_hours))
    return {"ok": True, "stayed": True}
```

`_stay_drawn_in_handler` runs the `scenes.yaml` `stay.accept` draw inside a `social_scene` `op: stay` effect (rule 3). The probability is `accept[archetype]`, falling back to `default`. There is NO `intent` term: the controller's ruling on pre-flight F34 drops it entirely, so the decision rests on the archetype and the evening's circumstances (`stay_offered`), and nothing about the person's dating intent biases it (Plan decision 26). It is forceable through `scene._stay_drawn`.

A "not tonight" is a plain answer, never a refusal: the receipt has `ok: True` and no `error`.

The reason comes ONLY from the scene's energy (below `stay.energy_min + 10` gives "they're shattered") or from the home template's `stay_reasons`. When neither applies, there is no reason (m5).

`_player_morning(state)` is the player's next waking band start, with the T11 sleep floor applied.

  - `people[pid]["life"]`: a person's running and past events, `[{id, at, until?}]`;
  - `state.social["player_life"]`: THE PLAYER'S events, the same row shape, written only by `social_life` (`who == "player"`) through `setdefault` (no other task writes or pre-creates it; Shared interfaces names it: pre-flight F28).

**`life_events.yaml` schema (§11.1)**, every row checked at load by `life.check_data`. Unknown keys are a load error.

```yaml
player:
  - {id: boiler, when: {}, prob_per_week: 0.08, cooldown_days: 60, effects: [], scene: boiler, news: player_news,
     flag: life_boiler, lasts_days: 2}
  - {id: parcel, when: {}, prob_per_week: 0.5, cooldown_days: 5, effects: [], scene: parcel, news: player_news,
     flag: life_parcel, lasts_days: 1}
  - {id: overtime, when: {}, prob_per_week: 0.1, cooldown_days: 30, schedule: {work_extra_hours: 2}, lasts_days: 7,
     news: player_news}
  # ... rough_week, promotion, a_cold, friends_birthday, family_visit, wedding_invite, lift_out, neighbour_party, payday (12)
person:
  - {id: new_job, when: {stage_min: talking}, prob_per_week: 0.02, cooldown_days: 120, canon: "They started a new job",
     followup: {about: "their first week at the new job", when: "days:3"}, news: news, job: draw}
  - {id: holiday, when: {stage_min: talking}, prob_per_week: 0.04, cooldown_days: 90, blocked_by_open_promise: true,
     lasts_days: 7, mood: {value: playful, days: 7}, news: news, unavailable: true}
  - {id: job_offer_elsewhere, when: {stage_min: dating}, prob_per_week: 0.01, cooldown_days: 365, proposal: stay_or_go,
     news: news}
  # ... lost_job, moved_house (home: redraw), new_pet, family_illness, siblings_wedding, birthday, ex_reappears,
  #     promotion, race, friends_breakup, a_bad_month (14)
seen:                               # §12.4, used by T18
  prob: 0.25
  trust: -25
  mood_days: 5
```

Each row's text is a plain fact. A person's `job: draw` re-draws the job from the person's own counterless key plus the event count (`attrs.key(seed, SOCIAL_LIFE, pid, f"job:{n}")`). This is a fact change written through `social_life`. `population.profile` stays the seed's, and `who_now` reads the life overlay.

**The daily hook (§11.1), complete:**

```python
def _event_drawn(state: GameState, who: str, row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """A week's odds as a day's (prob_per_week / 7), one SOCIAL_LIFE draw (lesson 1: forceable)."""
    rng = (world_rng(state, SOCIAL_LIFE) if who == "player"
           else behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_LIFE, who))
    return rng.random() < float(row.get("prob_per_week", 0)) / 7.0


def daily(state: GameState) -> None:
    """events.daily_hooks entry: end what has run its days; then the player's draws; then each live person's."""
    day_at = events.daily_at(state)
    _expire(state, day_at)
    for row in social.data("life_events").get("player") or []:
        if _eligible(state, "player", row, day_at) and _event_drawn(state, "player", row):
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_life", "op": "start", "who": "player", "id": row["id"], "at": day_at})
    for pid in messaging.live_people(state):
        for row in social.data("life_events").get("person") or []:
            if row.get("blocked_by_open_promise") and promises.open_by(state, pid, by="person"):
                continue                                  # a holiday waits for open promises (§5.3)
            if _eligible(state, pid, row, day_at) and _event_drawn(state, pid, row):
                apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_life", "op": "start", "who": pid, "id": row["id"], "at": day_at})
```

The `social_life` `start` handler records the event and then, per its row:
- the canon line (public rows, through `social_memory`);
- the follow-up (`memory.add_open`);
- the `news` flag for the next episode (`people[pid]["news"]`, which `convo.decide` carries once as `situation="news"`);
- mood (through `social_regard`'s mood path);
- schedule overrides (the player's `work_extra_hours`);
- the home redraw;
- the player-side flag (`life_<id>`);
- an `unavailable` window, which `convo.available` reads (unavailable while the event runs).

A `proposal: stay_or_go` row queues the person's ask: a request with `what: stay_or_go`, answered by the player with "ask them to stay" or "tell them to go". The engine decides the outcome by `growth.willing(state, pid, "stay_or_go")`:
- **They stay:** a history row, and `growth.gain("support")`.
- **They go:** `relationships.set_stage(state, pid, "ended")`.

**Support (§6.3):** a player reply in the event's window (any message to them while it runs), or a scene with them while it runs, gains `support`, once per event.

The decks `boiler.yaml` and `parcel.yaml` use the existing deck grammar with `when: {flag: life_boiler, at_location: apt_kitchen}` and `when: {flag: life_parcel, at_location: bldg_reception}`. Each has 1-2 cards, with plain beats that clear the flag through their outcomes. Copy a shipped deck's shape (`Glob games/hue-and-cry/data/scenes/*.yaml`).

### Task 18: Long-term (uncuttable) (§12) — L

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/test_social_long_term.py`
- Modify:
  - `engine/social/relationships.py`: the three stages' triggers and gates; `living_together` only from `exclusive`; the household as its own record; `exclusive_since`;

  - `engine/social/world.py`: `narration_block`'s household line; the `scene` verb offered at home with no plan when the partner is present;
  - `engine/social/kinds.py`: the stages exist since T1; `SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` gains `"social_household"`, its handler landing here (pre-flight F1, Plan decision 23);
  - `engine/social/voicing.py`: `YOU AND THEM` lists `move_in`/`propose` when willing;

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T5 `willing` (`exclusive`, `move_in`, `propose`); T13 scenes; T17 life events and `seen`; T11 world.
- Produces:
  - the `social_household` handler (`move_in`, `move_out`), and `kinds.SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` ∋ `"social_household"` (F1);
  - `relationships.ask(...)` with `what` in `exclusive`, `move_in`, `propose`;
  - the person's proposals `move_in` and `propose` (reply schema, through `legal_proposals`);
  - `scene.open_scene(..., kind="wedding")`, `scene.open_scene(..., kind="at_home")`;
  - `life.seen_check(state, pid)` and `life._seen_drawn` (forceable).

The rules (§12.1, m6):
- **`move_in` asked and accepted:**
  - at stage `exclusive`, the stage becomes `living_together` (`social_stage`), and the household is recorded (`social_household` `move_in`, `{"pid": pid}`);
  - at `engaged` or `married`, the stage does NOT change and only the household is recorded.

  This complete function lives in `relationships.py`:

```python
def apply_move_in(state: GameState, pid: str, *, at: Optional[float] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Moving in (§12.1, m6): the partner moves into the player's flat. From `exclusive` the stage becomes
    `living_together`; at `engaged` or `married` only the household changes. Gated by `willing.move_in`."""
    ok, why = growth.willing(state, pid, "move_in")
    if not ok:
        return _no(why)
    stage = str(state.social["people"][pid].get("stage") or "")
    if stage == "exclusive":
        set_stage(state, pid, "living_together", at=at)
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_household", "op": "move_in", "pid": pid,
                         "at": state.world_clock_hours if at is None else at})
    add_history(state, pid, "household", "Moved in together")
    return {"ok": True, "stage": state.social["people"][pid]["stage"], "household": pid}
```

- **`propose`** goes from `exclusive`/`living_together` to `engaged`. It is the player's scene closer `propose` (a `close` target, offered when `willing(propose)` holds) or phone ask, or the person's `propose` proposal. It writes `engaged_since`.
- **`married`:** the `wedding` life event fires `wedding_after_days` after `engaged_since` (a person-side row with `when: {stage: engaged, days_since: engaged_since >= wedding_after_days}` and odds 1). It queues a `registry_office` plan for the player. Arriving opens the wedding scene: three moments, only `quiet`/`tell_about`/`playful`, and every band floored at `fine`, so it cannot go badly (§12.2). The end sets the stage to `married`.
- **No stage is an ending.** `winding_down`, `ended` and cold recovery apply to all of them (v0.22's `cold:` rule; §12.4 says it IS the repair).
- **At-home scenes:** with a household, when the partner's schedule status is `free` and the player is at the flat, the `scene` verb is offered with no plan (`open_scene(..., kind="at_home")`), and the world turn's companion block includes them.
- **Seen (§12.4):** each scene with someone ELSE while the player is `exclusive` or later with a partner draws `seen.prob` once at the scene's end (`_seen_drawn`, SOCIAL_LIFE). On discovery:
  - the partner's trust drops by `seen.trust`;
  - `growth` `guard` (+1);
  - the mood is `hurt` for `seen.mood_days`;
  - the history row "They found out".
- Moving into theirs is NOT WIRED: a GOVERNANCE row naming `engine/social/relationships.py` `living_together`.

```python
    "CHANGELOG.md": "a changelog records what was removed, by name (every CHANGELOG.md under the scanned roots)",
    # T20 adds: "engine/social/migrate.py": "the v0.22 -> v0.23 migration must name the keys it drops"
}


def _exempt(path: Path) -> bool:
    rel = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
    return rel in EXEMPT_FILES or path.name in EXEMPT_FILES


def _sources(bases):
    for base in bases:
        for path in (ROOT / base).rglob("*"):
            if path.is_file() and path.suffix in (".py", ".yaml", ".md", ".js", ".jsx") and not _exempt(path):
                yield path


def _hits(bases, names):
    return [f"{p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()}: {name}" for p in _sources(bases) for name in names
            if name in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")]
```

### Task 20: Migration (§16, N8, I15) — M

**Files:**

- Modify:
  - `engine/persistence/saves.py`: `SaveStore.load` calls `upgrade`;
  - `engine/session/store.py`: `_build`'s schema guard;
  - `engine/social/places.py`: `LOCATION_ALIASES` is DELETED and its `from_dict` hook removed (§24's table);
  - `tests/test_social_places.py`: `test_the_home_alias_loads_a_v022_location` is replaced by `test_v022_home_maps_to_apt_lounge` here;
  - `engine/social/kinds.py`: `SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` gains `"social_migrate"`, its handler landing here (pre-flight F1, Plan decision 23); `tests/test_social_slice_v2.py` (`NEW_KINDS` gains it);
  - `tests/test_social_removed.py`: `EXEMPT_FILES` gains `"engine/social/migrate.py": "the v0.22 -> v0.23 migration must name the keys it drops"` (pre-flight F4);
  - the story CHANGELOG, docs/STATE.md.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every T1-T19 shape; `engine.persistence.migrations.MigrationError`; `attrs.materialise`; `social_go` `op: place`; `convo.next_available`; `plans.make`.
- Produces: `migrate.upgrade(state) -> bool` (True when it changed anything); the `social_migrate` handler (ops `apply` and `seal`), and `kinds.SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` ∋ `"social_migrate"` (F1); the `_build` guard.

**Never half-migrated (pre-flight F6).** `_plan` decides EVERYTHING first, reading only, including each unanswered read's `when` (`convo.next_available`, which can raise `ValueError` "never available in a week"). Then the writes are applied: `social_migrate` `op: apply` (which does NOT set `schema`), the attribute materialisation, the `home` place, the reads, the soon-plans. LAST, `social_migrate` `op: seal` sets `schema: 2`. Every exception from `_plan` or any write is wrapped in `MigrationError`, so `SaveStore.load` refuses the save. A fault after the first write therefore leaves the slice WITHOUT `schema: 2`: the next load starts again from the untouched save file, never from a slice marked done but incomplete.

**The hook, complete (`engine/persistence/saves.py::SaveStore.load`).** After `state = GameState.from_dict(migrated)`:

```python
        # READ RECEIPTS (v0.23.0 spec §16.1, N8): a phone story's slice is upgraded HERE, the one load path
        # every route takes (local resume, hosted, _flush_before_load), before _build, the first payload
        # and the voicer. Lazily imported (the clock.py pattern): no other story imports engine.social's
        # submodules, and CURRENT_SAVE_VERSION is untouched.
        from engine import social

        if social.declared() and getattr(state, "social", None):
            from engine.social import migrate as social_migrate

            try:
                social_migrate.upgrade(state)
            except MigrationError:
                logger.warning("[persistence] Save refused: its phone state could not be upgraded "
                               "(operation=load, id=%s)", save_id)
                raise
```

**The `_build` guard (`engine/session/store.py`).** It goes at the top of `_build`, before `GameEngine(state)`:

```python
        from engine import social

        if social.declared() and getattr(state, "social", None) and state.social.get("schema") != 2:
            # A route that bypassed SaveStore.load would voice a v0.22 slice (N8): fail loudly instead.
            raise RuntimeError(f"social: a schema-{state.social.get('schema', 1)} slice reached _build "
                               "without SaveStore.load's upgrade")
```

A fresh run's `state.social` is `{}` until creation's `social_profile` writes `empty_slice()`, which carries `schema: 2`. `{}` is falsy, so a new run passes.

**`engine/social/migrate.py`, complete:**

```python
"""
v0.22 -> v0.23 phone saves (spec §16). Idempotent; through ONE engine-only effect, `social_migrate`, whose
handler is the only writer (rule 3). A slice it cannot read raises MigrationError: refused, logged, never
half-migrated (the effect applies the whole upgrade or nothing).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from typing import Any

from engine.game.effects import EffectContext, apply_effect, effect_kind
from engine.game.state import GameState
from engine.persistence.migrations import MigrationError
from engine.social import attrs, convo, kinds, memory, messaging, plans
from engine.social.kinds import recorded

#: Stage -> the depth a v0.22 relationship starts v0.23 at (§16.2).
DEPTH_FROM_STAGE: dict[str, int] = {
    "matched": 0, "talking": 0, "numbers": 1, "meeting": 1,
    "dating": 2, "friends": 2, "fling": 2, "exclusive": 3,
}
#: Queue kinds dropped: re-derived from the threads (`read`), retired (`meet_start`).
DROP_KINDS = frozenset({"read", "meet_start"})
#: Initiation situations retired as bare situations (§13.1); the next daily re-queues by window.
RETIRED_SITUATIONS = frozenset({"good_morning", "check_in", "late_night"})


def upgrade(state: GameState) -> bool:
    """Bring `state.social` to schema 2 in place. A no-op at schema 2 (idempotent). Never half-migrated
    (pre-flight F6): everything is decided in `_plan`, the writes are applied, and `schema` is set LAST by
    `op: seal`; any exception anywhere is a MigrationError, so the save is refused, not marked done."""
    from engine.social import events

    slice_ = state.social
    if slice_.get("schema") == kinds.SCHEMA:
        return False
    try:
        plan = _plan(state)                              # every change decided first, reading only
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_migrate", "op": "apply", "plan": plan})
        for pid in plan["materialise"]:
            attrs.materialise(state, pid)                 # social_person, the counterless keys (I14)
        if state.location_id == "home":
            apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_go", "op": "place", "to": "apt_lounge"})   # Plan decision 16
        for pid, thread_id, when in plan["answer"]:      # `when` decided in _plan (F6)
            events.push(state, when, pid, "read", thread_id=thread_id)
        for row in plan["soon_plans"]:
            plans.make(state, row["pid"], kind=row["kind"], when="soon", venue=row["venue"], by="player",
                       at=state.world_clock_hours, migrated=True)
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_migrate", "op": "seal"})   # schema 2, LAST
    except MigrationError:
        raise
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- every fault refuses the save (F6)
        raise MigrationError(f"social: the save's phone state cannot be upgraded: {exc}") from exc
    return True


def _gone(state: GameState, pid: str) -> bool:
    """A person who left: unmatched, blocked, ended, faded, expired or ghosted (Review Focus 3)."""
    from engine.social import apps

    person = (state.social.get("people") or {}).get(pid) or {}
    return bool(person.get("ghosted")) or apps.gone(state, pid) or person.get("stage") in (
        "ended", "faded", "expired", "winding_down")


def _plan(state: GameState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    s = state.social
    people = s.get("people") or {}
    threads = s.get("threads") or {}
    growth_rows, stories, bridges, answer, materialise = {}, {}, {}, [], []
    by_pid: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for tid in sorted(threads):
        t = threads[tid]
        by_pid.setdefault(str(t["pid"]), []).append(t)
    for pid, person in sorted(people.items()):
        if not person.get("stage"):
            continue                                       # swiped-only people: nothing to grow
        materialise.append(pid)
        growth_rows[pid] = {"depth": DEPTH_FROM_STAGE.get(str(person["stage"]), 0), "openness": 0.0}
        own = sorted(by_pid.get(pid, []), key=lambda t: float((t.get("opened_at") or 0) or 0))
        stories[pid] = memory.cap_story(" ".join(str(t.get("summary") or "") for t in own if t.get("summary")))
        newest = max(own, key=lambda t: messaging.last_at(t), default=None)
        if newest is not None:
            voiced = [m for m in newest.get("messages") or [] if m.get("voiced") is True]
            bridges[pid] = [dict(m) for m in voiced[-6:]]
            last = messaging.last_shown(newest, spoken=True)
            if last is not None and last.get("from") == "player" and not _gone(state, pid):
                when = convo.next_available(state, pid, state.world_clock_hours)    # may raise: decided HERE (F6)
                answer.append((pid, str(newest["thread_id"]), when))
    queue = []
    for event in s.get("queue") or []:
        kind = str(event["kind"])
        if kind in DROP_KINDS:
            continue
        situation = ((event.get("decision") or {}).get("situation") or "")
        if kind in ("initiate",) and situation in RETIRED_SITUATIONS:
            continue
        queue.append(dict(event))
    soon_plans = []
    for row in (s.get("meets") or {}).values():
        status = str(row.get("status") or "")
        if status in ("agreed", "due", "planned"):
            soon_plans.append({"pid": row["pid"], "kind": row.get("kind") or "drinks", "venue": row.get("venue") or ""})
    scenes_done = {str(r["pid"]): 0 for r in (s.get("meets") or {}).values()}
    for row in (s.get("meets") or {}).values():
        if row.get("status") == "done":
            scenes_done[str(row["pid"])] += 1
    pending_meet_asks = [rid for rid, r in (s.get("requests") or {}).items()
                         if r.get("what") == "meet" and r.get("status") == "pending"]
    return {"growth": growth_rows, "stories": stories, "bridges": bridges, "answer": answer,
            "materialise": materialise, "queue": queue, "soon_plans": soon_plans,
            "scenes_done": scenes_done, "pending_meet_asks": pending_meet_asks}


@effect_kind("social_migrate")
@recorded
def _e_migrate(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    s = state.social
    if effect.get("op") == "seal":                          # the LAST write of an upgrade (F6)
        s["schema"] = kinds.SCHEMA
        return {"type": "social_migrate", "ok": True, "op": "seal", "save_reason": "migrated"}
    plan = effect["plan"]
    fresh = kinds.empty_slice()
    for key in ("promises", "plans", "scene", "household", "at_work"):
        s.setdefault(key, copy.deepcopy(fresh[key]))
    s["phone_up"] = True
    settings = s.setdefault("settings", {})
    settings.pop("auto_wait", None)
    settings.setdefault("wake_for_messages", False)
    counters = s.setdefault("counters", {})
    for key, value in fresh["counters"].items():
        counters.setdefault(key, value)
    for pid, row in plan["growth"].items():
        person = s["people"][pid]
        person.pop("pending_read", None)
        person["episode"] = None
        person["scenes_done"] = int(plan["scenes_done"].get(pid, 0))
        person.setdefault("episodes_done", 0)
        person["growth"] = {**row, "pace": 1.0, "guarded": 0, "guarded_left": 0, "revealed": [],
                            "peak_depth": row["depth"], "reveal_due": ""}   # pace overwritten by materialise
        mem = person.setdefault("memory", memory.fresh())
        mem["story"] = plan["stories"].get(pid, "")
        mem["bridge"] = plan["bridges"].get(pid, [])
        for moved in ("canon", "knows", "told", "history"):
            if moved in person and not mem.get(moved):
                mem[moved] = person.pop(moved)
    for t in (s.get("threads") or {}).values():
        t.pop("summary", None)
        t.pop("summary_through", None)
        for m in t.get("messages") or []:
            d = m.get("decision") or {}
            if m.get("voiced") is False and d:
                d.setdefault("mode", "quick")             # other due messages are voiced as reply, quick (§16.2)
    s["queue"] = sorted(plan["queue"], key=lambda e: (float(e["at"]), int(e.get("seq", 0))))
    for rid in plan["pending_meet_asks"]:
        s["requests"][rid]["when"] = "soon"                 # a pending meet request becomes a soon-plan ask (T12's `ask {what: meet, when}`)
        s["requests"][rid].pop("day", None)
        s["requests"][rid].pop("slot", None)
    s.pop("meets", None)
    counters.pop("meet", None)
    # `schema` is NOT set here: `op: seal` sets it after every other write has landed (F6).
    return {"type": "social_migrate", "ok": True, "op": "apply"}
```

`memory.cap_story(text)`, `memory.fresh()`, `messaging.last_at(t)` and `messaging.last_shown` are T6/v0.22 functions; make public any that are still private, with the rename applied at every caller. `plans.make(..., migrated=True)` skips the willingness and flake checks: the meet was agreed in v0.22.

**The recorded fixture (§16.3).** `record_v022.py`, run from the repo root of a worktree at `60215f4`:
0. Its first lines put the WORKTREE's code first on the import path and prove it (pre-flight F31). Run as a script, `sys.path[0]` is the script's own folder (this checkout's `tests/fixtures/social/v022_save`), so `import engine` would not resolve to the worktree:
   ```python
   import os, sys
   from pathlib import Path
   sys.path.insert(0, os.getcwd())                       # the 60215f4 worktree's root (the cwd)
   import engine                                          # noqa: E402
   assert Path(engine.__file__).resolve().is_relative_to(Path(os.getcwd()).resolve()), engine.__file__
   ```
1. It sets `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR` to a fresh temp directory (the owner's storage is never touched).
2. It activates `read-receipts`.
3. It builds a run with v0.22's `SessionStore.create(seed=7)`.
4. It drives `scripts.simulate_social`'s `Attentive` policy for 10 in-game days through v0.22's `Phone`.
5. It then, using v0.22's own functions:
   - sends one player message in the newest live thread with nothing advanced after it (unanswered);
   - makes sure one meet is `agreed` (`meets.propose` with `meets.answer_ask` forced True);
   - leaves the queue's `daily` in place.
6. It saves through v0.22's `SessionStore`/`SaveStore`.
7. It copies `save.json` and `memory.json` into `tests/fixtures/social/v022_save/` of THIS checkout.

Commands (the README carries both forms; rule 11, pre-flight F31):

```text
git worktree add ..\cd-v022 60215f4
cd ..\cd-v022
..\clockwork-dark\.venv\Scripts\python.exe ..\clockwork-dark\tests\fixtures\social\v022_save\record_v022.py --out ..\clockwork-dark\tests\fixtures\social\v022_save
cd ..\clockwork-dark
git worktree remove ..\cd-v022
```

POSIX `sh`:

```sh

cd ../cd-v022
../clockwork-dark/.venv/bin/python ../clockwork-dark/tests/fixtures/social/v022_save/record_v022.py --out ../clockwork-dark/tests/fixtures/social/v022_save
cd ../clockwork-dark
git worktree remove ../cd-v022
```

- `save_store_direct`: `store.load(...)`, then `GameState.to_client_dict()`.
