## Phase C — the client

Every Phase C task:
- writes its tests with `// @vitest-environment jsdom` on line 1, `createRoot` plus `React.act` through `ui/tests/helpers/dom.js`, and no `@testing-library` (lesson 5);
- runs `npm test --prefix ui`;
- rebuilds with `npm run build --prefix ui` and commits `content/scenes/clockwork/static/dist` IN THE SAME COMMIT (lesson 6);
- runs `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\run_tests.py files tests/test_ui_contract.py tests/test_ui_panels_manifest.py tests/test_no_story_literals_in_core_ui.py <GUARDS>`.

The phone fixture `ui/tests/fixtures/phone.js` and `ui/tests/fixtures/phone-harness.jsx` are v0.22's, and their REAL signatures bind every Phase C test (pre-flight F8, verified at `a2ed481`):
- `phoneFixture({ superLeft, likesLeft, hidden, phone = {} } = {})`: new payload keys go INSIDE `phone`, e.g. `phoneFixture({ phone: { world, plans, promises, hints, places, scene } })`; a top-level `world`/`plans`/… is silently ignored;
- `coreState(phone, story = initialState, busy = false)` is POSITIONAL and is NOT changed (462 v0.22 client tests call it). T21 adds a new helper beside it, `worldState({ phone, choices = [] } = {})`, which returns `{ ...coreState(phone ?? phoneFixture()), choices }` (core keeps a turn's choices at the top of its state, `state.choices`);
- `PlayScreen({ state, sessionId, onAct, onOpenMenu, onOpenSaves, blocked })`: a phone action goes through `onAct(intent)` (via `usePhoneAct`, which passes `{action, target, args, text}`), never an `act` prop; T21 adds `onChoose(choice)` for a world choice (below);
- `ComposeBar({ threadId, app, player, sessionId, act, sending, lost, onSend, inputRef, readyAsks })`: a send goes through `onSend(intent)`, and a send that never went comes back as `lost` (v0.22's draft-keeping path);
- `ContactCard({ contact, phone, act, pending, onBack, onOpenThread, sheet })`: it takes a `contact` ROW (`phone.contacts[i]`), not a `pid`;
- `Calendar({ phone, act, pending, onOpenContact })`.

Each task extends the fixture with the new payload keys, passed through `phone`:
- `phone.world`: `{phone_up, at_work, location, buzz: {count, who}}`;
- `phone.promises`;
- `phone.plans`;
- `phone.hints`;
- `phone.places`: `{venues: [{id, label}]}` (T11's section; pre-flight F18);
- `phone.scene`: `{pid, name, stage_words, closeness_words, portrait}` (T13's section; pre-flight F18).

Hidden-value scans plant sentinel VALUES and assert their text is absent; they never grep English words (lesson 7; pre-flight F9).

### Task 21: World and phone layers (§14.1) — L

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/world/World.jsx`, `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/world/Buzz.jsx`, `ui/tests/rr-world.test.jsx`
- Modify:
  - `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/PlayScreen.jsx` (two layers);
  - `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/phone/StatusBar.jsx` ("Put the phone down");
  - `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/phone/usePhoneAct.js` (`put_down`; any act raises the phone);
  - `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/state.js` (`phoneUp`, derived from `phone.world.phone_up`);
  - `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/theme/read-receipts.css` (the layers, ≥ 1200 px beside, under 1200 over);
  - `ui/src/core/App.jsx`: the story-owned `PlayScreen` is given `onChoose={(choice) => send(choice.id, null, choice.text, choice)}`, the same callback core's own `Play` gets (`App.jsx:775`). v0.22 passes a story's `PlayScreen` only `onAct` (phone actions), so without it a world choice has no way out; no other story has a `PlayScreen`, so nothing else moves (Plan decision 28);
  - `ui/tests/fixtures/phone.js`;
  - `ui/tests/fixtures/phone-harness.jsx`: `worldState({ phone, choices })` added; `coreState` unchanged (F8);
  - `engine/social/payload.py` consumer (the `world` section; T11 already exposes `phone_up`; T21 adds `buzz`/`location` words if missing);
  - the story CHANGELOG.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - core `ScenePlate`, `NarrativeLog`, `ChoiceRow`, `Chrome` (`ui/src/core/`; read their props first);
  - the `phone_action` socket event and the `player_choice` emit (v0.22's `socket.js`);
  - T11's payload keys and the 409 `phone_up: true`.
- Produces:
  - `World({state, phone, onChoose, phoneUp, wide})`, where `state` is the core state and its choices are `state.choices`;
  - `Buzz({buzz, onPickUp})`: renders `buzz.count` and `buzz.who` (names) ONLY, never any other key of `buzz` and never a notification's `preview`;
  - `PlayScreen`'s layer state, and its new prop `onChoose(choice)` (Plan decision 28);
  - `ChoiceRow` disabled with the note "Put the phone down to act" while `phoneUp`;
  - `ui/tests/fixtures/phone-harness.jsx::worldState` (F8).

The at-work verbs (`carry_on_working`, `leave_early`) are ordinary choices in the world's `ChoiceRow`, and need no special UI.

// @vitest-environment jsdom
// ui/tests/rr-world.test.jsx -- READ RECEIPTS' two layers (v0.23.0 T21, spec §14.1, §19.9).
import React from "react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import PlayScreen from "../src/stories/read-receipts/PlayScreen.jsx";
import World from "../src/stories/read-receipts/world/World.jsx";
import Buzz from "../src/stories/read-receipts/world/Buzz.jsx";
import { phoneFixture } from "./fixtures/phone.js";

let view;
beforeEach(() => { view = mount(); });
afterEach(() => { view.unmount(); vi.restoreAllMocks(); delete window.matchMedia; });

const wide = (yes) => { window.matchMedia = (q) => ({ matches: yes && q.includes("1200"), addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {} }); };
// New payload keys go INSIDE `phone` (phoneFixture's real signature; pre-flight F8).

const screen = (state, over = {}) => (
  <PlayScreen state={state} sessionId="s1" onAct={() => true} onChoose={() => {}} onOpenMenu={() => {}} onOpenSaves={() => {}} {...over} />
);

describe("world and phone layers", () => {
  it("greys the world's choices while the phone is up, side by side at 1200px", () => {
    wide(true);
    const phone = phoneFixture({ phone: { world: WORLD() } });
    view.render(screen(worldState({ phone, choices: [{ id: "c1", text: "Tidy up" }] })));
    const choice = button(view.host, /Tidy up/);
    expect(choice.disabled).toBe(true);
    expect(view.host.textContent).toContain("Put the phone down to act");
    expect(byTestId(view.host, "rr-phone-layer").className).toContain("beside");
  });

  it("covers the world at phone sizes, dimmed behind", () => {
    wide(false);
    const phone = phoneFixture({ phone: { world: WORLD() } });
    view.render(screen(worldState({ phone })));
    expect(byTestId(view.host, "rr-phone-layer").className).toContain("over");
    expect(byTestId(view.host, "rr-world-layer").className).toContain("dimmed");
  });

  it("sends put_down from the status bar through onAct", () => {
    const onAct = vi.fn(() => true);                     // PlayScreen's real prop (F8); usePhoneAct fills the shape
    const phone = phoneFixture({ phone: { world: WORLD() } });
    view.render(screen(worldState({ phone }), { onAct }));
    click(button(view.host, "Put the phone down"));
    expect(onAct).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ action: "put_down", target: "", args: {}, text: "" });

  });

    const onChoose = vi.fn();

    view.render(<World state={worldState({ choices: [choice] })} phoneUp={false} onChoose={onChoose} />);

});

### Task 23A: World choices, on top of T23 (A4 W1) — S

**What T23 already shipped (`6fe497c`).** `turn.run_mechanics` answers every phone action with `away.world_choices(state)` (test `test_a_phone_actions_answer_carries_the_worlds_choices`). So the row no longer empties, and a time jump always rebuilds it. **Read `engine/social/turn.py` and `engine/social/away.py` at HEAD first.**

**What is left** (A4 W1's three gaps):
1. The row is never written into `session.last_turn`, so its `resume_*` ids are refused as `STALE_CHOICE` after any narrated turn (`default_state._chosen`), and a refresh restores the old row.
2. There are no room moves (`travel`).
3. The narrator's row is thrown away after a glance at the phone, and a scene opened by a phone action is never said.

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `engine/social/turn.py` (`run_mechanics`: the fingerprint, the row written into `last_turn`, the scene-open line);
  - `engine/social/away.py` (`world_choices(state, *, prefix="resume")`, plus `travel`);
  - the story CHANGELOG and the root CHANGELOG.
- Test: `tests/test_social_world.py`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - T22's `away.world_choices`;
  - T23's `run_mechanics` row;
  - `intents.legal_intents`;
  - `scene.receipt_lines`.
- Produces:
  - `turn.world_fingerprint(state) -> tuple`;
  - `away.world_choices(state, *, prefix: str = "resume") -> list[dict]`, now with `travel`.

The fingerprint and the row, load-bearing:

```python
_FINGERPRINT_ACTIONS = frozenset({"do", "go", "scene", "close", "travel"})


def world_fingerprint(state: GameState) -> tuple:
    """What the world row depends on (A4 W1): the place, the scene, work, the clock, and the legal world targets."""
    from engine import social
    from engine.game import intents
    from engine.social import scene

    scn = scene.current(state) if social.scene_open(state) else {}
    targets = tuple(sorted((v.action, t) for v in intents.legal_intents(state)
                           if v.action in _FINGERPRINT_ACTIONS for t in v.targets))
    return (state.location_id, str(scn.get("pid") or ""), str(scn.get("kind") or ""),
            bool(state.social.get("at_work")), round(state.world_clock_hours, 4), targets)
```

In `run_mechanics`:
- take `before = world_fingerprint(state)` before the action runs, and `after` once it has run;
- build the row:

```python
row = (list((getattr(session, "last_turn", None) or {}).get("choices") or [])
       if after == before else away.world_choices(state, prefix="world"))
session.last_turn = {**(session.last_turn or {}), "choices": row}
turn_payload["choices"] = row
if social.scene_open(state) and not before[1] and after[1]:   # a scene opened by this phone action
    turn_payload["narration"] = scene.receipt_lines(state).splitlines()[0]
```

`world_choices` adds `travel` options from `legal_intents` (label: the place's `name`, never "Go to"), and its ids become `f"{prefix}_{action}_{target}"`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (append to `tests/test_social_world.py`):

```python
def test_a_phone_rows_choice_is_not_stale(receipts_active) -> None:
    """A4 W1 gap 1: after a narrated turn, a phone action's row is the frame; picking from it runs, never 409."""
    stub = StubNarrator()
    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=stub)
    _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    assert _choose(scene, session, "Tidy up a bit").status_code == 200          # a narrated turn: last_turn is the narrator's
    body = _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    pick = next(c for c in body["choices"] if c.get("intent"))
    resp = scene.app.test_client().post("/api/game/choice", json={"session_id": session.session_id,
                                                                  "choice_id": pick["id"]})
    assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.get_data(as_text=True)


def test_refresh_after_phone_action_matches_the_row(receipts_active) -> None:
    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    body = _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    assert session.last_turn["choices"] == body["choices"]


def test_phone_glance_keeps_the_narrators_choices(receipts_active) -> None:
    """Nothing in the world changed (a settings toggle costs no time): the narrator's row stays."""
    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    _choose(scene, session, "Tidy up a bit")
    narrated = list(session.last_turn["choices"])
    body = _phone(scene, session, "set_receipts", "natter", args={"to": "off"})
    assert body["choices"] == narrated


def test_put_down_across_a_jump_gives_the_new_row(receipts_active) -> None:
    """I1: a clock advance changes the fingerprint, so the row is rebuilt from what is legal now."""
    from engine.social import away, turn

    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    before = turn.world_fingerprint(session.engine.state)
    _phone(scene, session, "wait", "evening")
    body = _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    assert turn.world_fingerprint(session.engine.state) != before
    assert body["choices"] == away.world_choices(session.engine.state, prefix="world")


def test_head_home_row_reaches_the_rooms(receipts_active) -> None:
    """I2: the rebuilt row carries the flat's room moves (core `travel`)."""
    from engine.social import away

    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    state = session.engine.state
    rooms = {c["intent"]["target"] for c in away.world_choices(state, prefix="world")
             if (c.get("intent") or {}).get("action") == "travel"}
    assert {"apt_hall", "apt_dining"} <= rooms                                  # the lounge's two doors


@pytest.mark.parametrize("loc", sorted(__import__("engine.social.places", fromlist=["LOCATIONS"]).LOCATIONS))
def test_no_go_target_is_here(receipts_active, loc) -> None:
    from engine.game import procgen
    from engine.social import away

    state = procgen.new_game_state(player_name="Sam", seed=4242)
    state.location_id = loc
    assert all((c.get("intent") or {}).get("target") != loc for c in away.world_choices(state)
               if (c.get("intent") or {}).get("action") in ("go", "travel"))


def test_scene_opened_by_phone_is_said(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """A Places-app arrival that keeps a plan opens the scene; the answer says so in the engine's words."""
    from engine.social import plans

    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    state = session.engine.state
    pid = _matched_in(session, "matchstick").split(":")[1]
    monkeypatch.setattr(plans, "_flake_drawn", lambda s, p: False)
    with session.lock:
        row = plans.make(state, pid, kind="drinks", when="now", venue="the_anchor", in_person=True)
    body = _phone(scene, session, "go", "the_anchor")
    assert row and body["narration"] and "the_anchor" not in body["narration"]
```

`LOCATIONS` is `engine/social/places.py`'s table, verified at HEAD (`places.py:158`). If the Places-app `go` verb's target name differs, read `world.places_go` and `turn.py`'s `go` phone verb first. `in_person=True` keeps the test independent of T23C's arrival times.

### Task 23C: Plans kept in place (A4 W2-W4, C1, I3-I5) — L

**Files:**
- Create: `engine/social/presence.py` (the plan tier only; T23G adds the chance tier); `tests/test_social_arrival.py`.
- Modify:
  - `engine/social/plans.py`: `_make`, `_schedule`, `_upgrade`, `arrive`, `running_late`, the `late` op, a new `arrived` op, `plan_arrive`'s handler and `meet_if_here`;
  - `engine/social/world.py`: `go`'s arrival, `put_down`, the `scene plan:<id>` target, the end of `do`;
  - `engine/social/scene.py`: `end_scene` (`then: stay` → `meet_if_here`), and the `your_place` lounge move;
  - `engine/social/turn.py`: `_next_notifying_at` counts a `plan_arrive` at the player's place;
  - `engine/social/agreement.py`: `arrival_claimed`, its snapshot fields and the commit re-check;
  - `engine/social/voicing.py`: the whereabouts line;
  - `engine/scenes/default_scene.py::_run_world_turn`: `meet_if_here` after the drain;
  - `engine/social/kinds.py`: situation `arrived`;
  - `games/read-receipts/prompts/texter.md`: the `arrived` row;
  - `games/read-receipts/data/social/plans.yaml`: `arrival:`;
  - `games/read-receipts/data/social/relationships.yaml`: `arrival_claims:`;
  - `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml`, only if a global is added (none is planned);
  - docs/AUTHORING.md §3.15, the story CHANGELOG and the root CHANGELOG.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T12 plans, T13 scenes, T15 agreement on scene prose, T7 snapshots.
- Produces:
  - plan rows gain `due_at`, `arrives_at` and `arrived`;
  - `plans.meet_if_here(state) -> dict`;
  - `presence.where(state, pid, here_view=None) -> str`;
  - `presence.with_player(state, pid, here_view=None) -> bool`;
  - `presence.here_view(state) -> dict` (T23G fills its chance part);
  - `social_plan` op `arrived`;
  - event `plan_arrive`;
  - agreement check `arrival_claimed`.

The arrival scheduling, load-bearing (in `plans.py`):

```python
def _arrival(state: GameState, row: dict[str, Any], effect: dict[str, Any], at: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """(due_at, arrives_at) for a now/tonight row (A4 W3/W4). In person: both `at`, the person comes WITH the player
    (C1). Their place: home already. Else their travel plus a lateness keyed on the plan id (counterless, rule 4)."""
    if effect.get("in_person"):
        return at, at
    pid, when = str(row["pid"]), str(row["when"])
    due = at
    if when == "tonight":
        span = _tonight_span(state, pid, at)
        due = max(at, span[0]) if span else at
    if row["kind"] == "their_place":
        return due, due
    place = places.home_of(state, pid) if row["kind"] == "your_place" else place_of(state, row)
    lo, hi = (_data().get("arrival") or {}).get("late_minutes", [0, 20])
    late = stable_rng(state.rng_seed, f"plan_arrival:{row['id']}").uniform(float(lo), float(hi))
    return due, due + (places.travel_minutes(place) + late) / 60.0
```

`_make` and `_upgrade` set `row["due_at"], row["arrives_at"] = _arrival(...)` and `row["arrived"] = bool(effect.get("in_person"))`. `_schedule` then:
- pushes `plan_lapse` at `arrives_at + patience_hours + late_grace_hours + lapse_after_hours`;
- unless `in_person`, pushes `plan_arrive` at `arrives_at`.

Each push replaces the row's earlier event of the same kind. Read `events.push` and how `plan_lapse` is found by `plan_id`, and add a `replace=True` path to `social_queue` only if none exists.

The four origin sites read `arrives_at`:
- `arrive`: `now - row["arrives_at"] > patience_hours`;
- `running_late`: `now <= row["arrives_at"] + patience_hours`;
- `late`: re-push `plan_lapse` to the new end;
- `_upgrade`: as above.

`plan_arrive` only records:

```python
@events.handles("plan_arrive")
def _on_plan_arrive(state: GameState, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The person is there (A4 W4, I4): record it; text if the player is not. Never opens a scene (we may be
    inside clock.advance_time, mid-travel); meet_if_here runs where the clock has stopped."""
    row = get(state, str(event.get("plan_id") or ""))
    if row is None or row["status"] != "open":
        return {"ok": True, "quiet": True}
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_plan", "op": "arrived", "plan_id": row["id"], "at": event["at"]})
    if not _player_at(state, row):
        _queue_arrived_text(state, row, event["at"])        # a Decision, situation "arrived", voiced by the texter
    return {"ok": True}


def meet_if_here(state: GameState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Open the scene of an arrived now/tonight plan whose place is where the player is, phone down, no scene open."""
    if social.scene_open(state) or state.social.get("phone_up"):
        return {"quiet": True}
    for row in open_for(state):
        if row["when"] != "soon" and row.get("arrived") and _player_at(state, row):
            if row["kind"] == "your_place" and state.location_id != "apt_lounge":
                apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_go", "op": "place", "to": "apt_lounge"})   # "you let them in"
            return _on_arrival_scene(state, row, state.location_id)
    return {"quiet": True}
```

`_player_at(state, row)` is `places.is_flat(here)` for `your_place`, else `place_of(state, row) == here`.

def _tonight_at_noon(state, matched, monkeypatch):
    """A `tonight` plan made at noon, their evening 19:00-23:00 (forced after `_willing_meet`, which forces its own)."""
    from engine.social import plans
    from tests.social_fixtures import _set_clock

    _willing_meet(monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(plans, "_tonight_span", lambda s, p, at: (at - at % 24 + 19, at - at % 24 + 23))
    pid = matched("matchstick").split(":")[1]
    _set_clock(state, 12)
    row = plans.make(state, pid, kind="drinks", when="tonight", venue="the_anchor")
    assert row["due_at"] == row["made_at"] - row["made_at"] % 24 + 19
    state.location_id = "merrow_street"
    state.social["phone_up"] = False
    return pid, row

    assert world.go(receipts_state, "the_anchor")["ok"]
    assert receipts_state.social["plans"][row["id"]]["status"] == "met"

**Interfaces:**

  - `convo.names_label` and `convo._named_topics`;
  - `life.player_news`;
  - T23B's client `custom` send.
- Produces:
  - `typed.intent_for(state, text) -> dict` (`{}` or a legal intent with `typed: True`);
  - `typed.match_offered(state, text) -> dict`;
  - the `talk` move.

The matcher, in full (A4 §3.1):

```python
"""Typed world lines read against the engine's offer (v0.23.0 addendum 4 §3, §3.1). Stateless: no globals."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

from engine import social
from engine.game import intents
from engine.game.state import GameState
from engine.social import convo

_SPLIT = re.compile(r"[,;.!]|\band\b|\bthen\b")
#: Actions the matcher may run. `go`, closers, work and phone actions are never inferred (A4 §3).
_NEVER = frozenset({"go_to_work", "carry_on_working", "leave_early"})


def _cues() -> dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    block = (social.data_world("activities") or {}).get("typed") or {}
    return {k: tuple(str(w).casefold() for w in block.get(k) or ()) for k in
            ("modals", "negations", "shifts", "leads", "progressive_leads", "movement")}


def _forms(verb: str, *, acted: bool, progressive: bool) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    out = [verb]
    if acted:
        out += [verb + "es" if verb.endswith(("s", "sh", "ch", "x", "o")) else verb + "s"]
    if acted or progressive:
        out += [verb[:-1] + "ing" if verb.endswith("e") and not verb.endswith("ee") else verb + "ing"]
    return tuple(out)


def _starts(clause: str, phrase: str, *, acted: bool, progressive: bool) -> bool:
    words = phrase.split()
    if len(words) < 2:
        return False
    rest = " ".join(words[1:])
    return any(re.match(rf"{re.escape(f)}\s+{re.escape(rest)}(?!\w)", clause)
               for f in _forms(words[0], acted=acted, progressive=progressive))


def _strip_lead(clause: str, leads: tuple[str, ...], progressive_leads: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    for lead in sorted(leads, key=len, reverse=True):
        if clause == lead or clause.startswith(lead + " "):
            clause = clause[len(lead):].strip()
            break
    for lead in progressive_leads:
        if clause.startswith(lead + " "):
            return clause[len(lead):].strip(), True
    return clause, False


def match_offered(state: GameState, text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One legal `do` or room `travel` the line names, anchored at a clause start, else {} (A4 §3.1)."""
    line = " ".join(str(text or "").casefold().split())
    acted = len(line) > 2 and line.startswith("*") and line.endswith("*")
    line = line.strip("*").strip()
    cues = _cues()
    if not line or line.endswith("?"):
        return {}
    if any(convo.names_label(line, w) for k in ("modals", "negations", "shifts") for w in cues[k]):
        return {}
    verbs = intents.legal_intents(state)
    do = intents.find_verb(verbs, "do")
    travel = intents.find_verb(verbs, "travel")
    phrases: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []                        # (action, target, phrase)
    for row in social.world_activities():
        if do and row["id"] in do.targets and row["id"] not in _NEVER:
            for p in [row.get("label", "")] + list(row.get("say") or ()):
                phrases.append(("do", row["id"], str(p).casefold()))
    rooms = ((social.data_world("activities") or {}).get("typed") or {}).get("rooms") or {}
    found: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    for raw in _SPLIT.split(line):
        clause, progressive = _strip_lead(raw.strip(), cues["leads"], cues["progressive_leads"])
        for action, target, phrase in phrases:
            if _starts(clause, phrase, acted=acted, progressive=progressive):
                found.add((action, target))
        if travel:
            for move in cues["movement"]:
                for form in _forms(move, acted=acted, progressive=progressive):
                    m = re.match(rf"{re.escape(form)}\b((?:\s+\S+){{0,3}}?)\s+(.+)$", clause)
                    if not m:
                        continue
                    for target in travel.targets:
                        nouns = [str(n).casefold() for n in rooms.get(target) or ()]
                        if any(re.match(rf"(?:\S+\s+){{0,3}}{re.escape(n)}(?!\w)", (m.group(1) + " " + m.group(2)).strip())
                               for n in nouns):
                            found.add(("travel", target))
    if len(found) != 1:
        return {}
    action, target = found.pop()
    return {"action": action, "target": target, "typed": True}
```

`social.data_world` and `social.world_activities` stand for however `world.py` reads `activities.yaml` (`world.activities()`). Use that one loader, not a second. `intent_for` runs, in order:
1. `match_offered`;
2. in a scene, a topic among the legal `scene` targets (their topics first when the line ends in `?`; the `work` topic only on the job label, "my job" or "my work");
3. in a scene, `scene talk` if legal;
4. `{}`.

Every result is re-checked with `find_verb` and `targets` before it is returned.

In `_run_world_turn`, after `voicer.drain_inbox(session)`:

```python
            if typed_text:
                from engine.social import typed as social_typed

                intent = social_typed.intent_for(session.engine.state, typed_text) or {}
```

def test_typed_reading_is_under_the_lock(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """C2: a voicing commit closes the scene between the route and the lock; the typed turn reads the closed
    state after the drain and is never refused."""
    from engine.social import scene as social_scene, voicer

    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    state = session.engine.state
    with session.lock:
        pid = __import__("tests.social_fixtures", fromlist=["_matched_in"])._matched_in(session, "matchstick").split(":")[1]
        _force_stage(state, pid, "dating")
        state.location_id = "the_anchor"
        social_scene.open_scene(state, pid, "the_anchor", kind="drinks")
    _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    real = voicer.drain_inbox

    def drain_and_close(sess):
        real(sess)
        social_scene.end_scene(sess.engine.state, "theirs", then="stay")

    monkeypatch.setattr(voicer, "drain_inbox", drain_and_close)
    resp = scene.app.test_client().post("/api/game/choice", json={
        "session_id": session.session_id, "choice_id": "custom", "custom_text": "you have a nice laugh"})
    assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.get_data(as_text=True)
    assert not any(r.get("refused") for r in resp.get_json()["tool_receipts"])

    assert "talk" in names and "phone" not in names and len(names) <= 8
    assert "talk" in scene.MOVES and "talk" in scene.MOVE_WORDS

### Task 23G: Chance meetings with the cast (A4 W9; C3, I7) — L

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `engine/social/presence.py` (the chance tier, `here_view`'s chance part, the short-circuit);
  - `engine/social/scene.py` (`approach:<pid>`, `kind: chance`, its prior, `_end`'s branch, `_close_options`' gate);
  - `engine/social/world.py` (the "Also here" line in `narration_block`; `here` in the `world` section);
  - `engine/social/messaging.py` and `engine/social/voicer.py` (W5 reads the chance tier through `here_view`);
  - `games/read-receipts/data/social/scenes.yaml` (`chance:`);
  - `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/world/World.jsx` (the "Also here" line);
  - `ui/tests/rr-world.test.jsx`;
  - `tests/test_ui_contract.py` (the world section's sixth key);
  - `content/scenes/clockwork/static/dist`;
  - AUTHORING, GOVERNANCE, the story and root CHANGELOGs.
- Create: `tests/test_social_chance.py`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T23C `presence`, T23D's hold, T23E's typed reading (a typed name opens nothing), T23F.
- Produces:
  - `presence.chance_here(state, location) -> tuple[str, ...]`;
  - `here_view` carrying `{"location", "chance": tuple}`;
  - the `world` section key `here: [{name, kind}]`;
  - the scene target `approach:<pid>`.

The chance draw, load-bearing:

```python
def _chance_venue(state: GameState, pid: str, block: int) -> str:
    """The ONE venue pid is at in this block, or "" (A4 W9). Counterless: reading draws and writes nothing."""
    cfg = social.data("scenes").get("chance") or {}
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    status = behaviour.status_at(person, state.world_clock_hours)
    prob = float((cfg.get("prob") or {}).get(status, 0.0))
    if prob <= 0.0:
        return ""
    rng = stable_rng(state.rng_seed, f"chance:{pid}:{int(state.world_clock_hours // 24)}:{block}")
    if rng.random() >= prob:
        return ""
    fitting = places.fitting_venues(state, pid)          # pick_venue's hobby overlap, as a list, no draw
    return fitting[int(rng.random() * len(fitting))] if fitting else ""
```

`chance_here(state, here)`:
- returns `()` unless `here` is a venue;
- takes the candidates (A4 W9's list, capped at `max_candidates`, most recent contact first);
- keeps those whose `_chance_venue` is `here`, ordered by pid;
- caps them at `max_here`.

`places.fitting_venues` is the overlap half of `pick_venue`, factored out so `pick_venue` uses it too (no behaviour change; its tie draw stays where it is).

`scene._end`'s branch, load-bearing (C3), replacing the number and meeting lines at `scene.py:1126-1131`:

```python
    stage = str(person.get("stage") or "")
    chance = kind_of_scene == CHANCE                      # read from scn["kind"] before the row is cleared
    may_swap = not chance or growth.willing(state, pid, "number")[0]
    may_meet = not chance or growth.willing(state, pid, "meet")[0]
    if band in NUMBER_BANDS and 0 <= stage_rank(stage) < stage_rank(NUMBERS) and may_swap:
        relationships.set_stage(state, pid, NUMBERS, at=at, kind="numbers", line="Swapped numbers in person")
        messaging.open_numbers(state, pid)
    if person.get("stage") == NUMBERS and may_meet:
        relationships.set_stage(state, pid, MEETING, at=at)
    if chance and not (may_swap and may_meet):
        relationships.history(state, pid, f"Ran into each other at {places.label(str(scn.get('location') or ''))}")
    relationships.advance_stage(state, pid, at=at)
```

The `meets_good` increment near the top of `_end` moves below this block and becomes: `if band in GOOD_BANDS and (not chance or stage_rank(person.get("stage")) >= stage_rank(MEETING))`. Read `relationships`' history writer's real name first.

`_close_options`: in a `chance` scene, drop `back_to_yours`, `back_to_theirs` and `go_somewhere_else`, and `call_it_a_night`'s `plan` form, unless `growth.willing(state, pid, "meet")[0]`.

```python
"""The owner's playtest, replayed (v0.23.0 addendum 4 §7): four symptoms, each closed."""

from __future__ import annotations

from tests.social_fixtures import (  # noqa: F401
    _live_session, _matched_in, _phone, receipts_active,
)
from tests.social_world_stub import StubNarrator


def _choose_id(scene, session, choice_id: str):
    return scene.app.test_client().post("/api/game/choice", json={"session_id": session.session_id,
                                                                  "choice_id": choice_id})


def _typed(scene, session, text: str):
    return scene.app.test_client().post("/api/game/choice", json={"session_id": session.session_id,
                                                                  "choice_id": "custom", "custom_text": text})


def _set_up(monkeypatch):
    from tests.test_social_plans import _willing_meet

    _willing_meet(monkeypatch)
    scene, session = _live_session(llm_fn=StubNarrator())
    with session.lock:
        pid = _matched_in(session, "matchstick").split(":")[1]
        from tests.social_fixtures import _force_stage

        _force_stage(session.engine.state, pid, "numbers")
    return scene, session, pid


def test_a_they_arrive_where_you_already_are(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """(a): a `now` plan at The Anchor made AT The Anchor; Wait for them; they arrive; the scene opens; no
    "I'm here" passes before; nothing lands while together."""
    from engine import social
    from engine.social import plans

    scene, session, pid = _set_up(monkeypatch)
    _phone(scene, session, "go", "the_anchor")
    with session.lock:
        row = plans.make(session.engine.state, pid, kind="drinks", when="now", venue="the_anchor")
    body = _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    wait = next(c for c in body["choices"] if (c.get("intent") or {}).get("target") == f"plan:{row['id']}")
    assert wait["text"].startswith("Wait for")
    assert _choose_id(scene, session, wait["id"]).status_code == 200
    assert social.scene_open(session.engine.state)


def test_b_the_row_after_a_places_arrival(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """(b): no "go to <here>"; with a plan, "Wait for" is on the row; after Head home, the rooms."""
    scene, session, _ = _set_up(monkeypatch)
    body = _phone(scene, session, "go", "the_anchor")
    assert all((c.get("intent") or {}).get("target") != "the_anchor" for c in body["choices"])


def test_c_the_row_and_last_turn_agree(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """(c): the press that 'chose the city' was a stale choice; now the row IS the frame."""
    scene, session, _ = _set_up(monkeypatch)
    body = _phone(scene, session, "go", "the_anchor")
    assert session.last_turn["choices"] == body["choices"]
    assert session.engine.state.location_id == "the_anchor"


def test_d_choices_without_a_refresh(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """(d): put_down (with and without a jump), a Places arrival: the row is never empty."""
    scene, session, _ = _set_up(monkeypatch)
    assert _phone(scene, session, "put_down")["choices"]
    _phone(scene, session, "wait", "evening")
    assert _phone(scene, session, "put_down")["choices"]
    assert _phone(scene, session, "go", "the_anchor")["choices"]


def test_typing_in_a_scene_and_at_home(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """§7.5 steps 4, 5 and 8 with the stub model: a topic question, an unread line, an activity, a room."""
    from engine.social import scene as social_scene

    scene, session, pid = _set_up(monkeypatch)
    with session.lock:
        session.engine.state.location_id = "the_anchor"
        social_scene.open_scene(session.engine.state, pid, "the_anchor", kind="drinks")
    _phone(scene, session, "put_down")
    talk = _typed(scene, session, "you have a nice laugh").get_json()
    assert any("talk" in str(r.get("args")) for r in talk["tool_receipts"])
```

Read each phone verb's real target spelling first (`go` from the Places app, `wait {until: evening}`), and use those names. The client scenario `ui/tests/rr-playtest.test.jsx` drives `PlayScreen` through a `turn_update` from a phone action (choices present) and a typed send: the row is never empty, and the compose box is live once the phone is down.

**Interfaces:** Consumes T16's `stay_the_night`. Produces `scene.open_scene(..., kind="morning")`. It closes with `call_it_a_night`, then `walk to the door`, which is a closer row `see_them_out` that ends the scene with `then: stay`.

The test is T16's `test_morning_scene_after_stay_over`, already written whole: the evening goes `back_to_yours` (a home scene at `apt_lounge`) before the stay, and the morning is asserted in `apt_kitchen` (pre-flight F32). This task removes its `@pytest.mark.skip`, and adds the host's-home case beside it:

```python
def test_a_morning_at_theirs_is_in_their_home(in_a_scene, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Pre-flight F32: a `home_*` host's home is a single location, so the morning scene is there."""
    from engine.social import growth, places, scene as social_scene

    _, session, state, pid = in_a_scene
    real = growth.willing
    monkeypatch.setattr(growth, "willing",
                        lambda s, p, c: (True, "") if c in ("stay_over", "meet", "go_to_theirs") else real(s, p, c))
    social_scene.close(state, "back_to_theirs")
    home = places.home_of(state, pid)
    assert state.location_id == home
    monkeypatch.setattr(social_scene, "stay_offered", lambda s: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(social_scene, "_stay_drawn", lambda s, p: True)
    social_scene.stay_the_night(state)
    assert state.social["scene"]["kind"] == "morning" and state.social["scene"]["location"] == home
```

**Interfaces:** Consumes T10's skeleton and `engine.media.providers.shipped.lookup(request: ImageRequest) -> Optional[str]` (the module is `engine/media/providers/shipped.py`, and `lookup` takes ONE `ImageRequest`, `engine/media/providers/base.py:38`: `subject_id`, `kind="location"`, `time_of_day`, `evil_phase`, `base_image`, `story`; verified; pre-flight F22). Produces the 48-subject table (§18.1).

`subjects.yaml` `style:` holds §18.1's prose verbatim, with its tags. Each location row is `{id, base: <daypart>, times: {<daypart>: <prompt>}}` with no `alts:`. The prompts are short place descriptions, are unpeopled, and carry no `negative` key, no "no X" terms and no rating words. Example:

```yaml
  - id: apt_kitchen
    base: dawn
    times:
      dawn: "a galley kitchen at first light, kettle on the hob, window over a narrow lane"
      dusk: "a galley kitchen at dusk, warm under-cabinet light, a pan left to soak"
      night: "a galley kitchen at night, one lamp on, the window dark"
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import re

import pytest

from tests.social_fixtures import receipts_active  # noqa: F401

DAYPARTS = {"dawn", "day", "dusk", "night"}


def _subjects():
    from engine import yamlio
    from pathlib import Path

    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "games" / "read-receipts" / "data" / "art"
    return yamlio.safe_load((root / "subjects.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def test_there_are_48_plates_on_the_engines_dayparts() -> None:
    data = _subjects()
    plates = sum(len(row["times"]) for row in data["subjects"])
    assert plates == 48
    for row in data["subjects"]:
        assert set(row["times"]) <= DAYPARTS and row["base"] in row["times"] and "alts" not in row


def test_no_subject_carries_a_negative_key_or_no_x_term() -> None:
    data = _subjects()
    for row in data["subjects"]:
        assert "negative" not in row
        for prompt in row["times"].values():
            assert not re.search(r"(?i)\bno\s+\w+", prompt), (row["id"], prompt)


def test_lookup_pools_the_base_for_an_undeclared_daypart(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine.media.providers import shipped
    from engine.media.providers.base import ImageRequest

    def find(subject: str, part: str):
        return shipped.lookup(ImageRequest(subject_id=subject, kind="location", time_of_day=part,
                                           story="read-receipts"))

    for row in _subjects()["subjects"]:
        for part in DAYPARTS - set(row["times"]):
            assert find(row["id"], part) == find(row["id"], row["base"])


def test_the_story_activates_and_validates_with_its_art_paths(receipts_active) -> None:
    """Pre-flight F41: with the art paths declared and (if T27 is cut) an empty `art_root`, the story still
    activates and plays; `validate_content.py` and `doctor.py` are run in Step 4 as well."""
    from engine.games import registry

    row = next(r for r in registry.catalog() if r["slug"] == "read-receipts")
    assert row["playable"] and not row["problems"], row["problems"]
```

`lookup` is `engine.media.providers.shipped.lookup(ImageRequest)`: read the `ImageRequest` dataclass (`engine/media/providers/base.py`) before writing the call (F22). Its lookup is deterministic because there are no `alts` (m2). (`registry.catalog()`'s row keys are the ones `READ_RECEIPTS_ROW` mirrors: `slug`, `playable`, `problems`.)

```python
@pytest.mark.loopback
def test_no_other_window_for_one_session_world_then_voice(hosted_receipts) -> None:
    """§20.1: a world turn and a voice pass from ONE account never refuse each other `other_window`."""
    started = hosted_receipts.login_and_start("alice")
    from tests.social_fixtures import _offered, _three_due_hosted
    _three_due_hosted(hosted_receipts, started)
    from engine.social import voicer
    voicer.get_scheduler().resume()
    # `Started` carries `sid`, not `session_id` (tests/social_fixtures.py; pre-flight F21).
    resp = started.client.post("/api/phone/action", json={"session_id": started.sid,
                                                          "action": "put_down", "target": "", "args": {}, "text": ""})
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    # The world action goes by the OFFERED option's choice_id (pre-flight F3): the route never reads a client intent.
    turn = started.client.post("/api/game/choice", json={"session_id": started.sid,
                                                         "choice_id": _offered(hosted_receipts, started, "Tidy up a bit")})
    assert turn.status_code == 200, turn.get_data(as_text=True)
    assert "other window" not in turn.get_data(as_text=True)
    from tests.social_fixtures import _wait_until
    _wait_until(lambda: not voicer.pending(hosted_receipts.session(started)), timeout=10.0)


@pytest.mark.loopback
def test_no_other_window_for_one_session_voice_then_world(hosted_receipts) -> None:
    """The other direction: a pass holding the turnstile; the world turn waits one call, then runs."""
    import threading

    from engine.social import voicer
    from tests.social_fixtures import _offered, _three_due_hosted, _wait_until
    from tests.social_stub import StubVoice

    started = hosted_receipts.login_and_start("alice")
    _three_due_hosted(hosted_receipts, started)
    release = threading.Event()
    stub = StubVoice("zero", block=release)
    voicer.set_llm_fn(stub)
    started.client.post("/api/phone/action", json={"session_id": started.sid,
                                                   "action": "put_down", "target": "", "args": {}, "text": ""})
    choice_id = _offered(hosted_receipts, started, "Tidy up a bit")   # F3: by choice_id, never a client intent
    voicer.get_scheduler().resume()
    _wait_until(lambda: stub.calls == 1, timeout=10.0)          # a voice call holds the turnstile
    answers: list = []
    t = threading.Thread(target=lambda: answers.append(started.client.post("/api/game/choice", json={
        "session_id": started.sid, "choice_id": choice_id})))
    t.start()
    session = hosted_receipts.session(started)
    _wait_until(lambda: session.narration_turnstile.world_waiting == 1, timeout=10.0)
    release.set()
    t.join(30.0)
    assert answers and answers[0].status_code == 200, answers[0].get_data(as_text=True)
    assert "other window" not in answers[0].get_data(as_text=True)
    _wait_until(lambda: not voicer.pending(session), timeout=10.0)
```

Helpers added to `tests/social_fixtures.py`:
- `HostedReceipts.session(started)`, beside `state`: the live session object from the app's store, looked up by `started.sid` (pre-flight F21: `Started` is `(client, sock, sid, account_id)`; there is no `session_id` field).
- `_three_due_hosted(hosted, started)`: `_three_due` run on the hosted session (`hosted.session(started)`), holding its turn lock and releasing it through `release_turn`.
- `_offered(hosted, started, text) -> str`: the `id` of the option whose `text` is `text` in `hosted.session(started).last_turn["choices"]`, asserted to carry an `intent` (the opening's "Tidy up a bit", T11; pre-flight F3).

The hosted app's storyteller is `tests/hosted_app.build`'s default `llm_fn=scripted_model`, a stub, so no world turn here reaches a model.

Further tests:
- `test_voice_pass_never_blocks_a_scheduler_thread_hosted`: two accounts each in a world turn, held by a blocked `StubNarrator`, while a third account's pass completes on the 2-worker scheduler.
- `test_the_narration_share_metric_is_emitted`: a world turn and a pass each add seconds, and the metric row's fields are exactly the schema's.
- `tests/test_admin_no_play_text.py`: the planted sentinels `SCN-NARR-7f3a91`, `RECAP-5c2e08` and `STORY-d41b6e` in a scene narration, a recap and a story never appear in any admin page.

### Task 31: Live play (gated) (§20.2, R1, R3) — M

The owner runs or approves this task: it calls the owner's live model server. It runs only under `@pytest.mark.live` and with `CLOCKWORK_LIVE_LLM` set to the configured server, and never loads or unloads a model. If no model is already loaded, it is NOT run. Record "not run: no model loaded", the T9 assumed figures stand, and CLAUDE.md's deferred row says so.

- [ ] **Step 0: The playtest acceptance (addendum 4 §7.5, written by T23H).** The controller plays it by hand in the browser pane before the release: first with the stub model (`scripts/screenshot_runs.py --serve read-receipts --exit-with-parent`, a background task, stopped after), then here with the owner's model narrating. It runs at 1366x768 and 390x844, with NO refresh at any step. The engine side is held by `tests/test_rr_playtest_replay.py` and the client side by `ui/tests/rr-playtest.test.jsx`; this walk is what they cannot see (the real layout, the real model's words, the pane's own focus). Each step has an expected result; record pass or fail for each in Notes.
  1. Start a new game and match with a contact (Matchstick), get to numbers. In the Places app go to The Anchor. EXPECT: the plate names The Anchor; no choice reads "go to The Anchor".
  2. Open the contact's thread and ask to meet ("free now?" naming The Anchor, drinks). Let them read it (wait on the phone a while). EXPECT: a plan row exists ("drinks at The Anchor, now"), the phone still shows their answer.
  3. Put the phone down. EXPECT: the row is NOT empty and shows "Wait for {name}" beside The Anchor's own choices; the compose box is live; nothing needed a refresh; no text from them said "I'm here".
  4. Pick "Wait for {name}". EXPECT: in the same frame the log gains "{name} arrives.", "With {name}" shows, and the row holds the scene's moves and closers (no "Wait for").
  5. Type a question naming one of their topics (an "Ask about" label from the row). EXPECT: a turn of 80-200 words with them speaking; the receipt is `ask_about`; choices follow. (Word count and voice are T31 Step 1's reading of the live model.)
  6. Type a line that names nothing ("you have a nice laugh"). EXPECT: the receipt is `talk`; the scene continues; choices appear.
  7. Pick "Back to yours". EXPECT: the scene continues in the flat at once, with no wait and no "on my way" text.
  8. Pick the phone up. EXPECT: no message from them landed during the scene. Call it a night; put the phone down after a "this evening" wait. EXPECT: the row is the new hour's, never empty.
  9. At home type "make breakfast" (when offered) and "go to the kitchen". EXPECT: each runs as that choice (time passes, the receipt says typed). Type "the kitchen is a mess". EXPECT: narrated only, no time passes.
  10. Repeat steps 1-4 from the flat with a `tonight` plan accepted at noon and kept that evening by `go`, arriving before them. EXPECT: the scene opens when they arrive.
  11. Refresh once at the end. EXPECT: the row is unchanged.

  It passes only if the owner's four symptoms are each absent:
  - (a) the person arrives and never texts "I'm here" while not there; while together no text lands;
  - (b) the row never offers only "go to <here>" and "Head home";
  - (c) no choice resolves to "the city", and no pressed choice is refused as stale;
  - (d) the choices never need a refresh.

  A failure is fixed before the release, not deferred.

## Notes (written during execution)

Each task that measures something or rules on something appends its notes here, in its own commit: T5's depth 0→1 figures, T9's first v2 pass and the latency probe, T11's `_arrive` stub, C1's ruling, T29's tuning rounds and the save, and T31's live figures.

**Owner deferral (2026-10-23).** T29 was split (T29a: the world turn's engine cost; T29b: full sessions, measurement and tuning; T29c: the save pin). The owner moved T29b, T31 (live play, with the addendum-4 pane walk), T32 (the docs pass) and T33 (the whole-branch review) to the END of v0.24.0. v0.23.0 ships after T29a, T30 and T34; T34 does the minimum docs. The save pin stands as T9 left it (861,222 bytes, 13.9% under 1 MB) and is re-checked by the release's full run.

**Pre-flight (2026-10-14, before T1).** The 45 pre-flight findings (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-v0.23.0-read-receipts-rework/preflight.md`, F1-F45) were ruled on by the controller, every proposed ruling accepted, and applied to this plan in one commit before T1 was dispatched. Where a ruling offered options, the recommended one was taken; where none was recommended, the simplest, recorded at its task and in Plan decisions 23-33. One ruling's premise did not hold against the code: F40 says AGENTS.md "Verify a checkout" has no `simulate_social.py` line, but it has one in both blocks (`AGENTS.md:328`, `:344` at `a2ed481`), so T9's edit of that line stands. One accepted gap is recorded here rather than fixed:

- **F42, the client between T3 and T22.** From T3 (which removes the `15m` wait target) and T1 (which drops `settings.auto_wait` from the slice) until T22 (which removes them from the client), the client still offers "Let time pass" and `wait 15m` and still sends `settings.auto_wait`, and the server refuses them. Client tests mock the act, so nothing goes red; a hand-played build in that window shows the refusal. Accepted (ruling F42).

**T3 (2026-10-14).** The v0.22 harness (`scripts/simulate_social.py`) needed three changes beyond F26's `15m` -> `1h` to keep running on the episode model, each the minimum: `HOUR_WAKE` 7 (with `wait morning` landing at `clock.morning` 7, a waking hour of 8 made the attentive policy skip every day), its away row run under `social.away.mode: real` for its own call (`_away_mode_real`; the default is now `none`), and nothing else. On `tests/test_simulate_social.py` (10 seeds x 60 days, neutral model) every band holds but `talking_to_ghosted_14d` (18.2%, band 20-35%), deferred by data (CLAUDE.md row), not retuned: the ghost is drawn per episode start now, and T9 measures it. The file took 52 minutes (25 at v0.22.0): a 60-day seed runs about 440 s, because instant replies make more actions per day. `tests/test_social_commit_scope.py`'s played run looks every waking hour for four days (was `wait next` for seven, which no longer stops at a reply), forces a media budget and lets its proposing voice pick the least-used legal proposal, so it still makes 300+ commits of every proposal kind inside its timeout (about 10 minutes).

**T5 (2026-10-14), depth 0->1 on 10 seeds (Plan decision 17).** The v0.22 harness (`scripts/simulate_social.py`, `--days 14 --seeds 10 --policy attentive`, neutral model) has no depth row, so it was run through its own `run` with one extra observer noting, per matched person, the in-game hour and the run's action count when they first reached depth 1 and the stage `numbers` (a scratch wrapper; no harness or constant changed, rule 10). Of 565 matched people, 23 reached depth 1 within 14 days and 21 swapped numbers. Median from the match: depth 1 in 4.04 in-game days (391 of the run's actions, all people), numbers in 4.23 days (425). Per archetype (reached depth 1 of n; median days): steady 4 of 55, 2.65; romantic 2 of 59, 3.01; busy 1 of 26, 3.51; slow_burner 1 of 49, 4.23; night_owl 2 of 53, 5.61; needy 9 of 47, 5.94; drifter 1 of 43, 7.99; banterer 3 of 53, 8.83; breadcrumber, dry_texter, ghoster and turner 0. So the number now follows depth 1 almost at once (21 of 23, the offer and the `willing.number` gate), with no day in its gate, but depth 1 itself is rare under v0.22's attentive policy, which spreads its messages over some 55 matches and mostly talks in quick mode (`gain.exchange` 6 x `mode_mult.quick` 0.6). Recorded, not tuned: T9's harness is the first v2 measurement and T29 tunes.

**T9 (2026-10-19), the v2 harness's first pass and the latency probe.** `scripts/simulate_social.py --sessions 6 --seeds 10 --policy attentive --model-seconds S` (two seeds a shell, `--raw` then `--fold`; the flaky player runs beside each attentive report), neutral model, AFTER the latency levers:

| Row (band) | 6 s | 15 s | 30 s | |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `exchanges_per_episode.d0` (6-14) | 6.0 | 5.0 | 4.0 | deferred |
| `end_reason.schedule` (30-55%) | 24.8% | 28.3% | 33.3% | deferred |
| `end_reason.energy` (25-50%) | 12.9% | 8.3% | 10.0% | deferred |
| `end_reason.player` (10-30%) | 15.9% | 16.8% | 11.1% | banded |
| `cold_unanswered_1h.d0` (15-35%) | 52.3% | 56.5% | 52.6% | deferred |
| `ghosted_per_episode.d0` (5-15%) | 5.2% | 5.0% | 6.6% | banded |
| `chat_minutes_to_numbers.fast` (6-15) | 10.65 | 11.87 | 12.6 | banded |
| `chat_minutes_to_numbers.mid` (12-30) | 11.4 | 14.03 | 15.86 | deferred |
| `chat_minutes_to_numbers.slow` (25-60) | 17.03 | 23.85 | 30.6 | deferred |
| `numbers_within_session` (40-70%) | 19.3% | 18.4% | 11.6% | deferred |
| `person_promises_kept` (100%) | 1.0 | 1.0 | 1.0 | asserted |
| `player_promises_kept.attentive` (95%+) | 1.0 | 1.0 | 1.0 | banded |
| `player_promises_kept.flaky` (55-75%) | 71.8% | 71.1% | 76.5% | deferred |
| `full_replies_in_long_episodes` (60%+) | 54.6% | 53.8% | 53.8% | deferred (70.3% at 30 s before `chatting_after` 5) |
| `ratio.chat` (derived) | 1.557 | 1.27 | 0.974 | |

Definitions the brief left open, chosen before measuring: an episode counts toward `exchanges_per_episode` and `end_reason` only if the player wrote in it while it was open (an answer that landed while the player was away and lapsed unread is not a conversation: about half of all episodes, `also.unjoined_episodes_per_run`); `end_reason.schedule` is a boundary's words or a glance at work; a cold message is a send with no conversation going (or one signed off with nothing more to say); `numbers_within_session` is by the end of the first session in which they talked. No balance constant was tuned: no lever is named for these rows, and two harness findings distort them (docs/GOVERNANCE.md's T3 row): `wait {until: next}` does not stop at a read a reply follows (the harness's `_watching_reads` stops there instead), and a glance at work inside the lunch window promises `lunch` again, due at once, looping two-exchange conversations. The latency probe at the ASSUMED 20 tokens/s (no live run): p95 quick 15.0 s, chatting 28.0 s, deep 38.0 s (30.0 / 56.0 / 76.0 with the starvation retry), so all three of §20.2's levers were applied. The day-180 save `write_day_save` now plays (phone-only sessions) is 1,378,257 bytes, over the 1 MB budget: 1,168 closed promise rows (229 KB) are never compacted, threads 489 KB, media 180 KB (`tests/test_social_save_size.py` fails; T29's re-pin).

**T9 fix round 1 (2026-10-19, the controller's rulings).** (1) Closed promise rows are bounded at save compaction (`promises.forget_closed`, `promises.yaml` `closed_kept` 3 a person, tallied on the person as `promises_closed`; rows a reader still needs -- a `broken_promise` pointer, a queued event's or due message's, an open or recap-due episode's or one made inside it, the payload's newest five -- stay). (2) `wait {until: next}` stops at a `read` of a thread whose last word is the player's (`turn._awaited_read`); the harness's `_watching_reads` is gone. (3) A promise made inside its own window resolves to that window's NEXT occurrence (`promises.span_for`; spec §5.1 resolves a window to its band and §3.5 item 2 makes a promise a later time, so the next occurrence, not "not offered", keeps every chip and the glance's `lunch` offered). The harness also reads a spilled thread as the engine does (`messaging.last_shown`, the person's `last_player_at`), so a run on disk measures what one in memory does. Re-measured after them:

| Row (band) | 6 s | 15 s | 30 s | |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `exchanges_per_episode.d0` (6-14) | 6.0 | 6.0 | 5.0 | deferred |
| `end_reason.schedule` (30-55%) | 20.9% | 18.6% | 19.2% | deferred |
| `end_reason.energy` (25-50%) | 11.0% | 9.0% | 8.9% | deferred |
| `end_reason.player` (10-30%) | 26.5% | 32.3% | 35.4% | deferred (was banded) |
| `cold_unanswered_1h.d0` (15-35%) | 65.1% | 63.7% | 61.3% | deferred |
| `ghosted_per_episode.d0` (5-15%) | 5.6% | 5.7% | 6.4% | banded |
| `chat_minutes_to_numbers.fast` (6-15) | 11.0 | 13.65 | 14.75 | banded |
| `chat_minutes_to_numbers.mid` (12-30) | 11.43 | 14.45 | 17.68 | deferred |
| `chat_minutes_to_numbers.slow` (25-60) | 14.25 | 17.95 | 25.3 | deferred |
| `numbers_within_session` (40-70%) | 13.8% | 8.7% | 6.8% | deferred |
| `person_promises_kept` (100%) | 1.0 | 1.0 | 1.0 | asserted |
| `player_promises_kept.attentive` (95%+) | 1.0 | 1.0 | 1.0 | banded |
| `player_promises_kept.flaky` (55-75%) | 74.5% | 75.9% | 74.4% | deferred |
| `full_replies_in_long_episodes` (60%+) | 53.9% | 52.9% | 53.1% | deferred (the `chatting_after` 5 lever) |

The day-180 save: 1,519,674 bytes after fix round 1, then 861,222 after fix round 2 (the slice packed, closed conversations spilled to the archive): 13.9% under the 1 MB budget, `tests/test_social_save_size.py` passing

**T9 fix round 2 (2026-10-20, the controller's ruling: under 1 MB now, losslessly).** The slice is packed at save (`engine/social/packing.py`: a message's decision without default fields, a message's own `thread_id`, a media row's default fields; save -> load -> save byte-identical). State keeps per thread the open episode's messages, the player's unread ones, the last turn a reply reads (`archive._last_turn`: their run and the player's message before it, or the player's run up to 3), and one screen (`state_messages` 8) of the open thread, filled from the archive when state holds less; other threads `closed_thread_tail` 0. The day-180 save: 861,222 bytes (people 533 KB, media 168 KB, threads 164 KB with 122 messages, promises 41 KB), 13.9% under the budget. The harness's player opens a thread before answering in it (the measured rows unchanged).

**T11 (2026-10-20): the `_arrive` stub, and what the brief could not know.** `engine/social/world.py::_arrive` is a named STUB: every `go` arrival returns `{"quiet": True}` (a quiet visit, no scene); T12 replaces its body with `plans.arrive(state, to)` (the stub table's row; `test_arrival_records_met_stub`). Decisions taken in T11, each recorded in its report and GOVERNANCE: (1) `SOCIAL_WORLD_ACTIONS` is `{"do", "go"}`; T13 adds `scene` and `close` with their skills, so `test_every_world_skill_has_a_world_verb` holds at every task. (2) `voicer.request(session, now=True)`, called by `_run_world_turn` after it releases the turnstile, moves a pass waiting out a DEFERRAL's backoff to the ready queue (§20.1 step 6); a phone action's request keeps the backoff. (3) "Carry on" resumes to the original end through a saved `state.social["resume"]` (`{activity, until}`, written by `social_settings`), set when a buzz stops an activity and cleared by the next `do`: without it a sleep woken at 06:00 would run on to the next day's waking (the min-sleep floor). (4) `go_to_work` is offered only from `WORK_LEAD_HOURS` (2) before the player's work band, so it is never a night skipped. (5) Homes and `registry_office` are `go` targets only as an open plan's destination (§9.1's `venue`, or `home_of(pid)` for `their_place`); `tests/test_social_world.py::test_every_sentinel_place_is_a_go_target` places such a plan row for each of the 20 sentinel places. (6) The `places` payload section is not time-varying (a location does not move during an away advance). (7) No module-state row for `PHONE_UP_REFUSAL` (a str): the inventory scan rejects rows for immutable constants.

**Addendum 4 (2026-10-22, controller, at the owner's request).** The owner's addendum 4 (typing and people in the world, "like dev-story") and the playtest report added scope after T23: spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-v0.23.0-addendum4-world-interaction.md` (opus-reviewed twice), built as T23A-T23H (S, S, L, S, M, M, L, M: about 17 units) before T26. The overrun past C1's ledger is accepted by the controller at the owner's request; C1's cut is not re-opened.
